# NOOR TRADERS — MALIK KE PAKKE QAIDE (har AI pehle ye parhe)

Malik: Samiullah (Noor Traders, ~25 mulazim). Apps: Blue Khata (ye repo), Staff Hazri app (ALAG repo — kabhi is repo mein mat daalo), shop PC scripts (`pc/`).
Ye file har kaam se pehle parho. Koi naya qaida mile to yahan aur `AI_NOTES.txt` dono mein likho.

## 1. Baat cheet
- Jawab **Roman Urdu** mein (Latin harf). Hindi/Devanagari ya Urdu script NAHI — malik parh nahi sakta.
- Jawab **chhota** — phone par lamba parhna mushkil.
- **Chhota magar POORA (malik, 15 Nov 2026: 'jawab small ho aur saara jawab us mein ho')**: 4-6 line, seedhi baat pehle; wajah / tafseel / mashware ki lambi list sirf jab malik maange. Jo baat malik ko chahiye wo chhoot na jaye — lamba likhne ke bajaye faltu hissa kaato.
- Batao kahan AI use ho raha hai aur kahan nahi.

## 2. Kaam ka tareeqa
- Har kaam se pehle **PLAN do aur malik ke "OK" ka intezar karo**. OK ke baghair kuch mat banao.
- Sochte waqt **POORI app ka design** dimagh mein rakho; kam az kam 10 dafa soch kar **EK pakka jawab**. Complexity bilkul nahi — easy to use.
- Pehle bane hue kaam yaad rakho; batao naya kaam zaroori hai ya pehle se kuch maujood hai.
- Galti ho to seedha maano, theek karo, aur us ka qaida yahan likh do taake dobara na ho.
- Malik ko kabhi PC par command chalane ko mat kaho — PC scripts `pc/manifest.json` se khud update hoti hain (KHATA-DOCTOR ~3 min).

## 3. Design / chhoti suhulatein (har nayi jagah khud lagao)
- **Premium, elegant** look; chips premium. Purana **layout mat badlo** (staff aadi hai) — sirf rang/font/size.
- **🎀 CHIPS KA PAKKA QAIDA (malik, 27 Oct 2026: "jitne bhi chips banao premium look mein, chhote — baar baar na kehna pare")**: har naya chip / button patla (32–40px), gol (999px / 11px), halka border + gradient, nowrap text; screen ki aadhi jagah lene wale bare buttons KABHI nahi (jaanch v2.129.0 jaisi ghalti dobara nahi). Status ka chip hamesha (⏳ / ✅ / ✕) — khali intezar nahi.
- Item chunna ho to **list pehle se nazar aaye** (likhna na pare), **search**, **multi-select ✓ + "N jorein"**, **dohra (duplicate) roko**.
- Lambi list / parchi = **5-5 ki patti**: poori list dikhao, upar ke N par khud ✓, print ke baad agle N par khud ✓ (chips 3 · 5 · 10 · Sab).
- Jahan ginti/check ho wahan **har item ka ☐ checkbox** + "✓ n/N" chip.
- Har PDF ke saath **kholo + bhejo** option; kholne par poori screen.
- Har naya feature/screen = **Settings → Mulazim mein on/off CAP** (`can(k)` deny-list). CAP key ki shakal `^[a-z]+[.:][a-z0-9]+$` (warna saveStaff gira deta hai — 21 Oct 2026: stock.g2milao / stock.rack100 save nahi hote the). Jo screen malik shuru mein BAND chahe (Item report / Larka report) = `OPT_IN` set + `st.allow` (tick = khula), deny nahi (v2.105.7).
- **Speed pehle** — har tick/button foran (pehle UI, server baad mein).
- **🤖 Agent action — 22 Oct 2026 (v2.111.0) malik: "agent ka hukum ki ab zaroorat nahi" → naye kaam mein agent action NAHI banana (jab tak malik khud na kahe); purane action wahi rahein.** (Pehle ka qaida:) har naya feature / chip / qaida apna agent action bhi register kare (`agentRegister([...])` — pos-stock.js ke aakhir mein namoona): `name, desc, params, read|write, danger, summary(args), run(args)`; `run` WAHI function jo button chalata hai. Malik likh kar poori app chalata hai (v2.103.47).
- Shop ki parchiyon par English ke saath **Urdu** bhi. Har parchi ke upar **bari Urdu tareekh** (`UR.dateLine(date)` — آج / کل کا بل / N دن پرانا) + Urdu sar-khi; jo cheez insaan likhta hai (Tafseel / Note / Wajah) = `UR.note(text, px, 'تفصیل')` (Urdu label bara, "تفصیل: …") aur print se pehle `text = await UR.fillNotes(text)` (AI 4 s; na aaye to `roman2ur` — bina AI qaide wala Urdu, line kabhi gayab nahi) — 20/21 Oct 2026.
- **🧻 Parchi chhoti + tasveer saaf (27 Oct 2026, malik: "paper bohat lagta hai")**: tareekh SIRF upar (neeche wali bari tareekh / "Blue Khata app" / khali lines NAHI); kaatne (GS V 66 0) se pehle sirf 1 khali line; Urdu tasveer 20% chhoti — sab `nt-parchi` `compactJobs` se (naye parchi code mein bhi `'', '', ''` mat lagao). Tasveer: sirf PEHLI, `photo-print` v2 (512 chaurai, 270 lambai, beech ka 20% tak kaat, sharp + Atkinson). Shared module badlo to us ki service ki main file bhi chhuo (doctor sirf main file par dobara shuru karta hai).
- **🔤 Parchi par item ka Urdu naam (urdu-names v1.7, 6 Nov 2026)** (malik: "capri jumbo 3in1 ka Urdu nahi bana — larke ne nahi diya"): naye / purane parchi code mein item ka Urdu naam HAMESHA `UR.item(id, englishNaam)` (save hua naam, warna `roman2ur` bina AI) — akela `UR.get(id)` nahi (Urdu line gayab = Urdu parhne wala larka item chhor deta). Asal naam PC transfer-sync `fillItems` har 10 min AI se banata hai — malik / app ka likha naam KABHI nahi badalta (`mapReady` + taza get).
- Parchi par packet / tadad **item ke naam ke aage usi line par** (upar / neeche alag line nahi) — transfer-sync `pkNameLines` (21 Oct 2026).
- Theme CSS se bachne ke liye: naye widget mein class `on` nahi, `is-in`; `html body :is(#summary,#dialogBody) .x { … !important }`.
- **🧭 ⋮ dhancha (shell.js, v2.104.0)**: screen ke chhote kaam screen par nahi — button par `data-mn="key" data-mn-i="📋" data-mn-t="Naam" data-mn-s="chhoti line" data-mn-g="🧾 Group"` (options ka dabba `data-mn-seg`, poora hissa `data-mn-box`). Phone par yeh khud chhup kar header ke ⋮ mein jate hain; zaroori warning ho to `data-mn-keep`. Naya hand-made menu / chips ki qatar mat banao.
- **📱 Phone (v2.105.0)**: upar ka hero card chhota (`html:not([data-hero="big"])` CSS), chips ki qatar ek line + side scroll (`html:not([data-chip="big"])`, classes .mchips/.rk-nb/.sort-chips…). Nayi screen ka card / chips inhi classes se banao taake khud chhote hon. Screen chips ☰ ke peeche (shell.js). Agent ka card upar nahi — 🤖 gola.
- **⋮ PAKKA QAIDA (malik, 21 Oct 2026: "koi bhi chips ka kaam ho andar rakho, screen par nahi — three dot ke andar")**: har screen AUR har khirki ke chips / chhote kaam (filter, tareekh, tarteeb, edit, transfer, reminder, % ek click…) screen par NAHI — ⋮ ke andar (`data-mn*` tags). Screen par sirf asal kaam (khata / list / entries) + ek chhoti patti jo bataye kya chuna hai ("… ⋮ se badlein"). Sirf zaroori warning `data-mn-keep`.
- **⌨️ KEYBOARD QAIDA (malik, 1 Nov 2026: "jab hum keyboard se kaam karte hain to screen mazeed chhoti ho jati hai — is ka hal yahi: saamne sirf main screen, baqi cheezein three dot ke andar")**: phone par keyboard khulte hi aadhi screen chali jati hai — is liye har screen / khirki par SAAMNE sirf asal kaam (list / cards / tick); filter, tareekh, ginti (KPI dabbe), search ke ilawa ke chips, PDF, demo / madad ki likhai — SAB ⋮ ke andar. Zaroorat ho to DO ⋮: daayein = aam ⋮ (filter, din, PDF…), baayein = alag list ka ⋮ (jaise Gate pass mein 🔵 un-posted, badge mein ginti). Naya screen banate waqt pehle yeh socho: keyboard khula ho to bhi asal kaam nazar aaye.
- **🪟 Khirki ka ⋮ + neeche ki patti (v2.105.2)**: khirki (dialog) ke chips par bhi wahi `data-mn*` tags — phone par khud chhup kar khirki ke upar × ke saath ⋮ mein (shell.js dlgBtnSetup); upar ek chhoti patti `data-mn-sheet` (jaise rkPillHTML) jo bataye kya chuna hai. Neeche ki patti (#actions) ke kaam: screen ka view shell.js `FT_MOVE` mein daalo → phone par ⋮ mein; neeche sirf ek asal bara button (Naya account / ＋ Purchase / Naya transfer note / Saman diya·Paisa wasool).
- **👤 Mulazim ki apni screen (v2.105.4)**: malik kahe "har ek ki apni screen" to malik ki setting Firestore mein (sab ka default), mulazim ki sirf us ke phone par (localStorage, key mein login naam) + CAP; patti par "👤 Aap ka …" aur "↺ Malik wala". Rules ki zaroorat nahi.
- **👑 Mulazim ki tab mein malik (v2.105.8)**: alag Firebase app (`sessionStorage sam-malik-tab`, naam 'blue-khata-malik', session persistence, memory cache, push nahi). Is flow mein mulazim ka anonymous login KABHI sign out mat karo; har logout / idle 10 min / ↩️ = `malikBack` (reload → mulazim).
- **📷 Entry ki tasveer (v2.105.8)**: poori `entryPhotos/<id>` + chhota namoona `entryPhotos/<id>_t` (pn.t=1). List / chip mein `ephHTML` (poori tasveer sirf dabane par). Naye form mein bhi `epPickHTML` + `epSetup` + `...epCollect(f)`.
- **⛔ Item band (v2.105.9)**: band = `stockCount/_hidden.items[id]` → PC (post-farq) khud stock 0 bill + IsActive 0. App sirf **tabdeeli** bheje `cloud.setBand({id: true|false})` (false = deleteField) — **poori list merge:true se kabhi nahi** (nested map merge = hataya hua id reh jata hai; v2.103.84–2.105.8 mein "↩️ Chalu" isi se kabhi nahi hua) aur poori list replace bhi nahi (purani/khali list = sab band khul jayein). Band items Stock par "⛔ Band items" list mein dikhte hain (POS se ghaib bhi). POS mein active hone par app khud un-band NAHI karti (malik ka faisla) — 🟢 dikhati hai.
- **🔔 Agent ka reminder (v2.106.0)**: din / waqt / naam ka samajhna APP karti hai (`remParse` agent.js — AI nahi); agent sirf "lagta" hai. Naya hukum-type kaam ho jo seedha samajh aaye to pehle `quick()` mein app se karo, AI baad mein. Agent ke andar ke button `#agSheet .x` se style karo (halki theme `html.lt button` warna safed kar deti hai).
- **🧹 Hataya hua (v2.106.0)**: Reminder screen ka ⏰ "Khatam hone wala maal" + 🛒 "Purchase order" (posStats / po-formula / AI order) — malik ne band karwaye ("awe load"). Wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe; mangwana = Rack bharo → 🛒 Mangwao. PC raat-report ki subah wali khabar bhi band.
- **🏭 Factory ek-screen (v2.107.0)**: factory ka saamne wala screen = ek hi jagah ① item ② formula chips ③ batch ④ bana (Ctn + Pcs) ⑤ rate → 💾 Save (formula + batch + POS stock). Data WAHI (`cfg.formulas` / `cfg.packs` / `factoryBatches`, batch `batchCalc` se) — naya data model mat banao. Qaida: **jo khana khali chhoro, aakhri khali wala bachi hui tadad khud**; bulk mein kuch na likha = sab bulk stock. Purani 4 tabs `st.view 'old'` (⋮ se) — malik ne kaha "pehle wali mat chhero", hatao mat. Stock kam par "nahi" = kuch save na ho. "POS mein bhi rate" sirf tick par. 🏭 Factory stock chip = Stock screen `stockScreenOpen({screen:'factory'})`.
- **📷 Factory tasveer / 📄 rate list PDF (v2.108.0)**: tasveer Firestore mein (factory/phIdx index + ph_<id>_<v>_<i> Bytes hisse, likhe sirf malik — rules factory/* wahi, Storage / Drive nahi). Kaam fac-photo.js (zaroorat par import); chhoti tasveer window.ntFacThumb(id) (Stock / Sale / ek-screen). PDF = jsPDF JPEG seedha (HD) + openReportPreview({prepare, filename, hd}). App khud AI nahi chalati — AI command malik ChatGPT / Gemini mein deta hai. PC auto-backup Drive "Factory Photos" mein copy. Khirki ke upar kuch dikhana ho to apni <dialog> showModal (div fixed khuli khirki ke neeche chhup jata hai).
- **📋 Roz ki ginti (v2.109.0)**: stockConfig.roz + stockCount/roz-<jagah> (cloud.rozClaim TRANSACTION — do larkon ko ek item kabhi nahi) + _postQ (PC auto-backup ek ek _post → post-farq). Andhi ginti = `seeStock()` false (history mein bhi System / Farq nahi). Jagah ki ghalti = dono jagah fraq bill — YA (v2.114.0, malik: "transfer direct ho jaye") ⇄ TN + dono ginti wahi tadad par dobara (farq 0). **Phone par #homeBar chhupa hota hai** (chips sirf ⋮ "Aaj ki haalat" mein) — larke ka naya kaam ho to screen par bhi bara button do.
- **📡 Ginti ka PC jawab (v2.110.0)**: har ginti ⏳ (phone mein) → ☁️ (server) → ✅ (PC ne sysPos likha). 15 s server nahi = ❌, 90 s PC nahi = ⚠️ + larke ko "📲 Malik ko batao" + stockCount/pcwarn-<staffId> → malik ki chip. Malik ka qaida: **PC / server se jawab na mile to app khamosh na rahe — saaf bataye aur malik se rabta ka rasta de.** Status sirf apni jagah badlo (pcPaint), list dobara nahi.
- **📅 Label par tareekh (label-print v4.9, 27 Oct 2026)**: barcode label ke daayein kone par running number NAHI — chhapne ke din ki tareekh + mahine ka pehla harf (`27-O`, J F M A M J J A S O N D), font 2. Malik: Godam 2 theke ki packing (Rs 2 / packet) — larka aaj ke packet pehchane, kal ka maal mix na ho. Number wapas mat lao; factory label par batch no wahi.
- **🏷 Barcode mazdoori (v2.111.0)**: label printer EK hai — label ki godam = jis larke ne chhapa (stockConfig.lbl.g, uid yaad lbl.u). Mazdoori label se NAHI — godam → dukan transfer note par ✓ mil gaya hone par (packet × rate, blueAccess/lblPay pay ka nishan). Malik: "stock aane se hi mazdoori tay". **➕ Zyada label (v2.213.0, rules 2.95, label-print v5.0; malik 9 Nov: "Godam 2 par har item ke 5 barcode zyada default")**: `stockConfig.lbl {xg: godam branch, xn: 5}` (🏷 Mazdoori screen ⚙️ mein godam chips + N) → `cloud.requestLabel` khud `lblExtra`: jis ka label-godam (`lbl.g[staffId|'malik']`, staffId `deviceSeen` se `LBL_ME`) == xg → job `x` (ek item ke 200-200 hisson mein sirf pehle par, 5 s) → PC `copiesOf(j)` = copies + x. Naya label raasta bhi requestLabel se hi (x khud lagta). **🧾 v2.228.0 (malik 10 Nov: "us ka larke / godam se taluq nahi — transfer note wali screen se jo bhi barcode nikle, har dafa 5 extra, default")**: `rkLabelsGo` har label line par `tn: 1, tx` (`stockConfig.lbl.tx`, na ho = 5; ⚙️ "🧾 Transfer note ke saath") → requestLabel x = tx, lblExtra nahi. Transfer note ke saath label ka naya raasta = `rkLabelsGo` se hi. Sabaq: malik "X par zyada" kahe to pehle poochho KIS SCREEN se chhapte waqt — apni taraf se larke / godam ki shart mat joro.
- **⚡ Firebase Auth / boot (v2.112.0)**: cloud.js `initializeAuth(app,{persistence:[browserLocalPersistence, indexedDBLocalPersistence]})` — **`getAuth()` / popupRedirectResolver WAPAS NA lagao** (mobile par har dafa Google ka gapi + chhupa iframe internet se, login ki jaanch us ke baad = 10+ sec, phir login page ki jhalak). `setPersistence` ka await bhi nahi. Boot par login page sirf auth tayyar hone ke baad (login na ho / ghalat) — tab tak splash (BOOT CHOWKIDAR: 12 sec "🔑 Login page kholein", 45 sec khud; `window.bkHold` index.html ke 10 sec parde ko rokta). 8 sec wala timer wapas nahi.
- **🧺 Grid row ke andar naya dabba (v2.112.1)**: `.rk-r` jaisi grid row mein har poori-chaurai wala dabba (patti, lock, transit) par `grid-column:1/-1` zaroor — warna wo agle khane mein ghus kar naam ko patla kar deta hai.
- **💾 Mulazim save (v2.112.0)**: screen se `cloud.saveStaff(st,{fast:true})` (writeBatch → `{id,cred,done}`) + app.js `stWrite` — form / chips foran (stList bhi foran, warna jaldi ke doosre chip se pehla change mitta), card par ⏳, `done` par ✓ / ❌ + form wapas. Transaction (await) sirf agent ke purane actions mein.
- **🔍 Dhoondo (v2.113.0, shell.js)**: header ka 🔍 = har screen + har screen ke ⋮ chips + Settings ki har cheez (CAP `app.dhoondo`). Chips phone khud yaad karta hai (`sam-dh-cat`, screen kholte hi) — nayi screen / chip ho to bas `data-mn*` tags sahi naam (`data-mn-t`) ke saath lagao, khud aa jayegi. `dh-cat.js` = sirf pehla naqsha (test mein app chala kar bana). Settings row ko `id` + `.set-row` (setRow) se banao warna Dhoondo nahi pakarta.
- **📱 CAP har mulazim ke liye (v2.113.0)**: jo kaam har screen / har mulazim ka ho (scope koi bhi) = `CAPS.app` mein `app.<naam>` (capScopes(st) = perms + 'app'; card / form mein "📱 Har screen par"). Baqi CAP pehle jaisa scope ke andar.
- **‹ Settings ke andar wapas (v2.113.0)**: Settings khuli ho to `#dialogBody.innerHTML` ka setter (app.js dlgNav) pehli khirki ke nodes yaad rakhta hai — ‹ / phone back = ek qadam. Settings ke andar nayi khirki banao to title alag rakho; khirki ka apna "Wapas" / "‹ list" button ho to us par `data-dlg-back="1"` lagao (‹ pehle wohi dabata). Yeh setter / #dlgBack mat hatao. **Der se likhne wali khirki** (await / .then ke baad `#dialogBody` likhe) pehle apna nishan dekhe (`[data-dev-root]`, `[data-ai-ld]` jaisa) — ‹ se nikal gaye to mat likho (v2.113.1; aam hifazat dlgStale: ‹ ke baad 60 s, ungli na lagi ho, modal ke ilawa likhai = foran wapas pichhli screen).
- **🧾 Kharcha mein paisa (v2.113.0)**: HBL "Kis ko diya?" → kharcha = `bk<doc>b` (bank collection, posNo) + `bk<doc>x` (expense) · ⇄ transfer → kharcha = `kx<id>o` + `kx<id>x` (dono par `kx`). Dono entries HAMESHA saath (delete = dono, edit nahi, 🗑 wapas = dono). posNo = POS voucher nahi; Daily (galla) mein nahi (dailyIncluded false). Akhrajat screen ka "🏦 Bank / khate se kharcha" hissa inhi se. **Jora save karna (v2.113.1)**: `save()` ke andar sawal (dohri / ajeeb raqam / limit) hain — beech mein Cancel = aadha jora + dobara Save = dohra. Is liye saare sawal PEHLE ek confirm, phir dono `save(r, ex, 'noask')`, ek form = ek pakki ID (dobara Save = wohi). HBL jora `bk<sms>b`+`bk<sms>x` = `bkPair(e)` → edit / delete nahi (sirf HBL ↩). PC parchi: dailyIncluded false wale kharche par "BANK / KHATE SE · GALLE SE NAHI".
- **⚖️ Stock farq (v2.113.0, farq.js)**: ek item = sab jagah ek card. Jagah badli (ek jagah kam, doosri zyada) = DONO jagah "stock fraq" bill (Sale / Return — requestFarqQueue) ya "⇄ Transfer se theek karo" (v2.114.0 qtr.js: TN + `recountSame` dono jagah — warna wahi farq bill se DOBARA theek ho jata); "bara farq — dobara gino" sirf ASAL (jor) farq par, aur sirf FARQ wali jagah ki ginti dekho (us ginti ke din 2 dafa = theek; barabar wali jagah ki ginti nahi gintay — v2.113.1). Bill sirf malik. _postQ ek dafa 40 hisse tak. Andhi ginti (stock.see band / roz) mein farq / doosri jagah ka POS kabhi mat dikhao.
- **⇄ Jaldi transfer + 🏬 Har godam (v2.114.0)**: har stock screen par item ka ⇄ (qtr.js — apni `<dialog>`, `window.ntQtrClose` phone back) = WAHI `cloud.requestTransfer` (PC transfer-sync). Mulazim = `pw` (⛔ Band wala, rules 2.70 jaanchte — app ko password maloom nahi; banate hi `pw` mitao). Godam se stock kam = mana. "☐ Har godam" patti sirf jahan stock ≠ 0, sirf `seeStock()`. Naya stock-transfer kaam isi se karo — naya transfer form mat banao.
- **📅 Purana cash (v2.114.1)**: "Is cash se payment bhejo" = har din ka bacha `cashDays()` (pehle `src` wala din, phir apna din, phir sab se purana). Din ki chip se bheja = move mein `src`. 📅 chips sirf aaj ki tareekh par. Naya cash-bhejna kaam isi dabbe se karo. Mulazim ke custody rule (`validLastMove`) mein 'ref' + 'src' rules 2.71 se — move mein naya field = rules mein bhi.
- **💬 Paigham khirki ke upar (v2.114.2)**: `notice()` ka #toast popover (top layer) hai — khuli `<dialog>` ke upar dikhta. Khirki ke upar koi bhi naya dabba = popover / apni dialog (fixed div peeche chhup jata). Zaroori ghalti khirki ke andar bhi likho (jaise `.oc-err`).
- **🔍 Dhoondo item + ⚖️ farq (v2.114.3)**: Dhoondo ka "📦 Items" = pos-stock `window.ntItemFind` / `ntItemOpen` (Stock screen + Har godam). Farq: nahi gini + POS 0 jagah chhupi (＋ bhi gino); gini qatar par 🔁 Dobara gino (wahi oxSave). Kisi aur screen se item kholna ho to `ntItemOpen(id)`.
- **📲 Malik ki ijazat (v2.115.0)**: mulazim jo khud nahi kar sakta (purani entry badlo / hatao, farq bill, band din kholna) = `approvals` darkhwast (rules 2.71, sirf apni, status new) → malik ✓ = malik ki APP wahi kaam karti hai (mulazim ko likhne ka naya haq KABHI mat do). Naya "malik se poocho" kaam = `window.ntIjAsk({kind,title,lines,reason,rid,…})` + ijDo mein kind. Form ko darkhwast banana = `ijAsk={id,why}` (save() khud ijSend karta hai). PC auto-backup v2.1 push bhejta hai.
- **⚖️ Stock theek (v2.116.0)**: ⇄ khirki (qtr.js) ka doosra tab — do jagah asal stock → TN (note QTR_HISAAB "sirf hisaab") + baqi `requestStockAdjust` (stockCount/_adj-*, PC post-farq v18, afterTn ke BAAD; L.adj = ginti ka _posted ledger nahi). Larkon ki ginti (c-*, _posted, _bill) KABHI mat chhuo. "sirf hisaab" note (`tcHisaab`) = register mein haan, tick / raste / late / check-yaad (PC auto-backup) / raat report mein NAHI — naya TN list banao to yeh filter lagao. Mulazim ka bill = `ntIjAsk` kind farq (jobs `adj:1`).
- **📦 Packet-war bikri (rack-bharo v1.16)**: POS line ki tadad kisi packet size ke BILKUL barabar ho to hi 1 packet minus (0.250 = 250 gm). Guna (5 = 5 packet) / beech ki tadad (0.400) = khula, kabhi packet mat gino — malik: "packet ke ilawa koi bhi sale minus na ho". Sale, minus line, wapsi teeno par.
- **⏭ Roz ginti skip / ➕ mazeed (v2.117.0)**: skip = `stockCount/rozskip-<jagah>` {items:{id:{by,n,at,why,b,nm}}} (pakka, din se reset nahi; `cloud.rozSkip` TRANSACTION roz-<jagah> se bhi hatata + 1 naya item) → rozPool / rozMine / ➕ Aur / search / mahine ki coverage (roz.js locCover) sab mein filter — naya roz list / pool banao to yeh filter lagao. POS / stock / band par asar NAHI. ↩ = malik ya wohi larka (`cloud.rozUnskip` deleteField). "➕ Main ginoon" = WAHI rozClaim (ids:[id], n:1). CAPs stock.rozskip / stock.rozmore. Async ke baad `roz` dobara mat parho — pehle `const L = roz` (sk-screen class render mein pal bhar hat-ti hai; observer 400 ms baad).
- **📊 Packet % = ⚙️ Qaida (v2.118.0)**: malik — "% reh jaye to lao ka koi maqsad nahi, qaida jitna % par aaye usi waqt list mein". Packet ka apna % (rackPk p[z].pct) KABHI mat chalao / chips wapas mat lao — `pkQ()` = `rackPct()` (malik ya mulazim ka apna) || 20. % badalna sirf ⋮ → ⚙️ Qaide (Lane wale + Packet % dono tab).
- **📦 Ginti mein packet patti (v2.118.0)**: packet wale item (Packet % ya chhote sub-barcode) ki ginti = har packet ki tadad, app kg jore (`cntPk*`, CAP stock.ginpk). Doc `pkn` {size: n} + history `h.pk`; ⏳ Baqi = jama. Dukan + Packet % item + aakhri Save = rackPk `c` + `at` bhi (Rack bharo ka "abhi"). Naya ginti likhne wala code bhi yehi raasta (readCount → writeCount).
- **📦 Transfer check item-war (v2.118.0, rules 2.72)**: dene wale ka har item "de diya" = `transferGive/<TN key>.g[i]` (server) → lene wale ke "Aane wala maal" mein foran (Godam 2 jaisa). "Mil gaya" check ka qaida wahi — G (poora note diya) ke baghair nahi; sab ✓ + G der se = `tcAutoWait` (G aate hi khud). Rules na lagi = purana tareeqa + ek paigham.
- **🧾 Transfer note par tap = SIRF usi note (v2.173.1, ghalti 3 Nov)**: Transfer notes list ke card (`data-th-tc`) → `tcShow(key)` (tcFromTh, "← Wapas transfer notes" = khirki band). Kabhi ikathi "Aane wala maal" list nahi. Koi purana card / element hatao to us ko dhoondne wale sab click raaste (grep data-attr) bhi badlo.
- **🧺 Rack bharo saaf screen (v2.119.0)**: malik — "search upar · faltu cheezein nahi · tabs ⋮ mein · ek screen par 10 items · ek tick Sab items · 0 = nahi · Enter = agla · ek click print + barcode". Hero / lambi likhai wapas mat lao. Har item patli line (naam par tap = tools); patti mein = tadad > 0 (5-5 patti / ✓ tick lane mein nahi — malik ka naya hukum). ☐ Sab items = CAP stock.racksab. 🖨 = har godam ka EK note (aam + packet lines saath). Tadad likhte waqt screen dobara paint mat karo (focus na toote).
- **📊 Item report saaf (v2.141.0)**: items = card chips (poora naam, bika, 🏬 Godam · 🏪 Noor Traders stock); upar sirf patli `.ir-mini` patti (hero / KPI dabbe wapas mat lao); saare chips (mahina, waqt, wholesale, tarteeb, chart/table, reports, supplier-wise) ⋮ mein — PC par apna `.ir-optbtn` ⋮.
- **💰 Item report nafa = EK CAP (v2.142.0)**: mulazim ko nafa `ir.nafa` (OPT_IN) se — sab jagah ek saath (malik: "sab ikattha on/off", alag alag chips nahi). Item report mein naya nafa wala hissa banao to `NF()` se chhupao.
- **🛒 Supplier Order + 📦 packet size (v2.143.0)**: Supplier-wise mein Stock khana (🏬 + 🏪) + 🛒 Order = bika − stock (poore ctn, stock load na ho = Order nahi), Order PDF sirf item + tadad (rate / nafa kabhi nahi). Item report ka 📦 packet size = pk-report data (`posStats/pkReport`, sab branch jama) — nayi SQL mat banao; purane din ke hisse par "(poore X ka)" likho, chhupao mat.
- **🧾 Patli item chip (v2.144.0)**: Item report + Supplier-wise ke items = 2 line chip (naam · Rs / bika · 🏬 · 🏪, packet wale par teesri patli line) — phone par 10+ items (malik). Item list mein naya kuch dikhana ho to isi chip ki line mein (… se kato), bara dabba nahi; tafseel item khirki mein.
- **📱 Chip grid kabhi bahar nahi (v2.147.2)**: naya chip grid = `repeat(auto-fill,minmax(min(Npx,100%),1fr))` (sirf minmax(300px) phone par kat-ta hai). Item report phone par dabba-andar-dabba nahi (malik: "full size").
- **📄 PDF mein kai tables (v2.147.1)**: pdf-export kai tables ko hissa-war safhon mein deta hai sirf tab jab har `<table>` content ka SEEDHA bacha ho (h2 + table + h2 + table…). Table ko div ke andar mat lapeto. Pehle sirf pehli table batti thi, baqi har safhe par dohra kar PDF khinch jati thi.
- **🏭 Sab suppliers + 📢 demand (v2.147.0)**: item = AAKHRI khareed wale supplier ke neeche (ek hi jagah). Larkon ki khuli demand wala item har Order mein zaroor (kam az kam 1 ctn — malik ka OK). Naya order / supplier report banao to `dmMap()` demand bhi lagao.
- **🏬 Godam-wise (v2.145.0)**: Item report ka godam-war = app stock (`stockOf().at`, branch NAAM) + pk-report ki godam-war bikri (sirf tadad) — nayi SQL mat banao. Order = us godam ki bikri − us godam ka stock (supplier wala poore ctn setting). Godam chips screen par (wrap), baqi ⋮.
- **🏆 Larka ranking (v2.152.0 / v2.153.0 / v2.154.0)**: default tarteeb ⭐ SCORE (`rkCollect`: har din peers ke average se 100 = average; 50% sale + 30% gahak + 20% fi bill; jori = fi banda; 📈/📉 pichhle barabar arsay se; badges 🏅🧾💎📈). Larka report ka 🏆 = default 👥 JORI IKATTHI (malik: jori ek team, line par fi banda) ya 👤 har larka alag (jori ki sale/bill aadhi-aadhi), ek larke ke kai naam `blueAccess/larkaRank.al` (malik jode), default + 🚫 `out` bahar, din = jin din bill banaye, tarteeb roz ki sale. Larkon ka naya muqabla / inaam kaam isi `ranking()` se — naya hisaab mat banao.
- **🏷 Purchase barcode chip (v2.150.0, rules 2.79)**: item card / patti ka 🏷 = label khirki (asal + Show ✓ sub-barcode) → WAHI `cloud.requestLabel`; rate = screen ka NAYA R PCS (save se pehle bhi). Naya label kaam isi raaste (requestLabel + watchLabel status). **🔄 Refresh (v2.150.0)**: pull-to-refresh band (`html,body{overscroll-behavior-y:none}` — hatana mat); app ka har `location.reload()` se pehle `rlMark('update'|'malik')` zaroor (warna History mein jhoota "app dobara khuli").
- **✓ Check + 📌 Post ek click (v2.149.0)**: POS Purchase ka "✓ Check + 📌 Post" = pehle WAHI 💾 UPDATE (`save({direct:true})`, naye rates, aakhri sawal nahi) → PC ka `appPurchases` status done (watchAppPurchase) → check + post (`ntBcGoWait` / `ntPostWait`) → agla bill. Update na ho / PC chup = post KABHI nahi. ↩ Unpost sirf malik (patti + posted bill kholne par). Naya post / unpost kaam `postWait` (PC jawab) se — fire-and-forget nahi.
- **🏪 Online Dukan (v2.148.0, rules 2.78)**: customer website `dukan/` (bina login, Firestore REST sirf parhna) + app ki khirki `web-dukan.js` (CAP v:web). Data `webDukan/` sab ke liye KHULA — is mein khareed rate / nafa / stock ginti KABHI mat daalo (sirf naam, POS category, retail rate, unit, tasveer, dukan ka pata). `cat` = PC sync-stock pushWeb (badla ho to hi), racks/phIdx/ph_* = malik + Full mulazim. Order = WhatsApp link (koi server / payment nahi); cart ki jagah COUNTER + neeli tokri (malik). Website ka look dukan jaisa (neeli shelf patti, peeli Rs parchi, steel counter) — wapas aam cart / grid mat banao. **🎬 v2.151.0 animations** (shutter drum, batti, item lift + FLIP, tokri pop, rack 3D depth(), counter parchi print) + 🔊 WebAudio (shuru band) — LITE phone par saada; nayi class ka naam purani se na takraye (`.enter` = darwaze ka button). `html{zoom:.92}` wali app mein ungli / tasveer par dabba % se lagao, px se nahi. **🏪 v2.156.0 (malik: "customer ko lage dukan ke andar hai")**: har shelf kam az kam 4 items + 2 shelf zyada (`dims()`, `.bay.cmp`), neeche `#rkBar` racks ke chips (jis par khare = roshan), 🧑‍💼 dukan wala (bani hui SVG `AVA`, asal banda nahi — AI nahi, sirf racks chips), asal tasveerein `ph_shop` (shutter ke peeche) + `ph_` + `rkPh(rack)` (rack ke peeche; `rkPh` = hash → base36, app web-dukan.js aur dukan.js mein BILKUL ek jaisa), 🚶 `.floor`/`.ceil` + `--sx`/`--bob`, 📳 `buzz`, 🔔 ghanti, 🌙 `info.open/close` (Pakistan waqt — band = aadha shutter, order phir bhi; jhoote 'log dukan mein' KABHI nahi). **⚡ v2.156.1 (malik: "aage peeche karte hain to lag")**: scroll ke har frame mein KABHI bade dabbe (aisleWrap / bay) par CSS var mat likho (3000 items ka style dobara = 2.8 s fi frame) — sirf chhote element ka `transform` (`.floor-t`/`.ceil-t`, rack ka inline transform); scroll container par transform / translate nahi; CSS `filter: blur` nahi (rack tasveer canvas se pehle hi `soften`); door ke racks khali (`fillNear` ±2 bharo, ±5 se door khali) + `.bay{content-visibility:auto}`; DOM sirf rack badle to likho. Naya website effect isi qaide se (perf test 3000 items, CPU ×4: 18 ms/frame). **📸 v2.158.0 Asal rack ki photo = dukan** (malik: "rack ki photo par AI khud item pehchane, hum tap kar ke naam, rate naam se"): photo `ph_rp_<pid>` (≤1600px) + `racks.rp[pid] {k,o,w,h,s: JSON [[x,y,w,h,itemId] | [x,y,w,h,'',aiLabel]]}` (0–1000, % se — html zoom) + `racks.nmOn`; app ⋮ 📸 (rpe editor: 🤖 Gemini box_2d → malik tap/☑ → POS item, `sugg()` mashwara, same label khud, ✏️ khud dabba); website photo bay pehle, phir baqi items ka bana rack (photo wale dohra nahi), tap = sheet (rate cat se), tang jagah par sirf raqam / naam 🔍 zoom mein, photo ka tukda canvas se PHURL, 📱 gyro (`GY`, depth mein). Naya 'photo par' kaam isi rp data se. **🤖 v2.157.0 Racks khud banao (AI)**: POS mein category KHALI hai (sab 'Mutafarriq') — ⋮ 🤖 (sirf malik, `aiText` app.js se, Gemini key) `AIR` list se rack (Cosmetics … Chawal/Atta, Ghee & Oil aakhir, samajh na aaye = Mutafarriq), sirf jin ka `ic` nahi (haath wala kabhi nahi chhero), 150 fi chakkar → PREVIEW → malik ka ✅ Lagao hi save (order: malik ke apne rack Ghee & Oil se pehle; cn.Mutafarriq hatao). Naye rack naam isi AIR mein. App: ⋮ ☑ kai items → ek `saveWebRacks` (ic), har item ⏳/✅/✕; Settings mein 🌐 website link (Kholo / WhatsApp / Copy). **🌾 v2.159.0 Khula maal** (malik: "daalon ka section — neeche tore, upar sheeshe ke dabbe, customer chalta jaye, packet chune"): har rack mein photo bay ke baad KHULA BAY — upar kaala dabbon wala rack (aage dhalwan sheesha) + farsh par tore (jute / safed bori), daane canvas par (`dukan/khula.js` — app `web-dukan.js` bhi wahi `kindOf` import karti hai, qaida EK jagah). Kaun kahan: `racks.lp {id: g dabba | s tora | n shelf}` (malik, item khirki / ☑ kai items) warna khud (unit kg, ya packet wala daal / dry fruit; GLASS naam = dabba, baqi tora). Packet = PC sync-stock v4 `cat` row[5] `[[wazan, bechne ka rate]]` (sub-barcode, Show ✓ pehle — khareed KABHI nahi); na hon to kg wale par 250 g · 500 g · 1 · 2 · 5 kg (rate × wazan). Tokri key `id@wazan` (1 kg aam rate = sirf `id`) — tokri / parchi / WhatsApp har jagah `lines()` ka `price`. **Perf**: daane har dabbe mein alag mat banao — `tileOf` (har qism + naap ek seamless tile) + upar kinare ke asli daane; bori `weave` bhi tile; blur filter nahi; canvas `khQueue` 8 ms fi frame, door ke bay (FAR) ka canvas khali (memory). `.kbay{content-visibility:visible}` (canvas ka naap chahiye). Tasveer ho to dher ki shakal mein wohi. **🔍 v2.160.0 Smart dhoondo** (malik: "search smart nahi" — "DaDalDaD" par khali screen): `dukan/dhoond.js` `smartFind` — app (Online Dukan list, item chuno, rack photo naam) + website dhoondo EK qaida: Urdu harf → Roman, `k2` awaz (daal = dal = dhal, ee/oo, dohre harf, z=j, w=v) + `sk` consonant (thora vowel farq, `lev`), `SYN` (sugar = cheeni, rice = chawal…), "1kg" = "1 kg"; 3 se kam mile to "🤔 Shayad ye" (bigram). Naya item-search isi se banao — `includes()` wala mat likho. AI nahi. **▦ Bane hue racks BAND (v2.160.0, malik: "sab bane racks band — apne racks ki photos lagani hain")**: `racks.draw` (default false) = website par sirf asal photo bays + 🌾 dabbe / tore; baqi items sirf dhoondo se ("🧺 seedha tokri mein" → seedha khirki, `afterBack` — history.back aur pushState takrao nahi). Koi rack photo na ho = bane racks khud (dukan khali na lage). Rack number sirf nazar aane wale (`S.vis`). App ⋮ "▦ Bane hue racks: Band/ON" + 🔎 "📸 Rack photo par nahi N" (jin ki photo leni baqi). **🌾 v2.161.0 Alag section + 🌐 internet tasveerein** (malik: "wo aik alag section — naam Daalein, Masala Jaat, Cheeni, Chawal · pictures online utha lain"): sab dabbe / tore EK rack key `KHK = '@khula'` (`KH_NAME`, khula.js — app + website dono), tarteeb mein na ho to SHURU mein (`pos` −1), andar `grainRank` (dry fruit → masale → daalein → chawal → cheeni → atta); app items() mein bhi `c = KHK` (rack photo par laga item = 'n'). App ⋮ "🌐 Internet se tasveerein" (view `net`): **Wikimedia Commons** API (`origin=*`, CORS khula, muft / qanooni) — `netQuery` English lafz (chana dal, white sugar…), na mile to item ka naam; malik tap = `putPhoto(id, bytes, label, credit)` → `racks.cr[id]` (website khirki `#shCr` mein credit — qanooni haq; apni camera / gallery tasveer par credit khud hat-ta). **Google Images khud download KABHI nahi** (ijazat / copyright) — sirf "🔎 Google" chip (Google kholo) + 🖼 Gallery (wapas `net` par, `shotBack`). AI nahi.
- **🖥 Bank account ka PC auto (v2.167.0)**: HBL ke ilawa har bank account ke AAYE paise Server PC tabhi khud lagata hai jab `@pcin:<4 number>` = '1' (malik ki ijazat, HBL screen account chip → ⋮ "🖥 PC khud: ON/BAND"). 0294 (Noor Traders) + 5826 (Samiullah, malik ka OK 2 Nov) ON. Naya account = pehle malik se poochho (paise ka auto kaam). Alfalah MacroDroid trigger = Any number + contains "BAF" / "Alfalah" (sirf 8287 nahi — OTP server par na jaye). **bank-parchi v2.6 (8 Nov, malik: "aaye paisa dukan ka hai — counter sale mein jata hai, poochna kyun?")**: aaye paise par naam / khata poochna NAHI — sirf bank-khata (tgt) chahiye; Alfalah FT SMS "on 10-Nov-26 15:19:18" (DD-Mon-YY) parse; bank ki VALUE DATE (Sunday / chhutti = agle din ki tareekh) ho to SMS 6 ghante taza + tareekh −2…+4 din = khud lagao, entry AAJ (SMS ka din), note mein bank tareekh. **v2.7 (9 Nov, malik: "ye aaya paisa auto lagna chahiye")**: apne naam (Sami Ullah / Noor Traders) se AAYA paisa = pehle 5 min doosri taraf GAYE (wahi raqam ±30 min) ka intezar — mile = apna transfer (app jore), na mile = dukan ka, khud lagao. Naya bank SMS format = pehle `parseBank` test (day '' = PC khud nahi lagata, app poochti hai). Module badla to transfer-sync.js (main) bhi chhuo. **🏦 Bank parchi (pos-khata-sync k, 2 Nov)**: `bankSlip` = {dir, bk ALFALAH|HBL, at} — bank note "Alfalah/HBL aaye|gaye" se taraf (Alfalah khate par `bank` flag nahi, warna "GAYE" chhapta tha); Tafseel Urdu bina AI (roman2ur, foran); neeche "Waqt: Server > Parchi (N s)". Voucher parchi: "RAQAM WASOOL HUI" / "RAQAM DI GAYI" big2 + Urdu (malik: full size).
- **🧾 Rasid table 5 khane (nt-print v1.26 / sale-post v1.16, 7 Nov 2026)** (malik: "POS bill jaisa — pehle Rate phir Amount usi qatar mein"): `boxTable` = Items | PCS | CTN | Rate | Amt ek qatar, Font B (W×4/3 akshar), "Rs …" alag line NAHI — dono files mein EK jaisa code, ek badlo to doosra bhi. **🏦 Bank parchi par baqaya NAHI (pos-khata-sync r)**: HBL / Alfalah aaye-gaye parchi par Sabqa / Ab baqaya kabhi nahi (`receipt()` bank → before/after null). **📐 Camera khirki size (v2.192.0)**: `sam-scan-camsz` 1–5 (default 2) → `data-camsz` → CSS `--cw/--ch`; 📷 ▾ mein "📐 Size" chip — naya scanner UI isi var se.
- **👶 Bachon ke phone (v2.218.0, rules 2.99, bacha/bacha.macro)** (malik 9 Nov: "roz 1:20 · TikTok / Shorts / social / Play Store / Settings band · Phone · WhatsApp · Maps · Calendar · Alarm · Gemini · ChatGPT hamesha · patli awaaz · uninstall papa ki ijazat se · app mein kis ne kitna dekha · ek command se sab phones"): bache ke phone par EK MacroDroid macro (`bacha/build_macro.py` → `bacha/bacha.macro`, Pages par PUBLIC — chaabi KABHI nahi, har phone ka CODE = `bachaCfg/<code>` doc id phone par ek dafa likha jata hai) — sirf LOCAL variables (link / file import global nahi banata). Qaide string mein (macro REST + JsonParse se `{lv=r[fields][x][stringValue]}`): lim · add+addU · papa · stop (epoch — phone ki tareekh ki shakal par bharosa nahi) · allow / block `,pkg,`. Data `bachaUse {k,d,p}`. Macro mein MessageDialog NAHI (MacroDroid activity = khud ⛔ list mein) — FloatingText. App `bacha.js` sirf malik (`kdDlg`, `kd-*`). `DEF_ALLOW`/`DEF_BLOCK` (bacha.js) aur `ALLOW`/`BLOCK` (build_macro.py) EK jaisa rakho; macro badlo to MCP validator + bachon ke phone par link dobara. Naya "bachon ka phone" kaam isi data se. AI nahi. **📱 Ami ka phone (v2.220.0)**: ALAG macro `bacha/ami.macro` (`bacha/build_ami.py`, build_macro helpers) — doc kind 'ami' {tkL fbL ytL, tkX fbX ytX + xU, papa, block}; sirf TikTok / Facebook / YouTube ka time, baqi apps khuli; games (list + package mein 'game') / Play Store / installer / MacroDroid band; Settings KHULI, sirf us ka MacroDroid wala safha band; awaaz normal. `AMI_BLOCK` (bacha.js) = build_ami.py `BLOCK` ek jaisa. ⚙️ Bachon ke qaide sirf `kids()` par — Ami ka phone kabhi un se na badle.

POS (Cognitive) ka apna chhapa bill hum nahi badal sakte. **🔎 10 Nov (v2.227.0, pos-jaanch v1.0)**: malik ne POS bill par bhi delivery ki tahreer maangi — qadam 1 sirf jaanch (likhai DB ke kis khane mein, template files kahan → `blueAccess/posBillJaanch`, app PC scripts); POS ki koi setting / template badalna = nateeja dekh kar, malik ke ALAG OK par, backup + wapsi ke saath. - **📲 Gahak ko WhatsApp parchi (v2.196.0, PC wa-out.js + pos-khata-sync s)** (malik 7 Nov: "WhatsApp wala · ek NAYA number (business account) · SMS nahi"): dukan ka ALAG WhatsApp Business number PC par linked device (`wa-auth-out`, Baileys wahi wa-mods) — SIRF bhejta hai, aane wale paigham kabhi nahi; asal dukan number (wa-parchi) is se KABHI nahi bhejna (ban = bara nuqsan). Har nayi khata entry → `waQueue` (bank / transfer / kharcha nahi) → `waOut/<entryId>` text → wa-out (gap + roz hadd `blueAccess/waOutCfg`, WhatsApp par nahi = skip). Kis gahak ko = `party.wa` (khata ⋮, sirf malik, hash mein nahi). Setting app Settings `#waOutBtn` (`wa-out.js`). Naya 'gahak ko paigham' kaam isi queue + isi socket se — doosra bhejne wala socket / bulk kabhi nahi.
- **📋 Mubeen ki parchiyon ka peecha (v2.197.0, PC wa-parchi v2.6 `trackMsg`)** (malik: "main Mubeen ko forward karta hoon · 5 min mein 'start' warna yaad · bill ki tasveer reply = gahak ko khud · 2 parchiyan ikathi · likhi parchi bhi"): gahak → malik (sha / text yaad, download nahi) → malik → Mubeen forward = track (`TR.par`, wa-track.json; app `blueAccess/parchiTrack`) → Mubeen usi parchi par REPLY: START_RE = group start · tasveer = `waOut/pt-*` {im Bytes} gahak ko naye number se (wa-out) · bina-reply tasveer 3 min = doosra bill · `stMin` (waOutCfg) baad yaad-dihani Mubeen + malik ko (waOut). Likhi parchi sirf gahak-wali ya forwarded list — malik ki aam baat kabhi track nahi. **v2.9 (8 Nov, malik: "jis ke saath Print likha ho SIRF wo parchi")**: tasveer parchi = sirf "print" ke saath (caption / reply print / 10 min mein alag Print — `trPend`); bina print tasveer (app screenshot) = kuch nahi (9:58 ki jhooti yaad-dihani). Naya / badla phone macro = `md/tools` → `pkg.py <N>` (PAKKA m_GUID, aiGenerated 0, har item ka apna SIGUID) + push. Delete MacroDroid nahi karne deta — sirf band. md/ PUBLIC: chaabi / password KABHI nahi. Remote khud pkg mein kabhi nahi. Phone se jawab ka raasta nahi — malik batata hai. **Ghalti 6 Nov**: link se aaye macro ke chhote `globalVariables` phone par BANE HI NAHI ("Variable does not exist") — har phone macro `md/tools/full.py to_full()` se (SetVariable `createVar: true` + poore variable objects); Link import GLOBAL variable kabhi nahi banata (createVar bhi nahi) — link wale macro mein sirf LOCAL variables (`localVariables`, `{lv=}`), yaad rakhna file se (have.txt); Remote ab "🤖 Claude Remote 3". Globals sirf .mdr import (Remote) se. **Patch (Remote 4)**: phone par lage (chaabi wale) macro ka hissa badalna = pkg `patch` [{nm, cls, set}] — backup se wohi macro, chaabi kabhi bahar nahi; naya Remote = naya naam + purana `off`. Malik ke phone par "🏦 Bank mode" (bank app khule = saari accessibility + Developer options OFF, band = 30 s baad wapas) bhi chalta hai — accessibility chhoone wala naya macro is se takraye nahi. AI nahi. **📵 TikTok (pkg v5)**: `md/tools/tiktok.py` — sirf reels ("For You" screen par text) ke minute ginta hai (tkMin), tkLim (30) poore = UI click "Profile" (Profile / Saved khule); raat 12 reset; Paani popup mein minutes. Malik: SIRF TikTok (Facebook nahi); limit badalna = tkLim var Remote se. **🔒 Remote 5 (pkg v13+, 7 Nov)**: har update ke baad phone macros ka saaf export Gmail (googel6235, subject "NT phone macros vN") — Claude Gmail connector se dekhta hai; `del` list = asal delete (Replace all). **PAKKA (malik, 7 Nov: "meri ijazat ke bina koi bhi nahi hatao ge")**: phone ka koi macro malik ke saaf OK ke baghair KABHI `del` / `off` mein nahi — pehle naam likh kar poochho. NT Battery Saver + Auto Disable NFC malik ne rakhe. **pkg v23 (9 Nov)**: ⌨️ Haptics ON (battery saver mein bhi vibration — `battery_saver_constants disable_vibration=false`, bina paigham); 📵 TikTok reels ke toast KABHI nahi (malik: "baar baar aata hai"); 💧 Paani ka screen-band break HATA (malik: "mobile off kar deta hai") — wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe. Back ke baad list upar se khulti hai → pehle jitna scroll (`sc`/`rs`). Saathi = ScreenContent triggers parhte, Falcon band par bhejte; auto ke dauran chup (`at`). Macro badalna = `mac/build_falcon_v5.py` jaisa script (asal export templates), haath se JSON nahi.
- **🗺 Falcon pata list se (v2.207.0)**: MacroDroid ka text `'key' :'value'` (key ke baad SPACE) — parser hamesha key:value jore (fxDetail), akele quoted strings nahi. Auto print SIRF `data-a4-in` input (is screen ka) — kisi aur screen ki tasveer KABHI A4 / thermal ko mat bhejo (v2.174.0 ki ghalti: poori app ki tasveer bhej di thi). QAIDA: "sab / jo bhi" jaisa lafz ho aur kaam poori app ko chhoota ho to pehle poochho "alag screen ya poori app?". `.a4-pad` (number + kahan + 📷/🖼) EK dafa banta hai — paint mein kabhi dobara mat banao (picker khula ho to input hata = tasveer gum). A4 = app tasveer ke neeche number ki patti; thermal = PC parchi (TM-T88IV) par number mota. Data a4Img + a4Jobs {to, ph} → PC (browser print kabhi nahi); PC IPP JPEG → A4 PDF → Windows; 30 min purana khud nahi, ghante ki hadd = hold. AI nahi.
- **🚪 Gate pass ka record (v2.164.0, rules 2.81)** (malik: "jitne gate pass nikal rahe hain un ka record · transfer check jaisi screen usi tarteeb se · har customer alag · sirf POST hue bill ka, 1–1½ sec · upar gate pass # + customer, neeche tadad — Zeeshan / Sonu tick"): PC `pc/gate-pass.js` (server, sirf POS parhta) har 0.4 sec posted bills (aaj + kal) ki wo lines jin ka godam (GBranchID) ≠ bill ki branch → `gatePass/<SaleID>` {no, day, pt, br, p, l:[{k: ItemID_godam, n, q, pk, g}], gs, gn, off} — har counter / naya PC khud (sab ek SQL; NT-PRINT ki zaroorat nahi). Un-post / cancel = `off:1`. PC merge karta hai, `g` (tick) KABHI nahi chhuta; tick = `g.<k>` {n, t server, q} — rules sirf `g`. App `gate-pass.js` (CAP `app.gate` OPT_IN): Stock + Transfer screen ka 🚪 tile; card = bill × godam; tick ke baad tadad badli = tick khud baqi (✏️). Classes `gtp-*` / `data-gtp-*` (gaari ki `.gp-*` / `data-gp-*` alag — takrao nahi). **🔵/🔴 v2.165.0** (malik: "Bills jaisa — laal posted tick, neela un-posted baad mein laal · do ⋮"): PC v1.1 un-posted bhi (`ps` 1 neela / 2 laal, un-post = wapas 1); app saamne SIRF laal cards (+ hare chips, ⚠️ cancel jis ka maal diya); khirki header ka BAAYAN ⋮ (`#gtpLb`, ginti) = apni `<dialog id=gtpBlue>` (un-posted, tick band; phone back `ntGtpClose`); DAAYAN ⋮ = din / godam / dikhao / ginti / PDF / cancel list; 🔍 chhota chip. Naya gate-pass kaam isi data se — doosra listener / SQL mat banao. AI nahi.
- **🗄 Kai tasveerein -> shelf par khud (v2.266.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'pictures list mein lagayein, shelf par khud lagti jayein · mobile 7 fi shelf, PC 10 · rack 5-6 shelf · bina tasveer wale bilkul na dikhein · AI chhor do'): app `multi` view (`gotMulti` / `muSave`) + website `D.sc` / `D.sr` / `rk.rest` = sirf tasveer wale (tasveer ke waqt ki tarteeb), `.bay.peg` / `.d7`. Website ke auto rack par bina tasveer wala item wapas mat dikhao (▦ ON ke ilawa); rack photo par AI se item dhoondne wala kaam malik ne chhor diya — wapas mat chhero jab tak na kahe.
- **🏬 Door se dukan = website ka pehla nazara (v2.278.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'door se rack dikhe, click = paas, baqi rack bhi nazar aayein · upar ceiling, lights, cameras · poora counter · wahan banda baitha ho'): `ROOM_DEFAULT='1'` (dukan.js; kamzor phone LITE / `?kamra=0` = seedha rack). kamra.js: door ke racks par ASAL items (`.k-it`, wohi unit naap; tasveer `K.photo` / `phWant`, sirf qareeb ke ~7 rack), chhat `.k-ceil` + LED + 📹 `.k-cam`, aakhir mein `.k-end` = deewar + 🧑‍💼 `.k-man` + 🧾 `.k-ctr` EK plane (alag plane = Chrome 3D tarteeb mein tukda ghalat upar aata hai — scene mein aage-peeche wali cheezein ek element mein). Counter par baitha banda malik ka apna hukum hai (neeche wala avatar ab bhi BAND). Kamre mein filter / blur / scroll-time CSS var nahi; website par rate / maal ka naya refresh `applyFresh` se (kamra aur 'near' khirki nahi ginte). Wapas band mat karo jab tak malik na kahe; phone par dheera ho to pehle malik se poochho.
- **👯 Qatar + ↔ chaurai + 🔄 Ghumao (v2.282.0, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'ek hi item ke 4-5 piece · bohat small · chaurai barhane wale chips'): `racks.dq[id]` (dher ek qatar mein, har piece poora qad; ginti `racks.dh`) · `racks.wx[id]` (chaurai ka guna 0.5-3, tasveer khinchti hai) · `rzRotate` (tasveer 90°). Hisaab khula.js `itemU / rackLayoutU(..., ex = {dq, wx})` — app + website EK; rack ka naya naap / shakal isi `ex` mein jodo aur cloud.saveWebRacks + dono load mein field. Item ke chips sirf item dabane par neeche (saamne nahi).
- **＋ Rack wali screen = ek hi jagah sab kaam (v2.281.0, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'camera · gallery · item select · naya rack — ek poori screen, main screen wohi'): Online Dukan ki rack screen (rsz) mein search ke saath `＋` -> `rzPlusSheet` (📸 `@rz` ek tasveer = ek item · 🖼 `multi.rz` · 🔍 `rzPickSheet` ☑ + N jorein · ➕ `rzNewRack`); upar rack ka naam `#wdT` = `rzRackSheet` (rack badlo / naya / naam). Rack par item lagane ka naya raasta isi ＋ patti mein jodo (saamne naya chip nahi), data wahi `racks.ic / sh / order / cn`; 'naye items' = rack par lagana (POS mein naya item banana malik ne nahi chuna).
- **🔍 Dhoondo: godam ka stock sunehri ghere mein (v2.280.4, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'godam ke gird gold daira'): item ki line mein godam ka hissa `.dh-gd` (`ntItemFind().per` + shell.js `dhSubHTML`), dukan saada. v2.280.5: godam ka NAAM alag bhare gol `.dh-gn` mein, phir tadad (naam ka hindsa aur tadad kabhi saath-saath saada na likho — 'G1 10' = 'G110' lagta hai); line wrap, '…' se godam mat kaato. Godam ka stock kisi nayi item-line mein dikhao to yehi sunehri ghera.
- **🖼 Item ka 3D kinara + larki video ki tarah (v2.280.3, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'ek item ke ird-gird 3D border, door se mix na lagein · video ki tarah chal rahi ho'): rack ke har item par `::after` ubhra kinara (website `.it` + app `.wd-zi`, EK jaisi box-shadow — ek badlo to doosri bhi) — faasla / padding se nahi (items jure hi rahein). Larki: saans + palkein + har 8 s haath / jhukna (`wave` / `bow`), sirf nazar aate waqt. Kinara / harkat hatao sirf malik ke kehne par.
- **🙋‍♀️ Website: darwaze par khari larki (v2.280.2, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'jab bhi website par koi aaye to larki jhuk kar hello kare'): kamra.js `GIRL` + `#kGreet` = 3D scene ke BAHAR 2D parat (baayein neeche), har dafa website khulte hi `bow()` (jhukna + 'Assalam o Alaikum' bubble), aage chalne par `.away`. Hatao / jagah badlo sirf malik ke kehne par; kamre mein nayi shakal = isi tarah 2D parat (3D plane nahi), bani hui (asal insaan nahi).
- **👩‍💼 Website ke counter par larki (v2.280.1, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'yahan khubsoorat si larki bitha do'): kamra.js `MAN` = bani hui larki (lambe baal, dupatta, salaam) — topi wala aadmi wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe; shakal hamesha bani hui (asal insaan / kisi ki tasveer nahi).
- **🏪 Website saaf (v2.277.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'neeche wala baba khatam · chips small · tiles ka colour aur · daalon wala khana khatam, dabba khulna ab zaroorat nahi'): dukan wala avatar / khud khulne wala card BAND (`HELP_ON=false`, dukan.js) · 🌾 dabbe / tore ka alag section BAND (`KH_ON=false`, dukan/khula.js — app + website ek flag; ye items aam item jaise, tasveer ho to shelf par, wazan ke chips `it.kh` se wahi, parchi par `/kg`). Dono wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe; website par dabba / tora / avatar wala naya kaam mat banao. Neeche ki rack chips 25px (bari mat karo). Farsh halka slate-neela (`--floor1/2`).
- **🗺 Falcon naqsha = kahani (v2.276.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'kuch samajh nahi aaya — gaari kahan gayi, kahan se wapas aayi'): naqshe par har trip ke lambe label wapas mat lao — `fxJourney` (jagahein tarteeb se) -> upar 2 line `fxStory`, naqshe par sirf number wale gol + teer, neeche Roman list `#fxStops` (dabao = `fxLegFocus`), ▶ `fxPlay`. Malik naqshe ke Urdu naam nahi parh sakta: tiles halki + jagah ka naam hamesha Roman (`fxNm` / `fxTown`, Falcon ke pate se). Koi bhi naya naqsha (gaari GPS / delivery) banao to yehi namoona: pehle ek-do line ki kahani, phir number, phir list — sirf nuqte / lakeerein dikha kar mat chhoro.
- **🧾 Online order par 'bill' = POS bill (v2.275.0, PC wa-out v1.19)** (malik 13 Nov: 'order WhatsApp par aaye with item code — reply mein bill likhein to bill ban jaye · orders Business number par'): website order ki aakhri line `#NT <POS id>x<tadad> … =<kul>` (dukan.js) -> Business number se us order par REPLY `bill` -> wa-out `billTry` -> `appSales/wb<order id>` {role staff} -> sale-post ka PURANA raasta (un-posted counter bill, POS ke rate, print). POS mein likhne wala naya raasta mat banao; rate kabhi paigham se nahi; ek order = ek bill (doc id); gahak ka paigham khud kabhi nahi parhna (sirf apne reply ka quoted order); nateeja malik ko. Order ka format badlo to `NT_RE` (wa-out) bhi. **🔄 Website rate khud taza**: `peek()` har 60 s sirf 'kab badla' (mask) — sirf rate badle = `.tag` apni jagah (`ratesOnly`), maal badla = rack dobara jab khirki band ho; website par rate dikhane ki nayi jagah banao to `ratesOnly` mein bhi (warna purana rate reh jayega). **📲** website ka order number = `waLinkOut.me` ek dafa khud (`info.waAuto`).
- **🤖 AI auto crop (v2.274.0)** (malik 13 Nov: '1 bana do — background hatne ka koi issue nahi'): Online Dukan mein EK item ki tasveer aate hi `acRun` (web-dukan.js) Gemini `A.aiImage` se cheez ka dabba (`box_2d`) ✂️ Crop screen mein khud lagata hai — save hamesha malik ke ✅ Save par (AI khud kabhi save nahi); malik ne dabba chhoo liya ho to zabardasti nahi (`C.touch` -> 🤖 AI chip). Sirf kaatna — peecha mitana (cut-out) malik ne nahi chaha. Rack ki photo (kai items) / kai tasveerein ek saath par AI nahi (malik: wahan AI chhor do). Tasveer ka naya AI kaam isi `aiImage` + chip ⏳ / ✅ / ✕ namoone se.
- **🏷 Website: ek patli patti + 📦 rack dabbe jaisa (v2.273.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'Noor Traders wali patti aur rack ke naam ki patti ikathi, chhoti · rack deewar jaisa nahi — shelf ke 4 kone, item ke peeche usi ki mazeed items · item shelf ke upar tak'): phone (`html.one`, ek rack saamne) par latakta board (`.sign`) NAHI — rack ka naam upar ki patti mein (`#where` .wk / .wn); wapas mat lao. Rack ki shakal PAKKI (`--rh` = rows x `rkDeep().shU` x u) — rack ko screen jitna lamba mat khincho (item ke upar faasla aa jata); lambi screen ki bachi jagah sirf `pk` (peeche ki qatar) ko. Har shelf `.sbx` (farsh + deewarein + peeche jaali) + item ke peeche 2 qatarein `.bh` (`depthHTML`, `--dx`, `--rz`; LITE phone par nahi; `data:` link par nahi — `asBlob`). Rack ke andar z-index ki tarteeb (rk-bg -4 · sbx -3 · b2 -2 · b1 -1 · `.it` auto) mat chhero — `.it` ko z-index do to pichhli qatar parosi item ke upar aa jati hai. Website par naya effect = blur / scroll-time var nahi (purana qaida). Sabaq: malik ek jawab mein 'theek hai' + naye kaam jore to wo usi kaam ka hissa hain — kar do, sawal (jaise 'AI kar sakta hai?') ka sirf jawab do, banao nahi.
- **🧲 Items jure hue + ✂️ Crop + 📸 Camera (v2.272.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'item ke darmiyan faasla nahi · shelf kam nazar aaye, item zyada · item lagate waqt crop / camera'): bane hue rack ka naap UNIT mein (`dukan/khula.js` `itemU` / `rackLayoutU`, shelf = 100 unit, `RK_RA` 0.66 (v2.273.0), `RK_ROWS` 5) — item ki oonchai = shelf jitni x `racks.sz` (0.4–1), chaurai = tasveer ki apni shakal `racks.ar[id]` (app rsz khud likhti hai; website `fitIt` tasveer load par theek karti hai). Shelf par ginti fixed NAHI (7 fi shelf wala qaida khatam); items ke beech gap / padding wapas mat lao; rack par naya kaam isi unit se (px / cols se nahi) taake phone, PC aur app preview par har shelf par WAHI items. Grid ke andar flex shelf = `grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)`. Rack ke size se camera / crop = purani crop screen (`shotBack 'rsz'`), nayi mat banao.
- **🧱 Dher (v2.271.0)** (malik 13 Nov, Lux ke dher ki photo: 'item bara karein to mazeed soap jur jayein'): `racks.dh[id]` (2–12) — wohi tasveer dohra kar dher (`dherHTML`, khula.js; app + website). Har item ka apna chip (malik ne 'OK' — alag chip wala tareeqa); sirf dikhawa, stock / ginti se taluq nahi; rate ki parchi ek.
- **⚠️ Click ke baad paint = `closest()` jhoota (v2.270.2, ghalti 13 Nov)**: apna handler paint() kar de to element DOM se hat jata hai — document-level handler mein `e.target.closest('#x')` null deta hai. Kisi khirki ko document click se bahar rakhna ho = `e.composedPath()` se jaancho. Rack ke item ki oonchai = us ka apna size (`--hp` px), kabhi doosre item ke hisaab se nahi.
- **☰ Recent chips mein khate NAHI (v2.270.1)** (malik 13 Nov: 'yahan par khaton ke names nahi aane chahiye'): recent.js `k:` keys band — ☰ 'Is screen ke' mein sirf khirkiyan / screen ke kaam. Khaton ke naam wapas mat lao; khata kholna = list / 🔍 Dhoondo.
- **📐 Rack ka size (v2.270.0)** (malik 13 Nov: '⋮ mein · rack chips ek Category chip mein · ek rack par kitni shelf · pehle shelf chuno phir search se lagao · item ka size box se haath se'): `racks.rn` (shelf 3–9) · `racks.sh[id]` (chuni shelf) · `racks.sz[id]` number — naqsha `rackLayout` (khula.js; app `rsz` + website). v2.267.0 ka pinch / Bare-Chhote / Chhota-Aam-Bara UI HATA — wapas mat lao. Online Dukan ke ⋮ mein racks ki lambi chips qatar wapas nahi (📂 Category chip).

- **⚠️ `data-*` naam ka takrao (v2.268.1, ghalti 13 Nov)**: Online Dukan ki tasveer `data-th` = pos-stock ke document click `[data-th]` (Transfer register) — rack size mein item dabane par register khul gaya. Naya `data-` naam rakhne se pehle poori repo mein grep karo (document-level click handler); apni khirki ke naam apne prefix se (`data-wd-…`).

PC opening JV ab KABHI khud nahi; bank / HBL khata opening tool mein nahi; list mein khud ☑ nahi. Har opening theek "↩ Opening theek ki jaanch" se wapas ho sakta hai (milanJobs `@undo-opening`). CASH bill ka app hissa = `payment` + posNo + dailyIncluded false (purchaseCash NAHI — purane din ki Daily badal jati). Naya "bill farq" kaam isi function / doc mein.
- **🏭 Supplier-wise sale (v2.140.0)**: Item report ⋮ → "🏭 Supplier-wise" (CAP ir.sup OPT_IN): supplier search list → items (365 din ki khareed, PC item-report v1.8 `posStats/irep-sup`) ki bikri item report ke waqt mein. NAFA KABHI NAHI (screen / PDF). Khane ☑ Sale rate (default) · ☐ Purchase rate (default band — PDF supplier ko ja sakti). PDF = pdfOf (kholo + bhejo).
- **🧾 Bills chips (v2.137.0 / v2.138.0)**: "🧾 Bills" khirki poori screen — saamne SIRF bill: upar chhota 🔍 search chip + ⋮ (PC par apna `nb-more`, phone par header ⋮); Un-posted (neele) CHHOTE chips UPAR, Posted (laal) bare neeche, kul aakhri N (⋮: 10/15/20/25/35, tareekh, un-posted print, PC sync). Tabs (Sab/Posted/Un-posted…) aur total ki line NAHI (malik) — wapas mat lao. PC pos-sales-dekho v1.9: har 1 sec, bills ka haal PEHLE likho. **⚡ v2.1 / app v2.155.0 (malik: "scan ho aur yahan 1 second mein bill")**: har ½ sec; badlaav par sirf naye + badle (UpdatedOn) + aakhri 15 bill parho aur cache ko COUNT/MAX/SUM se milao (na mile = poori list) — Daily Sale / items / `sum` ALAG peeche ki lane (`slow()`), bill ka likhna kabhi un ka intezar na kare; main doc `set(...,{merge:true})` + `pm` raftar (app ⋮ ⏱ Raftar). Naya bill / naya post = chip ✨ chamak (`.nb-new` ::after, theme ka box-shadow !important), 🔔 awaaz ⋮ (CAP sale.bsnd, sam-bills-snd, shuru band). Bill ke raaste mein naya bhaari kaam mat daalo — peeche ki lane mein daalo. v2.139.0: Posted (laal) = POST KE WAQT se (naya post pehle, chip par 📌 waqt) — `pt` PC (pos-sales-dekho v2.0, pos-posted-seen.json; shuru se pehle wale = UpdatedOn). PC scan screen ki baayein list pehle jaisi rakho (malik). Bill ki tafseel = `nbDetHTML` (dono jagah).
- **🧾 Bills ⋮ + 20 laal (v2.167.1 / v2.168.0)** (malik: "amount small, number bara · un-posted right side three dot ke andar · search upar pakki · 20 laal chips ek screen par"): laal / bare card = bill no 22px, raqam 15px, patla (PC 5 × 4 = 20); 🔵 un-posted SIRF search ke daayein ⋮ ka panel (`.nb-upan`, badge `.nb-ub` ginti + chamak, 🖨 print wahin) — saamne wapas mat lao (search likha ho to milte un-posted saamne theek); N chips = sirf POSTED. `.nb-stick` (search + ⋮ + panel + PC options) sticky top:-14px — `.nb-wrap.nb-chipv` overflow CLIP (hidden sticky tor deta hai).
- **↕ Khate ki tarteeb (v2.171.0)** (malik: "app jaisi tarteeb PC / PDF / print par bhi · malik ka default"): `blueAccess/look.khOrd` ('new' naya upar = default | 'old'), sirf malik khate ke ⋮ (`.dt-ord`) se badle, sab phone / PC par khud. Screen + PDF + 🖨 thermal EK `khOrdered(rows)` se — naya khata list / PDF / print banao to yehi lagao (warna tarteeb alag ho jati). Thermal parchi par sirf RAQAM moti (`segs` + ESC E), tareekh / note patle.

Khate ki koi nayi list / print / PDF banao to har entry ke saath yehi baqaya.
- **🖨 Daily khane ka print (v2.172.0, rules 2.83, PC pos-khata-sync n)** (malik: "wasooli ka alehda · akhrajat ka alehda · koi bhi print kar sakta hai"): khane ki neeli patti ka `.dy-tp` chip (CAP d.tprint) → WAHI `khataPrints` (j.k 'day') → PC `daySlip`. **v2.173.0**: Sale + Change·Closing khane par bhi chip, toolbar `#dyAll` 🖨 Sab print (malik: "akhrajat zyada — do line, neeche wasooli, page kam lage" → PC daySlip 'a' = Akhrajat 2-2 khane, neeche Wasooli 2-2, phir + / − hisaab + KAM / ZYADA; raqam / farq WAHI `daily()`). Naya thermal list-print banao to isi raaste (`requestKhataPrint` + j.k naya + PC dispatch) — nayi collection / rules mat banao. Raqam PAISA bhejo.
- **📲 WhatsApp Parchi Sale (v2.178.0, rules 2.86, PC wa-parchi v1.0)** (malik: "parchi WhatsApp se khud · upload nahi · auto thermal + A4 · pehle maal nikle bill baad · Purchase jaisi screen ditto, default PCS"): malik gahak ki parchi dukan ke number par FORWARD karta hai (caption = gahak ka number / naam) → PC linked device (Baileys, ALAG folder wa-mods) sirf `waParchi.allow` numbers → Gemini → `pc/dhoond.mjs` (dukan/dhoond.js ki HUBAHU copy — dhoond badlo to yeh bhi) → `parchiOrders` + `parchiImg` → thermal + A4 "BILL BAAQI" (❓ = parchi ka tukda). Naya parchi / WhatsApp kaam isi screen + isi data se. PC par npm install sirf apne folder mein (C:\khata-sync mein karne se firebase-admin / mssql mit jate) aur `exec` se (execFile npm.cmd = EINVAL).
- **📷 Camera screen v2 (v2.179.0 / v2.179.5)** (malik: "camera on ho to items nazar aayein · 123 search ke neeche · side buttons chip ke peeche · 10 items" · v2.179.3: "camera background mein full size" — Sale + Purchase + WhatsApp): phone (<1100px) par `.scan-box.v2` — camera = `📷 ▾` ke saath CHHOTI LIVE KHIRKI (`.scan-row2>.scan-cam` 72×44, tap = focus; v2.179.5 malik: "scanning nahi ho rahi" — v2.179.4 ka poori screen peeche CHHUPA camera = nishana nahi lagta / kaun sa lens pata nahi; chhupa / poori screen camera wapas mat lao), `📷 ▾` chip ke peeche Focus / Zoom / 🔄 / band / Light, 123 patti search ke neeche, list naya upar patli (tap = khane), neeche ek patti (✓ Ho gaya · Kul · ✕). PC / transfer / pick camera purana. Line ka godam = EK chip (dabao = agla: NT → G1 → G2 … → NT) — scanner (`gChip`) + WhatsApp lines (`.wps-gc`). WhatsApp mein Gate pass switch NAHI (malik: zaroorat nahi). Scanner hooks global — screen jo hook use na kare `null` kare (purchase: `setSaleLineGodamHook(null)`).
- **👦 Larka chip (v2.180.0, rules 2.87, PC sale-post v1.14)** (malik: "jis larke ke naam par sale jaye — larke POS mein jorte hain — godam jaisa ▾ chip upar"): `sale-man.js` (Sale + WhatsApp Parchi Sale, CAP sale.larka) — list SIRF POS se (`blueAccess/supplyMen`, PC likhe), app mein larka banana nahi. Bill doc `sm` → PC POS `Sale.SupplyManID` (POS list mein na ho = null). Naye sale / bill screen par larka chahiye to yehi `smChipHTML` / `smForDoc`. Doc mein naya field = rules hasOnly mein bhi + `saveAppSale` jaisa bina-field fallback (rules der se lagti hain). **🚪 Gate pass switch ON** (`gate > 0`) = PC har godam ki parchi chhape, NOOR TRADERS ki bhi (sale-post `printGatePasses` gateOn + nt-print `gateJobs`) — teeno print raaste (PC / NT-PRINT / Yehi device) ek qaida.
- **🔕 Ajeeb raqam sawal BAND (v2.180.1)** (malik: kharcha entry par "aam raqam … bohat ZYADA" popup — "pehle nahi tha"): `oddAmountMessage` = `ODD_AMT_ON false`, har khate par. Wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe.
- **⚡ Voucher + parchi FORAN (v2.180.2, PC pos-khata-sync q)** (malik: "waiting time nahi — parchi usi time, voucher bhi foran, har entry"): PC mein nayi entry ka voucher ALAG line (`vRun`, kabhi print ke peeche nahi), parchi `printReceipt(text, true)` = printer mein AAGE (khata / Daily / galla / dobara = peeche), nayi parchi par Urdu Tafseel AI ka intezar nahi (roman2ur foran), har print ki hadd 45 s. Naya entry-print kaam = `hi` true. App card: `pcWaitOf` ⏳ → 15 s ⚠️ PC dheera (khamosh nahi). Bills search: sirf hindse = bill number ka aakhir EXACT (smart / fuzzy nahi); raqam se talaash KABHI nahi (v2.180.3 malik).
- **📒 Naam seekho / 🧠 yaad (v2.181.0, PC wa-parchi v1.6)** (malik: "jin ka naam nahi milta alag khane mein — hum AI ko yaad karwa dein · agli dafa yaad naam + parchi ka lafz ikattha chhape"): `wa-learn.js` (WA ⋮, sirf malik) — 30 din ki ❓ lines `lkey` se ek qatar → item = `blueAccess/waLearn.m`. PC: EXACT lkey = pakka item (`y:1`, s ≥ 0.7), thermal ">> yaad: <item>", AI prompt mein 150 yaad lafz (`glossOf`). Fuzzy yaad KABHI nahi (patti bari ≠ patti chhoti). Parchi ka naya 'yaad' kaam isi data se — itemAliases (purchase ke naam) alag rehne do.
- **✅ WhatsApp parchi PAKKA jaanch (v2.182.0, PC wa-parchi v1.8)** (malik: "white chana → shawer, maida → pista bill mein · tadad na likhi ho to poocho · garam masala pao = 0.250 packet khud"): bill mein khud SIRF jab parchi ka har asal lafz item ke naam mein ho + size na takraye (`pickPakka` → `covers` / `sizeTier`: 10 litre ≠ 900ml, pao ≠ 475gm, 3 kilo = 3kg); warna wohi item ❓ "andaza" (j `z` w|s). smartFind (dhoondo) ka adhoora / prefix milan parchi ke liye KABHI pakka mat maano. Tadad likhi nahi = Gemini `qn` → j `nq` → app line `ask` (peeli + "❓ Tadad?", Bill banao ROK) + thermal ">> TADAD ?". Wazan `qtyOf`: kilo wala / sub-barcode (bq) = wazan (0.250 + 📦 chip) · naam ka size = 1 / guna · na bane = ask — kabhi gol kar ke 1 (poora kilo) nahi.
- **🎙 Meri baatein (v2.183.0, rules 2.90)** (malik: "har baat record → AI notes · calls bhi · jagah ka naam · PC nahi · Drive + Sheet mein recording · Blue Khata mein bhi"): MacroDroid (bahar; dukan Wi-Fi / charging / gaana / call par NAHI) → phone folder "NT Awaz" (recording kabhi nahi mitti) → FolderSync two-way → Drive → malik ka Apps Script `awaz-gas.js` (jagah: Sheet "Jagahein" → Google Maps; file ka naya naam "Jagah - din waqt"; Gemini Roman Urdu notes; Sheet "Meri Baatein" ▶ Suno + 📍 Map) → `awazNotes` (khufia chaabi `blueAccess/awazKey`) → app `awaz.js` SIRF malik (☰ 🎙, apni dialog, ☐ kaam `dn`). AI sirf Apps Script mein — app mein nahi. Gas code mein backtick / dollar-brace KABHI nahi (String.raw). Naya 'malik ki awaz' kaam isi data se.
- **🧠 6 Qaide card (v2.183.1)**: `qaide.js` sirf malik — app khulte hi + har 2 ghante popover card (☰ 🧠 6 Qaide). **Popover + khuli modal `<dialog>`**: modal baqi document ko inert karta hai — click wala popover KHULI modal khirki ke andar append karo (dikhega par ungli nahi chalegi warna). Toast jaisa sirf-dikhane wala body mein theek.
- **🖨 WhatsApp "print" = A4 (v2.185.0, PC wa-parchi v2.0)** (malik: "Mubeen ke number par parchi bhejun, saath PRINT likha ho to A4 se print · print ke baghair nahi · koi bhi kaghaz · kisi ko kuch na karna pare"): PC malik ke APNE WhatsApp (0345-0412515) par juda — malik ke apne bheje (fromMe) paigham SIRF tab chhoo jab tasveer + `printReq` caption + chat `waParchi.prTo` (default Mubeen) ya apni chat → `a4Send` (wahi tasveer, AI / header nahi) → a4-print. Baqi apne paigham KABHI nahi parhne / chhapne. BILL BAAQI A4 khud nahi (`a4m` print|all|off). Naya 'WhatsApp se A4' kaam isi a4Send se. **v2.185.1 (ghalti 5 Nov)**: malik album bhej kar NEECHE alag "Print" likhta hai — "print" hamesha caption AUR alag chhote paigham dono mein pakro (usi chat ki pichhli 10 min ki tasveerein); har nateeja `waLink.pr` se app mein dikhao.
- **🖨 Print wala doosra phone (v2.186.0, PC wa-parchi v2.2)** (malik: "Mubeen apni khud ki chat mein print bheje to wo bhi auto print"): PC Mubeen ke WhatsApp par ALAG linked device (`wa-auth-print`, `blueAccess/waLink2` + hukum `waCmd2`) — SIRF us ke apne bheje + SIRF apni chat (Message yourself) → WAHI `selfPrint(m, s, CX2)` → A4; aane wale paigham / doosri chats KABHI nahi (order / AI / note bhi nahi). Jorna app ⚙️ se: 🔢 code (`requestPairingCode` — malik ghar, larka dukan) ya QR. Naya 'kisi aur ke phone se print' kaam isi CX raaste — teesra socket bina malik ke OK mat banao. **v2.187.0 (malik: "main logout kar doon, sirf Mubeen ka WhatsApp chale")**: print phone par AANE wale paigham SIRF `waParchi.prFrom` (default malik) se (CX3) — doosra number kabhi nahi; malik ka link bhi chale to dohra print mat hone do (CX3 chup jab malik + Mubeen prTo). Malik ka Unlink = `waLink.out` → PC khud QR nahi banata. Parchi Sale order Mubeen ke number par NAHI (malik: B). **v2.188.0 (malik: "gahak ki chat se — sirf tasveer ke SAATH print ya hum REPLY mein print")**: print phone `p2Msg` — gahak = SIRF caption print (alag likha / reply print = nahi), Mubeen / malik = reply-print (quoted tasveer, purani bhi). A4 ke upar `hd` patti (Tel · naam · likhe number · card) a4Jobs.hd → PS_PRINT -Heads. Group / status KABHI nahi; bina print tasveer download bhi nahi. 5 safhe fi ghanta ki hadd malik ne abhi NAHI chahi. **v2.189.0 (malik: "mera bhi same Mubeen wala")**: malik ke APNE WhatsApp (main socket) par bhi `mainExtra` — malik ka reply-print + gahak caption print (ijazat list = purana order raasta); dono socket ek hi helpers (replyOf / printOne / noteChat); malik ka link chale to P2 malik ka reply NAHI chhapta (dohra nahi).
- **🖨 HP "Manually feed" NAHI (v2.185.2, PC a4-print v1.3)** (malik: "har dafa dabna parta ha · speed tez · dabna na pare"): Windows print mein kaghaz KABHI zabardasti A4 mat karo — printer ka apna default (A4 / Letter) + ghair-manual tray (zabardasti size = HP Continue maangta). Har print ka "INFO kaghaz | tray" → `a4Pc.pi` (app ⚙️ 📄). **v1.6 (8 Nov)**: WiFi IPP raaste par bhi `media` A4 NAHI bhejna (sirf `cfg.a4Force`) — v1.3 ne sirf Windows raasta theek kiya tha, IPP par HP phir bhi Continue maangta tha (malik: "button dabna parta hai"). Kisi bhi print raaste mein kaghaz ka size zabardasti kabhi nahi. WhatsApp kaghaz (job k 'wa') = `cleanDoc` saaf PNG sirf jab kaghaz lage (photo / cheez = asal); bahar ka code (encoder waghera) repo mein KABHI copy nahi — apna likho. **v1.4 (malik: "parchi clear nahi")**: tone ka kala / safed point KABHI poori tasveer se mat lo (phone ka kala frame = phiki likhai safed) — syahi ka andaza sirf un pixels se jin ke paas kaghaz ho (`maxNear`), bara kala hissa halka gray; naya saaf karne ka kaam phiki pen + phone screen ki photo par test karo.
- **🔎 Parchi thermal = ASAL LIKHAI, har line zoom (v2.184.0, PC wa-parchi v1.9)** (malik: "Tibet cream ke zoom jaisa har item · AI ghalat parhta hai — ghalti bilkul nahi · auto print bhi aisa hi"): tasveer wali parchi (App + WhatsApp) ka thermal = `zoomSlip` (linePlan: flatten · skew · chhapi lakeerein sirf PEHCHANNE se bahar · mura safha dewarp · line-war 512 zoom, beech ka khali faasla sukera) — AI ka parha kabhi thermal par nahi (setting `waParchi.zm` false = purani AI list). Khana / kinara tabhi kaato jab likhai ki syahi 0 AUR asal syahi sirf lakeer jitni — likhai kabhi na kate; samajh na aaye = purana poori parchi zoom. App parchi = tasveer aate hi (`zoomNow`, AI ke saath saath); WhatsApp = AI "order hai" ke baad. Naya parchi-print kaam isi `zoomThermal` se.
- **🖼 Parchi jaisi print — bina AI (v2.181.1, rules 2.88, PC wa-parchi v1.7)** (malik: "har item jaise likha · AI ke baghair alag chip · zoom"): SIRF ⋮ chip (malik ne chuna) → `prImg` → PC poori parchi ki tasveer thermal par, `zoomPlan` (khali kinare kaat + column ka faasla ho to daayan hissa pehle). Khud har parchi par NAHI — sirf chip se.
- **↩ Daily Sale = POS sale − Sale Return (PC pos-sales-dekho v2.2, 4 Nov)** (malik: "sale return add hai ke nahi"): auto possale = dbo.Sale (cash poora + wholesale poora + udhaar ka mila cash) MINUS dbo.SaleReturn (cancel / stock fraq bahar; udhaar party ki wapsi minus NAHI — cash nahi gaya; wholesale wapsi minus). Daily / galla ka naya sale hisaab isi `saleSum(rows, crv, rets)` se.
- **🖥 PC ka naya CSS = `html.pcm …` se shuru (v2.250.1)**: `@media (min-width:1100px)` mein bina `.pcm` wala selector `tools/pcm.py` agli dafa `html:not(.pcm)` bana deta hai (PC par lagna band). Khanon ke naam / label 14px se chhote mat rakho (malik ki nazar).
- **🤖 Bill ki jaanch: items upar (v2.250.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'items ke naam upar, baqi chips neeche small ek line mein — scroll kam'): `aiRender` = pehle `jListRows`, baqi sab `.jz-low` (neeche chipki ek line). Jaanch / list wali kisi bhi khirki mein asal list ke UPAR tabs / ginti / warning / bara button mat rakho — neeche ki patli line mein.
- **🧺 Stock kabhi 0 mat dikhao jab maal ho (v2.249.0)**: carton wale item ka stock `rkQty` = 'N ctn + M pcs' (carton se kam = pcs) — floor kar ke '0 ctn' kabhi nahi. Naya stock dikhana bhi isi se. Item report: Godam / Noor Traders stock `.ir-ring` gol ghere mein.
- **⚠️ Shell: `sed -n "${n}p"` se pehle `n` khali na ho** (13 Nov: khali n = poori 600 KB file chhap gayi) — hamesha `[ -n "$n" ] &&` ya `| cut -c1-300`.
- **🖥 PC = MOBILE jaisi poori app (v2.248.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'sab screens mobile jaisi, neeli list bhi ☰ mein — POS purchase / AI chhor kar'): PC par `html.pcm` + `window.ntPcm`; mobile CSS ki copy `pcm.css` (KHUD BANI — `python3 tools/pcm.py`), PC CSS `html:not(.pcm)`. **style.css ka koi mobile block (`max-width:1099…`) badlo to `tools/pcm.py` dobara chalao** (warna PC purana dikhega). PC ke liye alag layout / sidebar / desk rule naya mat banao — ek hi (mobile) dhancha; JS mein phone ki jaanch = `window.ntPcm ||` ke saath. Wapsi ka raasta `?pc=old` (v2.316.0 se sirf usi session — agli dafa khud mobile jaisi).
- **🧮 Raqam + rate = tadad khud (v2.288.0, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'raqam likhein aur rate likha ho to pcs khud aaye'): purchase line mein tadad khali ho to `qFromAmt` (raqam ÷ khareed; carton wala CTN + PCS) — larke ki likhi tadad KABHI nahi badalti (tab raqam se rate banta hai). Purchase ka naya khana / form banao to yehi qaida. **％ W button (v2.289.0, malik ki tasveer)** = chamakta gol `.pp-wchip` + on/off switch `.pp-wsw`, list `.pp-wdrop` button ke NEECHE seedhi (ek ke neeche ek, har line par %) — alag khirki (dlg) nahi, chaurai wali chips qatar nahi. Malik: chhote chunao ke liye nayi khirki mat kholo; chunao ki list neeche ki taraf kholo. Is button ki shakal (neeli chamak + switch) malik ki pasand hai — on/off wala naya button banao to yehi namoona.
- **🧾 Phone Purchase: patti nahi + ％ chip + item ke andar chips (v2.287.0, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'neeche ki patti khatam · upar daayein ek % chip — sab items par, sirf wholesale carton + piece · godam item ke andar · items pehle se khule'): `CBAR_ON=false` — footer ki chips patti wapas mat lao; W % = `.pp-wchip` -> `wAllSet` (phone + PC ek); item ke chhote kaam (🏬 godam · 🏷 · ↺ · ✕) usi item ki doosri line `.pp-tl` mein — naya item-kaam bhi wahin. Items khule hi rahein (band / tap-to-open nahi), tang rakho. **🤖 Lagi tasveer se AI (phone + PC)**: chipki bill tasveer ka '🤖 Is se items' (`picAi` -> `aiRead {ai:true}`) — PC par AI SIRF is chip se (tasveer chun-ne par ab bhi sirf tasveer; v2.261.0 ka qaida itna badla).
- **🤖 Bill ki jaanch ka page BAND (v2.286.0, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'yeh poora page nahi hona chahiye — items par direct likha ho, naam yaad hai / nahi'): `JZ_PAGE=false` (purchase.js) — AI ke items seedhe bill ki list mein, har item ke neeche `.pp-jl` chip (📌 yaad / ❓ yaad nahi, dabao = item badlo) + bill ka naam; item na mila = laal `.pp-jnr`. Jaanch ka list / card / khulasa page wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe; AI ki line ka naya haal = isi patli line / upar ki `posJbar` patti mein. Naam bill save par yaad (`jLearnSave`), foran nahi. Supplier ke bill ka naam humare item se alag ho sakta hai — screenshot dekh kar 'ghalat' mat kaho, poochho.
- **🖥 PC par Jaanch = POS table (v2.257.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'Jaanch ki poori screen khatam, POS purchase jaisa — upar picture, baqi neeche'): PC (posMode) mein `aiRender` / `jCard` / `jSummary` = `posJ()` — Jaanch ka dialog / card PC par wapas mat kholo; AI ki line ka haal = table ki qatar ka rang (jy peeli / jr laal / jn item nahi mila) + upar `#ppPosPat` patti + `.pp-posj` ek line. Jaanch ka naya kaam = phone ke liye dialog mein, PC ke liye isi table / line mein dono jagah.
- **📲 Reply 'ai' = purani parchi (v2.256.0, wa-out v1.17 / wa-parchi v2.29)**: Business number par pehle bheji tasveer par reply mein `ai` = wa-out quoted tasveer download kar ke waIn (`qi`) -> `purAiPut` (purAi `th` chhoti tasveer). 'Reply mein lafz' wala naya WhatsApp kaam = quoted tasveer isi tareeqe se (bzPend ki 10 min yaad par bharosa nahi); bhejne wale ko hamesha jawab.
- **🖥 PC POS Purchase khanon ki tarteeb (v2.255.0)**: naam ke foran baad **Amount**, phir CTN Price · PCS Price · Pack Qty · Qty · Branch (malik ne khud rakhwaya — POS ki asal tarteeb par wapas mat le jao).
- **📄 PC POS Purchase mein AI NAHI (v2.261.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'AI khatam, bas poore bill ki picture laga do jo hum select karein'): PC (posMode) par `aiRead` AI nahi chalata — tasveer sirf table ke upar lagti hai (`billPin`), items haath se. PC purchase mein AI se items parhna wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe (phone par pehle jaisa).
- **📲 WhatsApp ki bill tasveerein = chips, 1 din (v2.260.0, wa-parchi v2.30)** (malik: 'chhote chips · ek din rahe, agle din delete · dobara select se bhi khule'): `wai.day()` 24 ghante (parhi hui '✓' ke saath bhi chunne ke qabil), 'Bill ki tasveer se items' khirki mein seedhe chips. purAi ko parhne ke baad chhupao / mitao mat — 1 din tak dobara khulni chahiye.
- **⌨️ Search mein do Enter + Total neeche (v2.259.0)**: PC purchase search mein likhe naam par Enter = pehli item par roshni, doosri Enter = jorna (barcode ke hindse par Enter seedha jorta — scanner na toote). Table ka dabba `posFit` se screen bharta hai taake Total ki line hamesha neeche rahe.
- **⌨️ Qatar ka aakhri khana -> search (v2.258.0)**: PC purchase table mein qatar ke aakhri khane se Tab / Enter = item search (agli item). Keyboard ka chakkar: search -> ↓ item -> Tab qatar -> Tab … aakhri -> search.
- **⌨️ Table mein ↑ ↓ = qatar badlo (v2.254.0)**: PC purchase table ke number khanon mein arrow se raqam KABHI na badle (malik: ghalti se amount barh jata) — ↑ ↓ usi khane ki upar / neeche wali qatar. PC ki nayi table banao to yehi.
- **⌨️ PC POS Purchase keyboard (v2.253.0)** (malik: 'search se ↓ items mein, ← → aage peeche, Tab = chuni item ki qatar'): PC purchase mein naya chunne wala hissa banao to keyboard (arrow / Tab / Enter) se bhi chale — malik PC par mouse kam chahta hai. Table par `min-height` zabardasti mat lagao (Total footer ke neeche chhupta) — `posFit` par chhoro.
- **🖥 PC POS Purchase: 15 items + godam qatar se (v2.252.0)** (malik: 'kam az kam 15 items ek screen · godam sirf screen (qatar) se, upar wale khane se nahi'): PC purchase header mein Branch wapas mat lao; qatar EK line (22px) — qatar mein doosri line / bara khana mat joro; upar ka har naya hissa = usi line mein. Sticky sar-khi ke saath sticky column mat rakho (z-index takrao).
- **🖥 PC POS Purchase: pattiyan nahi + scroll nahi (v2.251.0)** (malik 13 Nov: 'dono pattiyan khatam · sirf wholesale % upar, sab items par auto · POS jaisa scroll na karna pare'): PC (posMode) mein neeche ki chips patti / W-R chips ki qatar wapas mat lao; Wholesale = header ka EK 'W %' (`wAll`, naye item par bhi khud). Table `posFit()` se screen mein fit (apne andar scroll) — PC purchase mein table ke upar / neeche naya dabba joro to band `<details>` ya ek patli line, warna Save neeche chala jata.
- **🖥 PC Purchase = POS jaisi (v2.247.0, rules 2.103)** (malik 13 Nov: 'PC par purchase screen hamare POS software jaisi — same fonts, same rang, purani screen nahi'): `posMode()` (>=1100px) = hamesha table + `.pp-posh` Basic Information + POS tarteeb ke khane + `.pp-posr` CSS (Tahoma, halka firozi, chokor). PC par Purchase mein gol chips / rangeen card wapas mat lao; naya khana = `C` object + dono `ord` list. Phone alag (nahi badla). **📲 WhatsApp 'ai' tasveer**: Business number par bill ki photo + `ai` -> PC `purAiPut` (purAi + purAiImg) -> app `wai` -> WAHI `aiRead`. Purchase ki tasveer ka naya raasta isi se; PC par AI mat chalao (app mein hai). ➕ Naya item = `stockNewItemOpen`.
- **⚡ Apne number ke jawab tez (wa-out v1.15, 13 Nov)**: malik / Mubeen / jure number (`isFast` = `KHW.who`) ke paigham line mein aage, gahak wala faasla (`cfg.gap`) nahi; gahak ka faasla KABHI kam mat karo (ban). Naya 'staff ko jawab' = in hi numbers par, khud tez ho jata hai.
- **🦒 Gardan ki yaad + Claude 11 baje (md pkg v34, 13 Nov)**: `cb.py` — Claude subah 11 se khulti hai; Claude chalte hue har 10 min 2 s patti (malik ne khud maangi — hatao mat, aur naye paigham bina kahe mat joro).
- **👤 Number reply se — har jagah (wa-parchi v2.31 / wa-out v1.20, 14 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: reply mein number diya, phir bhi 'gahak? (number nahi)'): parchi ke baare mein malik / Mubeen ko jane wala HAR waOut paigham `pp: t.id` ke saath (naya paigham banao to bhi) — us par reply mein number = `gahakSet`; 'Print auto 0344…' reply bhi. Malik aksar Business phone se hi jawab likhta hai: wa-out `ownNum` (fromMe + REPLY + chhota + mobile number) -> waIn malik ke naam se; bot ke apne paigham (id `3EB0…` / `ownSent`) kabhi nahi, number na ho to paigham chhua bhi nahi. 'Is paigham par reply karein' likho to jaancho ke woh paigham `pp` rakhta hai aur Business phone se reply bhi chale. BILL_HOLD sirf yaad-dasht (restart = bill photo dobara).
- **👤 Number baad mein (wa-parchi v2.27, 13 Nov)** (malik: 'number nahi mila ke reply mein number likhein to reh gaya paigham customer ko jaye'): parchi / '✕ number nahi mila' / bill photo par reply mein number = `gahakSet` → ruka bill `billSend` (BILL_HOLD). Gahak ko jo bhi paigham number na hone se ruke, us ka tareeqa yehi — tasveer rakho + reply se number; jawab mein number dohrao (ghalat number par bill na jaye). Bill ja chuka ho to number mat badlo.
- **🖨 Business Print par gahak (wa-parchi v2.23, 13 Nov)**: Business number se chhapi parchi ki patti = caption / alag 'Print naam number' paigham ka GAHAK — bhejne wale (malik / Mubeen) ka naam nahi. Naya print raasta banao to caption ka naam `gahakHd(wid, cap)` se zaroor.
- **📥 Business number = markaz (12 Nov, wa-out v1.8 · wa-parchi v2.20)** (malik: 'business wala WhatsApp hi main — parchi isi par bhejein, wohi print kare, baqi bhi wohi · mera / Mubeen ka link ho ya na ho · Print auto = sab auto, Print = sirf print'): wa-out `inboxPut` (sirf ijazat wale number: `kh-wa who()`) → `waIn/<id>` (+ `_p<i>` tasveer) → wa-parchi `inOne` → `bizMsg` / `bizDo` → WAHI printOne / trTrack / trReply (m._buf, `dlBuf`); jawab `bz-*`; Mubeen ko parchi ki tasveer `nw-` {im, pp} — us par reply = start / bill. Parchi ka naya kaam ab isi raaste se socho (malik / Mubeen ke link par bharosa nahi); gahak ka paigham parhna ab bhi nahi.
