- **🔢 Kharcha WhatsApp se (v2.241.0, PC kh-wa v1.0 · wa-parchi v2.19 · wa-out v1.7)** (malik 12 Nov: 'har kharcha account ko number 001 002 · apni chat mein "001 Imran ne paisa liya 4900" = Daily Sale + kharcha account · sirf akhrajat · mazeed number add (Sonu, Bilal) · meri / Mubeen ki chat ya business WhatsApp · hadd nahi'): `pc/kh-wa.js` EK jagah (parse / ijazat / entry `wk<msgid>` / cancel / jawab waOut) — wa-parchi `khTry` + wa-out `onMsgs` sirf bulate hain; numbers `blueAccess/khNum` (app `khNumAssign`, kabhi nahi badalte, party doc mein nahi), bhejne wale `blueAccess/khWa`; app `kharcha-wa.js`. Naya 'WhatsApp se entry' kaam isi file se — wasooli / doosre khate bina malik ke OK nahi. wa-out mein location + yehi do cheezein parhi jati hain, teesri nahi.
- **🏷 Parchi: party ka naam SAB SE UPAR (pos-khata-sync u, 13 Nov)** (malik: 'party name upar aaye Urdu English mein — wasooli, kharcha, payment di / li, transfer'): voucher parchi mein NOOR TRADERS ke foran neeche naam (English mota + Urdu), beech mein dobara nahi. Naya voucher / parchi layout banao to naam upar. Bank parchi alag (nahi badli).
- **⚖️ Stock theek = 'stock fraq' account (post-farq v19, v2.245.0)**: stock theek ka bill HAMESHA stock fraq party ka Sale / Return (ginti farq jaisa) — counter sale / Adjustment kabhi nahi; Sale ke baad PartyID wapas parh kar jaancho. POS mein likhne wala naya bill raasta = banne ke baad usi transaction mein account jaancho.
- **🔔 WhatsApp reminder (v2.246.0, PC kh-wa v1.5)** (malik 13 Nov: 'reminder WhatsApp par · roz jab tak off na karo · all reminder show · 3 off · sirf mujhe · Mubeen ka apna'): `remCmd` + `blueAccess/waRem-<number>`, roz 1, 3, 5 baje (`REM_HOURS`, malik ne chuna — v1.6) `remTick` (sirf wa-out). Reminder SIRF lagane wale ko jata hai — likhai mein kisi aur ka number ho tab bhi us ko KABHI nahi (gahak ko roz ka paigham = number ban). App ki Reminder screen alag hai; jorna malik ke kehne par.
- **📝 WhatsApp entry ka note (kh-wa v1.4, 13 Nov)**: PC / WhatsApp se bani har entry ke note (Tafseel / narration) mein KHATE KA NAAM pehle — sirf 'WhatsApp Malik' kabhi nahi. Sabaq: malik 'print' kahe aur kai print hon (A4 parchi / entry parchi / khata / Daily) to pehle number wale sawal se poochho — 13 Nov ko andaze se ghalat print theek kiya.
- **📲 WhatsApp: pehle naam, phir kaam (v2.244.0, PC kh-wa v1.3)** (malik 12 Nov: 'pehle naam -> suggestion -> phir 1-2-3 · PDF + print + baqaya · cancel sirf malik · "reports" = report menu'): `khPend` stages n -> m -> t / r; naam wala raasta SIRF Business chat (`biz`), na mile = chup. WhatsApp ka naya kaam = menu (stg m) mein naya number; khPend hamesha `pendSet` (update, merge nahi). PDF = `pdfMake` (apna, ASCII) + waOut `doc`. Cancel / reports sirf malik — larkon ko mat kholo.
- **💵 WhatsApp se payment li / di / transfer (v2.243.0, PC kh-wa v1.2)** (malik 12 Nov: '1 li · 2 di · 3 transfer · naam ki list wapas, number chunein · "no" = Daily mein nahi · sirf parties · sab jure number'): `parse2` -> `ask` (smartFind) -> `blueAccess/khPend` -> `pick` -> entry. Bina number ke jawab KABHI entry nahi (naam ek mile tab bhi). WhatsApp ka naya paise wala hukum isi sawal-jawab namoone se; bank khate bina malik ke OK nahi.
- **🔢 Kharcha list bhejo (v2.242.0, PC wa-out v1.9)** (malik 12 Nov: 'save numbers par list · mera / Mubeen ka chip'): app se Business number ke zariye paigham = `blueAccess/<hukum doc>` (malik likhe) -> wa-out waOut banaye -> nateeja blueAccess mein wapas (`khWaSend` / `khWaSent`). App waOut seedha nahi likh / parh sakti (rules) — naya 'app se WhatsApp bhejo' kaam isi namoone se, waOut ka rule mat kholo.
- **📊 Reports malik ke WhatsApp par (v2.239.0, PC raat-report v1.5)** (malik 11 Nov: 'daily sale ka hisaab · larka-wise · dukan ka profit apne number par · waqt khud tay, default 6 pm · click se bhi · WhatsApp parchi screen par'): `wa-report.js` (⋮, sirf malik) → `reportConfig` waOn / waD / waL / waP / reportTime / reportNow → PC `runReport` → `waSend` (waOut `rp-*`, dukan ka Business number) + `blueAccess/reportWa`. Malik ko naya 'report bhejo' kaam isi raaste (raat-report `waTexts` mein naya hissa) — naya bhejne wala socket / service nahi. PDF abhi nahi.
- **🩺 Parchi ka safar (v2.233.0, PC wa-parchi v2.18)**: WhatsApp print / Printauto ki har shikayat = pehle ⋮ → 🩺 Parchi ka safar (`wa-safar.js`): rabte 🟢/🔴, `parchiTrack.dl` (PC ne kya dekha, aakhri 40), har parchi ke qadam. WhatsApp ka naya qadam / wajah banao to `trDbg` / `ringAdd` se yahan dikhao — khamosh nahi. Malik 'print nahi aaya' kahe to pehle poochho KAUN sa print (HBL parchi / WhatsApp Print auto / bill).
- **↩ Minus wali items (v2.263.0, PC pos-sales-dekho v2.11)** (malik 13 Nov: 'minus wali items kahan, kaun se bill mein'): POS report ▾ ke andar `posMinusHTML` — `sum.rtd[].it` (wapsi ki items) + `sum.mn` (sale bill ke andar minus lines). SIRF dikhana; wapsi / minus ka naya dikhana isi data se, auto sale ka hisaab is se mat badlo (malik pehle dekhega phir faisla).
- **🖥 POS report line (v2.232.0, PC pos-sales-dekho v2.10)**: Sale khane ke auto dabbe mein `posRepHTML` — POS Sale report ka kul (`sum.g` = cash `gc` + udhaar `gu`) → − udhaar ka baqi `un` − cash wapsi = galle ki auto sale. SIRF dikhana; Daily ki sale kabhi POS report ka kul mat banao (udhaar = galla jhoota KAM). sum ki nayi raqam RUPAY mein (amt paisa).
- **🪶 Entry halki (v2.181.2)** (malik: "wohi paigham aa kar atak jata — mulazim ke mobile par load"): dohri entry ka native sawal SIRF jab wohi entry 2 min ke andar bani ho; isi phone ki 10 s ke andar = double tap, chup-chaap rok (sawal nahi). Daily khanon ka account `<select>` lazy — poori list sirf chhoone par (`fillAll`); naye form / list mein bhi har render par saare khaton ki option mat banao.
- **🧾 WhatsApp parchi thermal = JAISI LIKHI (wa-parchi v1.3–1.4)** (malik: "lafz same waise hi · shuru mein tick ka box · simple" · "same as it parchi · jis ki tarteeb aage peeche lage us ka woh hissa bhi"): AI raw = HOOBAHOO, jori-war; shak wali line ke neeche likhai ka tukda; thermal par sirf gahak ke lafz + ☐, tasveer / rack-war list nahi (A4 par hai). Isay wapas lambi list / tasveer mat banao bina poochhe.
- **🖥 PC update = ~3 MINUTE mein, raat ko NAHI** (2026-11-04 ghalti: malik ko baar baar "PC raat ko khud update hoga" kaha — asal mein doctor har 3 min GitHub manifest dekhta hai, naya version FORAN lagata hai): push ke baad kaho "PC on hai to 3-5 min mein khud lag jayega" · dekhna = Settings → 🖥️ PC scripts → version. Khatarnak PC badlaav raat ko chahiye to push hi raat ko karo.
- **🧩 nt-print.js mein naya `require('./x.js')` = `nt-print-setup.ps1` + `nt-fix.ps1` ki file list mein bhi** (9 Nov 2026: naya counter RN6QU6D par setup ke baad MODULE_NOT_FOUND urdu-names.js — list v1.20 se purani thi).
- **↵ .ps1 / .bat = CRLF** (2026-11-04 ghalti: python ne wa-a4.ps1 ko LF kar diya — poori file badli dikhi, pakra gaya): Windows files ko edit ke baad CRLF hi rakho (bytes mein \r\n), manifest hash usi ke baad.
- **🔁 WhatsApp parchi Dobara parho (v2.179.2)**: {st:'new'} = PC phir parhe + naya print (thermal + A4). Bill bani / maal nikla par kabhi nahi.
- **🗑 WhatsApp parchi hatao (v2.179.1)** (malik: "parchi katt bhi sake"): hatana = `st: 'x:<pehle>'` (kabhi delete nahi), ↩ Wapas + ⋮ ♻️ Hatai hui. Parchi ki koi nayi list / ginti / PC kaam = `hid()` wali chhod kar; PC `upd` chhupi ko chhupa rakhe.
- **🤖 AI model band (v2.179.1)**: Google purane model band karta hai (2.5-flash: 2 Nov 2026, 404 "no longer available"). Model ka naam kabhi pakka fallback na ho — 404 par Google ki LIST se agla, chal jaye to aiConfig mein (malik). 2.5 par wapas le jane wala koi kaam kabhi nahi.
- **🧾 Sale chips ki qatar (v2.179.3)**: `.sale-copies` (Counter / Printer / Gate pass / Bill print) WRAP + chip nowrap "…" — dukan PC ke printer zyada hon to bhi page chaura nahi (chaura page = phone poori screen chhoti kar deta hai). Sale mein item chunne par 1-2-3 (qtyPad) khirki NAHI (malik).
- **🔎 Item wale bills (v2.146.0)**: Bills ⋮ ki ALAG screen (Bills screen mat badlo) — malik ki chuni items (sam-ib-items) wale bills, chip ke upar item naam × tadad. Lines = posSaleLines (PC pehle bill, phir lines likhta hai — naye bill ko jaldi dobara parho). Naya "items se bills" kaam isi screen mein.
- **📄 Chipki bill tasveer (v2.146.0)**: POS Purchase ki upar wali tasveer par "▲ Chhupao" = patli patti (items upar), ▼ Dikhao = wapas. Naye chipke hisse mein bhi chhupane ka raasta do.
- **📄 Daily Sale PDF (v2.136.0)**: upar chips (Sale · Kal ka change · Aaj ka change · Closing cash · Farq — `dailyPdfChips`), table sirf Wasooli · Akhrajat · Sirf Khata (pehle jaisi). Change / Closing ke khane table mein wapas mat lao.
- **📒 Daily khane ki entry chhoti (v2.176.0)** (malik: "3 se zyada nazar nahi aate — 10 screen par"): Sale / Wasooli / Akhrajat khane ki har entry = `dyEntryHTML` 2 patli lines (Rs · naam / 🕒 · 🖨 haalat · 📷 · 🔔 · note), tap = chips (`.dy-ex`). Khane mein naya button / chip = `.dy-ex` ke andar (band haalat mein KABHI nahi); print fail ki ⚠️ chhoti line par zaroor.
- **📒 Daily Sale neeche ke khane (v2.127.1)**: upar ki patti purani (mat chhero); neeche Kal ka change + Aaj ka change + Closing cash = EK khana `cashColHTML` (data-col cash, teen hisse); **v2.171.0 (malik: "three dot se nikal kar")**: Auto-sale dabba (`.dy-auto`) + Sale ka haath wala form (`.dy-saleform`) Sale khane mein SEEDHA (⋮ mein NAHI — wapas ⋮ mat daalo), phone par Sale khana pehla (4 khane side scroll); HBL khana Daily se hata (malik: "zaroorat nahi"). Malik "khana" = neeche ka column, "patti" = upar — pehle poochh lo.
- **📦 Packet report (v2.132.0, v2.133.1 har branch)**: PC `pk-report.js` (sirf parhti, har ghante / pkReq) → `posStats/pkReport` (meta.branches) + `pkReport-<branchId>-<k>` (JSON text); app default NOOR TRADERS (naam se) + "Godam 2 ke items" filter (item.st stock ya Godam 2 bikri), branch chips ⋮, Aaj/Kal/tareekh = roz ke bucket aakhri 14 din (m["YYYY-MM-DD"]); app `pk-report.js` khirki (Transfer tile, CAP stock.pkrep OPT_IN). Packet ki ginti WAHI qaida (tadad == packet size), scan column mile to exact. Naya packet/barcode report isi data se — nayi SQL query mat banao.
- **🛒 Purchase list (v2.130.0)**: dashboard / filter patti NAHI (wapas mat lao); har bill = naam + floating chips `.pu-k` (Raqam·POS·Haalat·Tareekh, dy-c jaisa) + tap se khula (`puOpen`); POS bill ka ✏️ = `openKhataBill(id,'@pu')` → save par `ntPpSaved` wapas list. Naya chip card ke `.pu-x` mein.
- **🧾 POS Purchase upar (v2.129.0)**: naya bill = sirf patli patti `pp-hbar-need` (Supplier · Godam ▾), form sirf tap par (`hdOpen`/`supOpen`), chunte hi band; Bill# / Carton ⋮; total sirf items par. Larke ki entry ka card: `ppAppStatusOf` status chip + `ppWatchToday`. "POS bill banao" = larke ki tasveer `billPics` pin.
- **🧾 Purchase (v2.128.0, rules 2.76)**: ✅ check list se bill = seedha POS Purchase EDIT (openKhataBill) + "✓ Check + 📌 Post · agla ›" (wahi `bcGo`; ‹ › list `window.ntBcNav`; ← / aakhri = check list `ntBcBack`). 📷 scan search ke andar (`scanInSearch`), mic nahi, footer `.pp-small`. Larke ki entry par `via` gaari/supply (form chips, supplier se khud yaad `viaOfParty`); card "🧾 POS bill banao" = `ppOpenFrom` (📎 fromEntry → save mein via+larka, poochna nahi; PC purchase-post v4.5 gaari par bhi jorta). Naya purchase-link kaam isi `larka` raaste se — alag link mat banao.
- **📒 Khata list saaf (v2.127.3)**: list ke upar "👥 Sab accounts · 📅 · ↕" patti aur "Daily Closing Cash Khata" qatar NAHI (malik: khate zyada upar) — wapas mat lao; filter ⋮ se, Closing Cash sidebar se.
- **📒 Khate ki entries chhoti (v2.126.0)**: ledger card band = raqam · label · tareekh · baqaya · chhoti line (🖥 POS no / note) ▾; sab chips `.li-x` mein, tap = `liOpen` + render. Entry card mein naya chip/button `.li-x` ke andar hi lagao (band haalat mein sirf ek line).
- **🔍 Farq ki entries (v2.125.0, rules 2.75)**: PC har 15 min har jude khate ki POS lines `posLines/<appId> {j: JSON [[date, code, docType, docNo, dr, cr, desc, st]], pre, n, at}` (sirf badle, cancel st 3 nahi) — app `pmDiff`: posCode (CPV/CRV/JV) → PO docNo (pos-<id>) → raqam+tareekh ±3 din; "Sabqa baqaya" = `pre`. Malik: "click par foran, sab khaton ka auto" — darkhwast wala tareeqa nahi (pehle se likha hua, 1 read).
- **⚖️ Barabar karo (v2.133.0, rules 2.77)**: Farq screen chips → `milanJobs` {partyId, dir app|pos, ids} (malik) → PC pos-khata-sync `runMilanJobs` (dir pos = purani / borrow entries bhi POS voucher, Saman liya = JV purchase account; dir app = `importVoucher(v, force)`). Purchase bill kabhi nahi (sync.js); transfer v2.134.0 se haan (milanTransfer). Naya "POS mein voucher banao" kaam isi job raaste se — alag writer mat banao.
- **📦 Saman liya → POS (v2.133.7)**: khate ka app wala "Saman liya" (borrow) 27 Oct se khud POS JV (purchase account Dr / party Cr); purana sirf "📤 Sab khaton ki baqi entries" / milanJobs (pehle taza data, phir list ☑). Bank/loan khata, purchase bill, larke ki entry, transfer kabhi is raaste nahi. v2.133.8: tez raasta (quickEntries) bhi — seconds mein voucher; card par ⏳ / ✕ status.
- **🤖 Auto milan (v2.135.0)**: PC `autoMilan` har 15 min sirf PAKKA farq khud theek karta hai — sirf ek taraf ki entries (POS-only saaf voucher → app, ya app-only → POS). Opening ab KABHI khud nahi (v2.147.3). Dono taraf entries / raqam farq / bill / nayi entry = KABHI auto nahi (malik ke chips). POS ki opening lines app mein kabhi import nahi (opening ke khane mein). Naya auto-theek kaam isi function mein, isi hifazat ke saath.
- **✕ Entry-war POS mein nahi (v2.134.0)**: khate ke card par farq wali entry = "✕ POS mein nahi" + malik ka "📤 POS mein bhejo" (pos-milan `pmEntryHTML`, EK entry milanJob). Transfer bhi ab job se (PC `milanTransfer` — POS mein pehle se ho to sirf jor). PC har milan entry pehle marker + `posTwin` dekhe (dohra voucher kabhi nahi). Naya "entry POS mein bhejo" kaam isi chip / job se.
- **⚖️ Ek voucher se POS barabar (v2.235.0, PC pos-khata-sync t)** (malik 11 Nov: 'ek hi voucher daalein aur app jaisa baqaya POS mein aa jaye · koi bhi khata ho'): Farq screen `bfixHTML` → milanJobs ids `['@balance','dry']` (🔎 jaanch) → `['@balance']` (EK JV khata vs Equity; transfers pehle apne voucher se; baqi app-only entries `posNo` + `balFix`) → ↩ `['@undo-balance', job]`. Sirf malik ke chip se, kabhi khud nahi. Naya 'baqaya barabar' kaam isi job se.
- **↩ Barabar wala kaam wapas (v2.293.0, PC pos-khata-sync w, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'jo voucher abhi bane — POS se bhi aur app se bhi delete'): Farq screen ke natije par '↩ Is kaam ko wapas' -> milanJobs ['@undo-job', job] -> SIRF us job ki bani cheezein (POS voucher + app entry 🗑). Har naye milan raaste mein bani cheezon ki list job mein (`fix.mk` + `mkJob`) zaroor likho. Bani hui entry malik se haath se delete mat karwao (PC asal POS voucher bhi mita deta hai) — yehi chip.
- **⚖️ Barabar se bani entries (v2.296.0, PC pos-khata-sync w3)**: milan se bani entries ki TAREEKH purani hoti hai (aaj bani, 16 Sep ki) — 'aaj ki entries' maange to tareekh ke ilawa 'kab bani' (job.at) bhi socho. Wapas = `['@undo-job', job, ...codes]` (sirf tick wale) — job par `undone` list. PC ka 🤖 autoMilan bhi milanJobs likhta hai (dir 'auto', dono taraf ek job mein) — milan jobs ki koi nayi list / wapsi banao to 'auto' bhi gino (16 Nov ghalti: amjad ke voucher nazar nahi aaye). Sticky patti ka peecha transparent ho to list ke upar chadhti hai — sticky tabhi jab pakka rang ho.
- **🗑 Din ki entries hatao (v2.295.0, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'aaj / tareekh ki entries delete, POS mein bhi · default sab un-tick'): khata ⋮ `ddOpen` (sirf malik) — tick ke baghair kuch nahi hatta (default ☐ — kabhi khud ☑ mat karo); app se delete = `save({...e,deleted:true})` (PC POS voucher khud hata deta), transfer = `commitAccountTransfer` remove. Entry hatane ka naya raasta isi se — POS purchase bill / HBL jora / posted yahan se nahi.
- **⏱ POS baqaya har second (v2.169.0, PC pos-khata-sync l)** (malik: "har second check · auto" · farq khud barabar NAHI — "hum khud dekh lenge"): PC `ledgerWatch` har 1 s chhota sawal (Voucher ginti / max id / UpdatedOn / status + VoucherDetail ginti) → badla = `balPush` (sirf baqaye, 3 s mein ek dafa se zyada nahi) `blueAccess/posLedger`. Lines (posLines) pehle jaise 15 min / Taza. App `pmRow` state `wait` = khate ki entry POS data se nayi (3 min tak) → "🖥 POS ⏳" (jhoota ⚠ nahi). Naya auto-barabar KABHI nahi bina malik.
- **📄 Khata PDF + 🖨 dukan printer (v2.170.0, rules 2.82, PC pos-khata-sync m)** (malik: "khata ki PDF stuck · jaldi show · print bhi apne printer par ek click · ek side wasooli ek side paisa" · printer = THERMAL dukan PC): khata PDF = DO SIDE table `kh-two` (Tareekh · ledgerTitles khana · Note | wahi daayein, aakhir Kul) — pdf-export `textPdf` async (40 qatar par saans, "safha N · %"), td rang, Urdu = canvas tasveer (`urImg`), `wa()` sirf WinAnsi (→ = ->), chhote khane (avg ≤12) kabhi na tootein, tasveerein aakhir mein seedhi. Khali `.report-photo-gallery` canvas raasta NAHI kholti (pehle har khata PDF html2canvas = atakti thi). Sheet mein 🖨 Print (CAP kh.tprint) → `khataPrints/<id> {j JSON}` → PC `khataSlip` Font B 27|27 + status (45 s jawab nahi = ⚠️). 60+ entries = meter ka confirm. **Raqam PAISA (2 Nov m2)**: khataTpData paisa bhejta hai, khataSlip ÷100 — app se PC ko jo bhi raqam jaye PAISA mein, PC par ÷100 (pehli parchi ×100 chhapi thi).
- **🔄 POS milan taza (v2.133.4)**: farq dikhe to pehle POS data ka waqt dekho ("🖥 POS data: X pehle") — purana data = jhoota farq (sehna 27 Oct). PC once(): syncEntries ki ghalti ledgerPush ko kabhi na roke; milan job / `blueAccess/posLedgerReq` = foran ledger + lines. Malik ko "farq hai" kehne se pehle POS GL report se milao.
- **⚖️ POS milan (v2.124.0, rules 2.74)**: PC pos-khata-sync har 15 min `blueAccess/posLedger {m:{appPartyId: paisa (lena +, dena −)}, at}` (sirf jude khate, sirf badle to) — app pos-milan.js: khate ke upar `pmLine` (✓ barabar / ⚠ farq / POS mein nahi), Khata ⋮ `⚖️ POS milan` screen (Farq / Theek / POS mein nahi / Sab). Rs 1 tak farq = theek. CAP kh.posmilan (malik + full). Naya ledger data isi doc mein (per-party docs nahi — reads/writes).
- **🔒 Khata band = POS bhi (v2.123.0)**: khata band/chalu = `party.active` (acctBand, ⋮ ac.band) → PC pos-khata-sync `link.act` tabdeeli par POS `Account.IsActive`. Pehli dafa sirf yaad — POS ke haath se band accounts kabhi khud chalu mat karo. Naya "band" flag hash mein mat daalo (sab parties ka faltu POS update).
- **🟡 Purchase list: check = peela, check + post = aadha peela aadha laal (v2.308.0, 17 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot): `puCard` class `chk` / `chkp` + chip `.pu-k.chk`, badlaav par `puChkPaint` (list dobara nahi). Ye rang malik ka chuna hai — check / post ka naya nishan kisi aur POS-bill list mein chahiye to yehi classes; rang bina kahe mat badlo.
- **🧠 NT-PRINT ka POS watcher = memory chowkidar (nt-posui v1.15, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'memory konsi apps kha rahi hai' — PC par nt-posui.ps1 akela 4.5 GB, 9:19 se, ~800 MB / ghanta; managed UI Automation (System.Windows.Automation FindAll) memory nahi chhorta): nt-posui.js har 2 min `tasklist /FI PID` (halka, spooler / POS nahi chhoota) se watcher ki memory; `posUiMem` (default 600 MB) se upar = `memKill` → band + 2 s mein naya (30 s wala intezar / fails ginti nahi). Koi bhi hamesha chalne wala PowerShell / C# watcher banao to us ki memory ki had + khud naya chalana saath banao. 'PC dheera / memory' ki shikayat = pehle EK read-only line (Get-Process group by naam, MB) aur phir PowerShell ke har hisse ka `.ps1` + `Kis_ne` (parent ka `.js`) — command line poori kabhi nahi (password).
- **🖨 WhatsApp A4 = ASAL nateeja + spooler se kam sawal (wa-parchi v2.37 / a4-print v1.7, 17 Nov)** (malik: '"Parchi A4 ko gayi" likha, abhi tak print nahi hua · taiz treen kar do · dekh lena kisi script se printer spooler ka issue na aaye jaise kal aaya tha'): WhatsApp ka jawab ab pehle '⏳ A4 printer ko bheji', phir `a4Tell` (a4Jobs ka haal) = '✅ Chhap gayi (printer · N s)' / '✕ Nahi chhapi — wajah'; 90 s na chhape = ek dafa '⏳ abhi nahi chhapi — wajah' (`a4Why` = blueAccess/a4Pc: script chup / printer nahi mila / kaghaz). 'gayi' = sirf line mein laga, chhapi NAHI — printer wala naya raasta banao to jawab asal chhapne ke baad do. a4-print: aakhri chalne wala IPP printer `a4-last.json` mein yaad + app ka `cfg.prIp` bhi talaash mein; printer na mile = har 30 s SIRF yaad wale IP par IPP sawal (`quick`, network — Windows nahi); poori talaash (Windows `Get-Printer` samet) 2 min mein ek dafa (pehle A4 baqi ho to HAR 20 s spooler se poochhti thi). QAIDA: spooler ko chhoone wala kaam (Get-Printer / Add-Printer / Windows print / RAW) kabhi har kuch second ke loop mein mat daalo — network (IPP) se kaam ho to wohi pehle.
- **👤 POS Purchase phone: Supplier = chip + neeche list · godam item ke andar · ‹ › = BILL NUMBER (v2.310.0, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'search upar · godam wala chip hatao · supplier chuno = chip, dabao to chuno + search mein naam · Edit chhodo ki zaroorat nahi · 10 number bill khula ho to agla 11, pichhla 09'): phone (posMode nahi) par upar ka bara form (supplierBox / Godam (sab items) / ✏️ EDIT dabba) KABHI nahi khulta (`hdShow = pos && …`) — upar sirf `.pp-hrow` (`.pp-supchip` + W %), chip = `supOpen` -> `.pp-supdrop` (WAHI #ppSupQ + supList, ⭐ Aksar pehle se, Enter = pehla, bahar tap = band). Godam: naya item = pichhle item ka (`addItem`), kisi item ka godam chuno = khali godam wale baqi items bhi wahi; save par laal `.pp-gsel` tak scroll. ‹ › / ☐ check / Check + Post ke baad agla = `numNbr(d)` (pcBillsOf, billNo ki tarteeb; `navCur` = khule / abhi bheje bill ka number, save ke baad bhi rehta) — cancel + POSTED dono khud chhoot-te (v2.379.0 — posted par sawal NAHI), mulazim sirf aaj ke khule; `navGo` purana bill tab tak rakhta hai jab tak naya poora na khule (`navBusy` patli line, ppLoadBill `force`). PC (posMode) ka upar ka form / supplierBox / EDIT dabba waise hi. Phone par upar ka naya hissa = isi 2 line patti mein; bara form / godam khana wapas mat lao.
- **🏦 HBL account = sirf HBL screen se (v2.311.0, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'payment aayi 6303 wale account mein, lagi loan wale mein · Hbl Noor Traders Purana mein · bank ke chips seedha saamne'): wajah — Reminder ke 'HBL se payment' form ka 'badlein' (notes.js `pickBank`) jo khata chunta, wohi poori app ka HBL account (`bankSms/_alias @hbl`, event `sam-bank-pick` -> `saveHbl`) ban jata (sab phones + PC bank-parchi) -> Raast (bina number) / 6303 ke AAYE paise loan khate mein. Ab `pickBank` sirf usi payment ka; `@hbl` sirf HBL screen ✏️ / ⋮ 🔢 se. Ek dafa `@fix1117` (malik ke phone, sirf jab naam saaf ek-ek milein): @hbl + @a:6303 = 'Hbl Noor Traders (Purana)', @a:7999 = 'Hbl loan 7999' (+ @pcin:7999 — pehle loan HBL account tha, PC khud lagata tha). ⋮ 🔢 'Kaun sa account kis khate mein' (`amRows`: @hbl · @a:* · @c:*, ✏️ = bankChoices). Ghalat khate ki AAYE entry (bk<doc>a, 7 din, sirf malik) par ⇄ chip (`mvTarget` / `moveTo` -> `O.repost` WAHI ID — PC pos-khata-sync usi CPV ko naye khate par likhta, naya voucher / parchi nahi; Daily wahi). Bank chips `.bkp-accts` wrap (side-scroll nahi). QAIDA: kisi ek form / ek payment ka chunao kabhi poori app ki setting (sab phones / Server PC) chup-chaap na badle — global setting sirf apni saaf jagah se, aur badalte waqt saaf likho 'sab phones + PC par'.
- **🏦 Bank account = EK chhota chip (v2.312.0, 17 Nov)** (malik ne POS Purchase ke supplier chip ki tasveer dikhai: 'bank ka naam aisa chip — us par neeche list aaye · chhota, zyada jagah na le'): HBL screen ke upar saare accounts ki chips ki qatar NAHI — `acctChips` = ek `.bkp-accchip` (🏦 gol + chuna account + doosre accounts ke baqi ki narangi ginti + ▾), dabao (`data-bk-accdd`, `accOpen`) = neeche `.bkp-accdrop` (har account 36px patli line, chuna = neela + ✓, WAHI `data-bk-cur`), bahar tap = band (capture + composedPath, list dobara paint nahi). Kai cheezon mein se ek chunna ho to yehi namoona: ek chip + neeche list (supplier chip / W% jaisa) — chips ki qatar nahi.
- **🖥 Counter ka naam = PC ka APNA naam (v2.316.0, PC pc-names v1.1, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'RN6QU6D wala aur Abdurehman wala system ek hi hai — Abdurehman ki bajaye PC ka naam, NT-PRINT se'): naam = NT-PRINT setting ka naam (host se alag ho to) warna host DESKTOP- hata kar — insaanon wale naam (Mithu / Abdurehman / Bilal) kahin naya mat likho; purane naam sirf `LEGACY` (channel dhoondna, `PN.chFind`). App tally.js `disp()` = pc-names ka qaida HUBAHU. Ek PC ke do naam (POS ka 'at PC:' ≠ NT-PRINT host) = malik ⚙️ Camera setup ki ⚪ qatar par 🔗 (blueAccess/pcAlias) — andaze se khud mat jodo. Camera channel ki naya kaam = `PN.chFind(CF.ch, naam)` (seedha `CF.ch[naam]` nahi). **🖨 NT-PRINT dabba** PC scripts mein server ke neeche (`#pcNt`); khirki khuli = `blueAccess/ntWatch` -> NT-PRINT v1.33 har 20 s (warna 60 s) — har-20-s likhai sirf jab koi dekh raha ho (Firestore likhai). **🎥 NVR tak rasta (bill-clip v2.7)**: Windows ffmpeg 'Error number -138' = waqt khatam; rasta na ho to har bill par koshish nahi (NETBAD, 5 min baad TCP sawal) aur koshish nahi gini — camera / network wala naya kaam bhi isi tarah: rasta band = ruko + wajah app ko (billClipPc.net), setting khud mat badlo. **`?pc=old`** ab sirf usi session (sessionStorage).
- **🗂 Haal hi mein = recents (v2.315.0, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'recents tab jaisa · baar baar search na karna pare · kisi bhi screen / khata par hon to direct usi jagah se open'): header ka 🗂 (shell.js `rtBtnSetup`, CAP `app.recent`) -> recent.js `rtDlg` (aakhri 12 card: 📱 screen · 📒 khata · 🪟 khirki) -> WAHI jagah + scroll; 2 dafa = pichhli screen / khata. Khirki khud yaad hoti hai agar `main` ke `[data-mn]` chip ya ☰ `…Nav` button se khule (nayi `dialog[open]`) — nayi khirki banao to chip par `data-mn` + sahi `data-mn-t` lagao, khud aa jayegi; khirki apni haalat ke saath kholni ho to `recentPush({key, icon, label, open})` + app.js `ntRecentOpen` mein kind (stock screens jaisa). Nayi upar wali `<dialog>` banao to us ka close hook app.js popstate fehrist + recent.js `UP_CLOSE` dono mein. Khate ab is list mein (malik) — purani #recentBar chips patti ab nahi banti.
- **⚖️ Ek voucher = SIRF EK JV + ✕ Rok do (v2.314.0, rules 2.105, PC pos-khata-sync w6, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'pos main aik hi voucher bana kar pos ko apps jaisa — SB entries nahi'): @balance ab transfer bhi ALAG voucher se nahi bhejta (pehle milanSendOne) — POS mein sirf EK JV (khata ↔ Equity), app mein kuch nahi banta; har app-only entry (transfer ki dono taraf bhi) posNo + balFix. Malik 'ek voucher' kahe to koi bhi pehla / beech ka qadam jo POS mein ALAG voucher banaye wo bhi na ho. Milan job jo PC ne abhi shuru nahi kiya (status new) = '✕ Rok do' (status 'stop', sirf malik) — PC ko bheja koi bhi naya job-type banao to rokne ka yehi raasta socho. **Tooling**: notes / manifest ki python script hamesha NAYE naam (`/tmp/notes-<version>.py`) se Write tool se — purani /tmp script dobara chal sakti hai (17 Nov ghalti).
- **🚫 POS mein na bhejo + milan = TOTAL (v2.313.0, PC pos-khata-sync w5, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'nayi entry ko POS mein na bhejna ho to upar chhota check box' · 'entries milane ke bajaye total raqam barabar ho to theek'): khate ke naye entry form (entryForm: Wasooli / Saman liya / Saman diya · unifiedExpenseForm: Payment di) ke UPAR `posXHTML` ☐ '🚫 POS mein na bhejo' (sirf NAYI entry, malik + CAP `kh.posno` OPT_IN) -> `posXOf` = {posNo: true, posX: 1}. posNo = PC voucher / parchi nahi (pos-khata-sync pehle se); posX = jaan bujh kar POS se bahar: app `pxOf` (pos-milan pmRow farq = app − posX − POS, pmDiff mein nahi) + PC `milanDiffPc` mein nahi (⚖️ @balance / 🤖 autoMilan us ki raqam POS mein KABHI na daalein). Purani entry par ☐ nahi (POS mein ja chuki ho to voucher nahi hatta) — sirf nishan; khate mein `.kt-px` '🚫 POS nahi'. Milan pehle se TOTAL baqaye par hai (Rs 1 tak = ✓, entry-war ✕ sirf jab total farq) — wahi rakho. posNo akela (bank kharcha / balFix / sabqa) = POS mein kisi aur tareeqe se shamil, us ko milan se KABHI mat nikalo — sirf posX.
- **🧾 Purchase list patli + do tap + ‹ › (v2.309.0, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'upar wale chips small kar ke upar — screen par bill nazar aaye · ek screen par kam az kam 10 purchase · do dafa click = POS bill · neeche arrow · upar bill check kiya par click = agla bill'): list ka har bill 2 patli lines (`.pu-nmrow` naam + `.pu-amt` Rs · `.pu-fc` chhote chips, label `small` chhupa) — bare chips wapas mat lao. Do tap (450 ms, `puTap` / `puDbl`) = `openKhataBill(id,'@pu')` + `window.ntBcNav {list, at, pu:1}` = list ki tarteeb (`puRowsNav`) ke BAQI bill (check / post / cancel / mulazim ka purana nahi). POS Purchase phone (`.pp-cmp`): upar 2 patli lines — `pp-hrow` (naam · godam ▾ | W %) + `pp-bc` (☐ check · ✓ Check + 📌 Post › · `.pp-btot` Rs + 3/12); 'POST ho gaya' = patli `.pp-flow`; neeche `#actions` mein ‹ › (`.pp-nv`, WAHI `navGo`) — tab bara 'Pichhla / Agla' dabba nahi. ☐ check par tick (sirf jab list / agla ho) = `tickNext`: badlaav ho to pehle WAHI `save({direct:true})` + PC ka intezar, phir `window.ntBcSet` (bill-check) + `navGo(1)`; aakhri = wapas list (`ntPuBack`) / check list. Un-tick = purana raasta. POS bill ke upar naya kuch = isi 2 line patti mein, bara dabba nahi. **Tooling sabaq**: app.js mein account ka email likha hai — kuch sessions mein Bash ka koi bhi hukum jis mein app.js ka naam ho (grep / sed / node --check, commit note bhi) privacy plugin rok deta hai: tab Read / Edit tool se kaam karo, version bump mein app.js alag (Edit replace_all `?v=` + `APP_VER`), naya code alag file mein `node --check`, notes wala python Write tool se file mein.
- **✅ Bill check (v2.122.0, rules 2.73)**: POS purchase bill ka check = `billChecks/<pos-id>` (kis ne, kab) — UI bill-check.js (`bcOpen` screen: Baqi / Check hue × Aaj·7·15·Mahina·Sab; `bcTickHTML` khate ke card + bill khirki par ☐). "✓ Check + 📌 Post" = check + WAHI `sendPost` (naya post raasta mat banao). CAP pur.check. Naya POS-bill card / khirki banao to `bcTick(id)` lagao.
- **☰ Purchase compact lines + chips patti (v2.121.0)**: phone default = har item 3-line `.pp-cr` (sab khane + nafa % label mein), chips screen par NAHI — footer `#actions.pp-foot` ke andar `cbarHTML` (chuna hua item `selKey`: W/R chips · ↺ · 🏷 · 🏬 · ✕). PC default table. Naya purchase khana = rowsHTML + gridHTML + card teeno. **Nested grid qaida**: grid ke andar grid/inputs ho to parent `grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)` + `min-width:0` (1fr track input ki intrinsic width le kar side mein nikalta hai). Footer ke buttons par `html.lt body footer#actions … color:#fff!important` lagta hai — footer mein rangeen chhota button = apni !important rule.
- **📊 Purchase bill TABLE (v2.120.0)**: malik — "POS jaisi screen, har cheez nazar aaye, PC + mobile same, upar ka form party par click se". Default table (`gridHTML`, WAHI data-pp-* inputs), naam tap = neeche purana card (`lineCardHTML`), ⋮ Table/Card, CAP pur.grid. Upar supplier+godam ke baad sirf `.pp-hbar` patti. Table + card dono mein ek hi input ho sakta hai → paint hamesha querySelectorAll. Purchase ka naya khana = table column + card dono mein.
- **⛔ POS cancel purchase (sync.js v3, 2026-10-26)**: POS mein cancel (DocStatusID 3) hua purchase bill = khate ki `pos-<id>` entry bhi 🗑 (deleted, posStatus 3, note "⛔ POS mein CANCEL") — PC sync.js har chakkar 60 din ke cancel dekhta hai. Cancel bill ko sirf skip mat karo. Judi cash payment par `posCancelWarn` nishan, hatao mat.
- **👤 Ginti history (v2.113.0)**: har history line mein `n` (jis larke ke login se save) — writeCount mein, naye count likhne wale code mein bhi yehi.
- Halki themes (`html.lt`) header ke har button ko surf2 kar deti hain (themes.css) — header mein rangeen button ho to `html.lt header .stock-head .x:not(.selected):not(.on)` se wapas rang do (v2.105.9: ginti ka button safed par safed tha).
- **🖥 Live = POS screen ki tasveer (v2.229.0, nt-print v1.28 / nt-posui v1.13 `shot` / pos-sales-dekho v2.9)** (malik 10 Nov: "bill print ke baad aata hai — us ko live nahi keh sakte · item likho wo app mein · remove = remove"): POS bill SQL mein sirf Print par likhta hai aur Sale list UIA ko text nahi deti → bante bill ka WAHID raasta counter PC ki screen (posLive/`<naam>__shot`, sirf badle to, sirf jab server `posLive/_want` mein wo counter ho). 'Banta bill' ke liye SQL / UIA par dobara waqt mat lagao. NT-PRINT ke liye nayi FILE mat banao (purana nt-print sirf UPD_FILES lata hai aur manifest version likh deta — nayi file kabhi nahi aati) — code UPD_FILES wali file ke andar. Counter ka naya kaam = sirf jab koi dekh raha ho.
- **👀 Tally = Live jaisi (v2.230.0, bill-clip v2.5 / nt-print v1.32)** (malik 10 Nov: "upar teeno PC ke chips · select = neeche us ka bill · sab ka bill ⋮ mein · bill chip neela un-post / laal post · print ke baad tally · pehli item se"): tally ke upar counter chips (`pcChips` / `setPc`), 'Kis counter' khirki nahi; tally mein bill = `inQ(b)` (posted YA chhapa `ct`) — naya tally list / ginti isi se. Clip PRINT par banti hai; pehli item ka waqt `posAdds/<HOST>` (counter ki POS screen ke ADD) se `addStart` — `ft` (SQL) print ka waqt hota hai, us par bharosa mat karo. Naya counter laga = NT-PRINT + firebase-key + `nt-fix.ps1` (log mein AWAZ-ONLY na ho) warna Live tasveer / pehli item nahi.
- **🧱 Side par na khiske (v2.105.1)**: grid ke bachon par `min-width:0` / `minmax(0,1fr)`; chaura content (table, chips) apne `overflow-x:auto` dabbe mein. Poori khirki / screen kabhi daayein-baayein nahi khiskni chahiye (shell.js `lockX`).
- **👥 Hazri app jori (v2.279.0, rules 2.104, PC raat-report v1.7 / kh-wa v1.7; hazri app v251)** (malik 14 Nov: 'hazri wali app ko is app se connect karo, larkon ki report WhatsApp par · subah 10, shaam 6, jab maangein'): Hazri app ALAG repo (`nt-traders-`) + ALAG Firebase (nt-traders) — us ka code is repo mein KABHI nahi. Rishta sirf ek taraf: hazri ka Cloud Function `blueSync` aaj ka khulasa `hazriIn {k, p}` mein (chaabi `blueAccess/hazriKey`, app ke '🔗 Hazri app jorein' se) -> PC `hzPull` / `hzText` / `hzSend` -> waOut. Hazri ka naya report / hissa isi khulase (`blueSummary` rows, hazri repo functions/logic.js) mein field jor kar — PC ya app hazri ke Firebase ko seedha kabhi na parhe / likhe. Report sirf malik ko. AI nahi.
- **☰ Slider mein '📌 Is screen ke' NAHI (v2.279.1)** (malik 14 Nov: 'in ki upar zaroorat nahi ab'): `#chipDrawer .dr-pins` chhupa — ☰ = sirf Screens. Screen ke chhote kaam sirf ⋮ mein; slider ke upar chips ki koi nayi qatar mat banao.
- **👤 Number alag paigham mein + 0371… (wa-parchi v2.33, 14 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: tasveer -> number alag paigham -> Print auto = 'number nahi'): gahak ka number caption / reply ke ILAWA alag chhote paigham mein bhi (`bzNum`, 10 min, tasveer / Print se pehle ya baad). Mobile number = 030–039 (`PH_RE`; 036–039 sirf 0 / 92 ke saath) — naya number-regex likho to 3[0-5] par mat roko. Number 'nahi mila' ki shikayat = pehle wohi number `parseCaption` se guzaro.
- **🚶 Bahar jane ki request WhatsApp se (PC hz-wa v1.0 / wa-out v1.22; hazri functions v252, 14 Nov)** (malik: 'larkon ke code 1 Abdurehman… · larka Business number par code likhe -> wajah chune · roz ki pehli 2 khud manzoor, hamein sirf khabar · teesri se mujhe aur Mubeen ko, hum OK · kisi bhi phone se'): `pc/hz-wa.js` (wa-out `hzIn` / `hzOwn` / `HZ.tick`) — code `blueAccess/hzCodes` (kabhi nahi badalta), likhna SIRF hazri ke function `bluein` se (a: out / ok / no / back, wohi chaabi), manzoori = request wale paigham par reply 1 / 2 (waOut `hq`). Hazri ka naya WhatsApp kaam isi file mein; hazri ke Firebase ko PC seedha kabhi na likhe. Muft ginti `FREE` (2) aur wajoohat `REASONS` malik ke kehne par hi badlo. Hazri app ka layout Blue Khata jaisa karna malik ne BAAD ke liye rakha — bina kahe mat chhero.
- **📝 WhatsApp entry mein tafseel LAZMI (PC kh-wa v1.8 / wa-out v1.23, 14 Nov)** (malik: 'tafseel ke baghair nahi banni chahiye'): kharcha / payment li / di ki WhatsApp entry tafseel ke baghair KABHI nahi banti — na ho to entry se pehle sawal (`askTf`, khPend stg 'f', 10 min; 0 = chhoro; jawab `tafseel()`); aam likhai ka jawab wa-out `KHW.waits` se kh-wa tak. WhatsApp se entry ka naya raasta banao to `okNote` + `askTf` zaroor. Transfer par tafseel nahi maangi — malik ke kehne par.
- **🖨 Report par reply 'print' = thermal (PC wa-out v1.24 / pos-khata-sync v, 14 Nov)** (malik: 'koi bhi report aaye, reply mein print likhoon to print aa jaye · thermal par'): `repPrint` (wa-out) -> `khataPrints/wr-…` {j.k 'txt', wa} -> `txtSlip` -> nateeja malik ko (`prd-…`). Sirf malik, sirf report paigham (waOut id `rp-…`); malik ko jane wali nayi report ki id `rp-` se shuru karo taake print khud chale. Thermal ka naya likhai-print isi `k:'txt'` se — nayi collection nahi. PC band = paigham PC tak nahi pohanchta (subah dobara).
- **⚡ WhatsApp sab se tez (malik 14 Nov: 'jitna bhi kaam WhatsApp par ho raha hai, jo ho gaya aur jo hona hai, sab ki service tez treen')**: apne number (malik / Mubeen / jure) ka har jawab tez line (`isFast`) se, jawab se pehle AI / lamba kaam / intezar nahi (pehle jawab, kaam peeche); naya WhatsApp hukum banao to pehle foran chhota jawab (⏳ / ✅). Gahak ka faasla (`cfg.gap`) phir bhi KABHI kam nahi.
- **☑ Hazri app: kai larke ek saath Band / Delete (hazri v253, 14 Nov)** (malik: 'list mein 1-2-3-4-5 select, delete — larke kaam chhor gaye · jaldi ho'): hazri repo `data.bulkStaff` + Staff tab 'Kai chunein' patti. Kaam chhor gaye larke ke liye pehle 'Band' ka mashwara (record mehfooz), Delete wapas nahi aata. Larka hata = us ka WhatsApp code (hzCodes) wahi rehta hai, kisi aur ko nahi milta.
- **📱 Hazri app: malik ka panel CHHOTA (hazri v254, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'ek screen par kam az kam 8 larkon ke naam · upar / neeche bilkul small · requests / notifications ek button mein · baqi chips ⋮ mein · Urdu sirf mulazim ki hazri wale page par'): hazri repo owner.js — `.hz-bar` (tareekh · 🔔 `notiSheet` · ⋮ `menuSheet`), larke ki line `personRow` band = ek line, tap = `.cr-x`; `html.no-ur` = malik / manager ke panel par Urdu ki line NAHI (mulazim ke page par pehle jaisi). Hazri panel mein naya chip saamne mat lagao: khabar = 🔔, kaam = ⋮, larke ka = line ke andar. Tafseel hazri repo ke AI_NOTES mein.
- **🧾 Udhaar ki subah wali report (v2.284.0, PC raat-report v2.0)** (malik 15 Nov: 'rozana subah WhatsApp par · 1 month se wasooli nahi'): `udSend` -> malik ko `rp-…-u-…` (waU · udDays 30 · udTime 10:00 · udNow; app wa-report.js). Baqaya ka hisaab kh-wa `balOf` ki copy (`udSgn` / `udSkip`) — ek badlo to doosra. Udhaar ka koi paigham gahak ko KABHI khud nahi (sirf malik ko); gahak ko yaad-dihani alag kaam hai, malik ke OK par.
- **📍 Jagah-war recording (md pkg v47, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'har location ki alehda recording, andar folder'): `md/tools/jagah.py` — `JNAME` har tukde se pehle (globals jgNm / jgXY), folder `NT Awaz/<jagah>/`; nayi jagah ka naam = unlock par ek sawal (din mein 1 dafa, 0 = kabhi nahi). Jagah ki list (`jgList`) SIRF phone mein — coordinates / pata md/ (PUBLIC) mein KABHI nahi. Recording ka folder / naam badlo to FolderSync (NT Awaz -> Drive, andar ke folder samet) yaad rakho; Java se global = `variableSetter.setString / setInt`.

- **👥 Hazri number se (PC hz-wa v1.1 / hazri functions v255, 15 Nov)** (malik: 'number select kar ke hazri · sirf main / Mubeen · subah paigham dono ko'): `hazri 1 3 5` · `hazri nahi 3` · `hazri list` -> hazri function `a: in / unin` (manual, via wa). Sirf approvers (waParchi prFrom[0] / prTo[0]); larka khud is se hazri KABHI nahi laga sakta (WhatsApp ko jagah maloom nahi = jhooti hazri). Subah `ASK_AT` 9:15 ka sawal malik ke kehne par badlo. Kisi ka phone number repo (PUBLIC) mein mat likho — setting se lo.
- **📊 WhatsApp reports sab jure numbers ko, NAFA sirf malik (PC kh-wa v1.9 / raat-report v2.1, 15 Nov)**: `reports` menu har jure number ko, report maangne wale ko (`reportConfig.waFor` -> `WA_FOR`); 3 Profit / 4 Teeno sirf malik (menu mein line nahi + `WA_FOR` ho to `waP` false). 7 = `codesTxt` (sab hukum + larkon ke code + kharcha number) — naya WhatsApp hukum banao to is list mein bhi jodo. Roz wali reports sirf malik ko, hazri Mubeen ko bhi. Nafa / khareed rate kisi aur number ko KABHI nahi.
- **🏬 Larke ki sale = sirf dukan ka maal (larka-stats v3 / raat-report v2.1, v2.285.0)** (malik 15 Nov: 'Godam 1 / 2 / 3 se biki items larkon ki sale mein na aayein — apps aur WhatsApp par wohi'): godam ki lines (`GBranchID`, na ho = bill ki branch; naam mein 'godam') bill ke total se minus (`gdSrc` / `GD_SALE`, dono files mein EK jaisa — ek badlo to doosra); SQL na chale = purana hisaab (`sum.gd` 0). Larke ki sale ka naya hisaab / report isi se; POS user wala hissa alag (nahi badla).

- **📵 Nayi report = sirf maangne par (malik 15 Nov: 'itni reports parhi nahi jayengi')**: malik ko roz khud aane wala NAYA paigham / report bina us ke kahe mat lagao — nayi report `reports` menu (kh-wa stg r) mein ek number bana kar do (8 = 📦 maal khatam hone wala, raat-report `khSend`). Roz khud sirf wohi jo malik ne khud maanga (shaam ki reports, hazri, udhaar, hazri ka sawal).

- **🏦 Bank screen chips chhote + Mahina-war patti (v2.298.0, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'chips small kar do · Mahina / weekly dabayein to upar wali patti bhi usi par'): bank-inbox `pDone` / `pPend` = 🔔 · ↩ · 📝 Note · 🖨 Chhapi · Dobara print EK patli qatar (26px, `.bkp-acts`, voucher number chhota `shortCode` sirf dikhawe mein); din ki patti `gHead` range se: Aaj = din-war · 7 din = ek dabba · Mahina / Sab = mahine-war (har line par `.bkp-dt` tareekh). Bank screen mein naya chip = isi `.bkp-acts` qatar mein, alag bara dabba nahi.

- **📏 Bank screen = ek screen par 10 entries (v2.299.0, 16 Nov — PAKKA QAIDA)** (malik: 'upar wala chart bohat chhota · ek screen par ek time par 10 payments ke chips · qaida mein likh do'): hero `.bkp-hero.cmp` = 2 patli lines (naam · baqaya · Lene/Dene / ↓ Aaye · ↑ Gaye · chhota 20px chart) — bara hero / bara chart / 'BANK ACCOUNT' dabba wapas mat lao. Har lagi entry `.bkp-row.cmp` = 2 patli lines (naam + raqam / waqt · 🖨✓ 🔔 📝 nishan + ✓ tag); Reminder · ↩ · Note · Dobara print sirf TAP par (`.bkp-x`, `rowOpen`, ek waqt ek, poori list dobara paint nahi). Aaj / 7 din / Mahina / Sab + 'Kaise kaam karta hai' ⋮ mein (`data-mn-seg` / `data-mn-box`). Bank screen mein jo bhi naya jodo, pehle socho: phone par ek waqt 10 entries nazar aati rahein.

- **📄 Khata PDF + parchi = EK table (v2.300.0, PC pos-khata-sync w4b, 16 Nov — PAKKA)** (malik ne DigiKhata ki PDF dikhai: 'jab bhi PDF / print nikle aisa aana chahiye — dono sides ke aage ek table'): PDF = # · Tareekh · Tafseel · baayan khana (laal) · daayan khana (hara) · Baqaya (us waqt ka, `runningOf`), neeche Kul; raqam daayein (`pdfCell` `r:1`). Do-taraf wala layout wapas mat lao. Thermal = `khataTpData` `A` [[tareekh, 1|2, paisa, tafseel, baqaya]] -> PC `khataSlip` v2: line 1 '#. tareekh tafseel', line 2 baayan | daayan | baqaya; purana L/R bhi bhejte hain (purana PC purani parchi). Khate ki koi nayi PDF / print isi ek-table shakal mein.

- **🧘 10 min break BAND (md pkg v48, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'wo 10 minute wala band karna tha'): Paani popup ke baad break ka sawal HATA (`pkg.py` mein `BRM.ask()` nahi jorta) + '🧘 10 min break' macro `off` mein (delete nahi). Wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe; `brk.py` file rakhi hai.

- **📅 Khata PDF / print par tareekh ke chips (v2.301.0, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'khata print PDF par date — aaj kal week monthly apni date bhi, upar chips'): khate ki Report preview ke upar `.kr-row` (Aaj · Kal · 7 din · Mahina · Sab · 🗓 do date) — chip = WAHI `report()` dobara (`krGo`: from/to sirf us dauran, khate ki screen ka filter wapas wahi); PDF + 🖨 thermal dono isi tareekh se. Khate ki koi nayi PDF / print ka raasta banao to `kr` option + yehi chips.

- **🖥 Counter ka naam = NT-PRINT se (v2.302.0, PC pc-names.js v1.0, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'computer ke naam auto aane chahiye · naam device ka NT-PRINT ke zariye · server par NT-PRINT chal raha hai ke nahi'): host -> naam EK jagah `pc/pc-names.js` (PC_DEFAULT < local-config pcNames < printPCs.name) — bill-clip / live-cam / pos-sales-dekho / gate-pass / raat-report sab `PN.names()`; camera channel purane naam se likha ho to `PN.oldName()` se bhi milta. App ⚙️ Camera setup ki list = NT-PRINT wale PC (🟢 chalu vX / 🔴 band N min / ⚪ NT-PRINT nahi, 🖥 server) + aaj ke bill wale counter; Save par purane naam ki line hat jati hai. Naya counter naam wala kaam isi file se — scripts mein naya PC_DEFAULT mat likho.

- **📒 Khata = EK TABLE (v2.303.0, 16 Nov)** (malik ne DigiKhata ki tasveer dikhai: 'jaise ye hai — payment di, wasooli aur aage balance'): khate ki screen default = `ledgerTblHTML` (Tafseel · baayan khana · daayan khana · Baqaya, `runningOf`), har qatar `.ledger-item.li-c.kt-r` (tap = wahi `li-x` chips, `liOpen`). Purana do-khana view ⋮ 'Khate ka view' -> ▥ Purana view (`sam-kh-view` = old, har phone apna). Khate ki entry ka naya chip / nishan dono view mein (li-x EK jaisa) — do khanon wala view default wapas mat lao. **v2.304.0** (malik: 'thora small ho to table mein aa jaye'): khane 68 · 68 · 78px, font 12px, paise chhote (`ktN` -> `.kt-p`), tafseel 2 line tak — khata table mein naya khana / bara font mat jodo (raqam phat-ti hai). **☑ Tally (v2.305.0)** (malik: 'sirf hamari tally, khate se koi taluq nahi · tick = raqam peeli'): table ke upar chip `.kt-tg` (`sam-kh-tally`), har qatar `.kt-ck` -> `kt-y` (peela), tick `sam-kh-tk:<khata>` sirf isi phone par — hisaab / POS / Firestore par KABHI asar nahi; tick par poori list dobara paint nahi (sirf wohi qatar + ginti).

- **📏 JAGAH BACHAO — PAKKA QAIDA (malik 16 Nov: 'qaida mein likh do — jagah bachani hai screens par')**: naya chip / button kabhi apni ALAG qatar mein nahi (Tally chip ne poori qatar le li thi). Pehle socho: search ke andar daayein (`label.search` mein absolute chip, `pp-scanbtn` / `.kt-sbtn` namoona), header, ya ⋮. Chip chhota (30px, 12px font) aur upar. Screen ki har nayi cheez se pehle poochho: kya yeh asal list ki jagah kha raha hai? (v2.306.0: ☑ Tally = search ke andar `ktMount`.; v2.307.0: malik ne GOL 32px button maanga — ginti chhota narangi badge. Search ke andar ka button kabhi search se bara / bahar na nikle: height / min-height / padding `!important` (aam button rule 44px bana deta hai).)

- **📒 Daily Sale: closing cash HATI + change Sale ke neeche + Sirf khata = chip (v2.317.0, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'closing cash ki ab zaroorat nahi · payment wala dabba bhi hata do · change aur kal ka change sale ke neeche — jagah bach jaye · Sirf khata purchase ke button jaisa — dabane par neeche khule'): Daily = 3 khane (Sale · Wasooli · Akhrajat, `.cs3`); Kal / Aaj ka change `chgHTML` Sale ke total ke neeche (patli line / <details>); ⚖️ sirf 'Galle mein hona' — Closing / Farq / Barabar / 💸 'Is cash se payment bhejo' / 🔒 Closing button wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe (data mehfooz, code para hai). Sirf khata = dy-strip ka `.dy-kc` chip -> neeche `.dy-kdrop` (WAHI dyEntryHTML). Daily PDF / 🖨 Sab print (`nc:1`, pos-khata-sync x) / raat WhatsApp report (raat-report v2.5) mein bhi closing / farq nahi. Sabaq: malik ne pehle 'upar chip -> neeche wali list par jao' rad kiya — 'neeche khule' ka matlab hamesha chip ke FORAN neeche dropdown (supplier chip jaisa), page ke neeche nahi.

- **📈 Rate change = dukan ke rates + FORAN (v2.318.0, PC rate-change v1.2, 17 Nov)** (malik: 'rate change wali screen ghalat rate bata rahi hai' · 'jaise rate badla item us list mein seedha'): POS ke item rate DO jagah — dbo.Items (master) aur dbo.ItemBranchRate (branch). POS screen se rate badlo to sirf ItemBranchRate badalta hai; asal / item screen wala rate = dukan (NOOR TRADERS, naam se) ki ItemBranchRate (sync-stock jaisa). Koi bhi nayi PC script jo SALE / W / R / khareed rate dikhaye, dbo.Items ke SaleRate* se KABHI nahi — ItemBranchRate se. App mein W pcs = SaleRateSize || SaleRate3, R pcs = SaleRate2 || SaleRate (item screen jaisa). 'Foran' wali PC list = sasta CHECKSUM stamp har 10 s (sirf badle to Firestore) — sirf MAX id nahi (bill ka rate badalna / cancel chhoot jata).
- **🚨 Dukan nigrani = phone alarm (v2.319.0, rules 2.106, PC doctor v3.13 + nabz.js v1.0, md pkg v55)** (malik 17 Nov: 'dukan ka system ruke to phone bataye · baqi notifications jaisa screen par pop'): server PC `pc/nabz.js` (doctor ke andar, sirf server) har 2 min `pcBeat/<chaabi>` {d, at, act, hz, r, rk, y, yk} — sirf haal ki likhai, paisa / gahak KABHI nahi; malik ke phone ka '🚨 Dukan nigrani' (md/tools/nabz.py) har 3 min (9:30 am – 9 pm) parhta hai: 🔴 heads-up awaaz (PC / internet 10 min chup jab aaj bill bane hon, thermal phansa, WhatsApp toota, der se bill nahi; 30 min baad dobara, 3 dafa tak) · 🟡 khamosh (NT-PRINT / camera band, ijazat, 11 baje tak PC nahi); hazri chhutti = kuch nahi. Chaabi = doc id (24+ harf, rules sirf get) — app Settings → PC scripts → '🚨 Phone alarm jorein' webhook `nt_nabz` se phone ko; repo / chat mein KABHI nahi. Naya 'malik ko phone par batao' wala kaam = nabz.js `judge()` mein naya r / y (key + likhai) — naya macro / naya socket / naya paigham ka raasta mat banao; 🔴 sirf us cheez par jis par malik ko foran uthna pare (baqi 🟡), aur har alarm ka 'theek ho gaya' raasta bhi. Phone ka apna net na ho = kuch nahi (PC par ilzam nahi).
- **📊 Item report ka stock + order (v2.320.0, 18 Nov)** (malik: 'godam par 1 likha ho, Noor Traders gol daire mein · pieces mein bhi order likha aaye'): item report / Supplier-wise / Sab suppliers ke chips mein stock HAMESHA `stkRingsHTML` (har godam alag sunehri gol + number, 'Noor Traders' laal pill) — 🏬 / 🏪 emoji wali saada likhai wapas mat lao; 🛒 chip `odTxt` = poore ctn + asal zaroorat (ctn + pcs) jab alag. Item list mein naya stock / order dikhana isi se. **🧾 PC Purchase 📱 Mobile jaisa**: ⋮ `data-pp-mob` (sam-pp-mob, har PC apna, default POS jaisa) — PC par phone wali screen; naya posMode-wala kaam `posMode()` se hi jaancho (seedha window.innerWidth nahi), warna yeh switch toot jata hai.

- **🖨 Purana server = sirf counter: NT-PRINT C:\nt-print (nt-print-setup v3 / nt-fix v2 / nt-print v1.34, 18 Nov)** (malik: 'pehle humara server tha, ab sirf client · NT-PRINT auto, off na ho · khata-sync wala sab delete?'): jis PC ke C:\khata-sync mein server ke scripts pare hon wahan NT-PRINT / chaabi / local-config purane folder mein KABHI nahi (chaabi wapas = purane scripts jaag kar app ki entries hata sakte — 16 Nov) — setup khud C:\nt-print banata hai (apni bat, purani .BAND chaabi sirf wahan, SQL / naam nahi), purana folder malik ke 'h' par startup se hata kar NAAM badalta hai (mitata nahi). Counter ka naya PC-command bhi pehle yeh pehchan kare. 'Chal raha' sirf NAYI log line se (purani line = jhoota). Repo ki `nt-print-auto.bat` mat badlo (chalti bat badli = cmd ghalat offset se parhta, loop toot-ta). Scanner: dheema scanner `scanGap2` (6+ harf / 5+ hindse, 90 ms) — C# badlo to DLL ka NAYA naam (purana cache naya code rokta).
- **🆔 NT-PRINT ka number = PC ka ALAG naam (nt-print v1.35, 18 Nov)** (malik: 'server par NT-PRINT chal raha magar nishan red' · 'poora naam do'): PC_ID pehle naam ke pehle 8 harf tha — sab 'DESKTOP-*' = 'desktop-' (ek printPCs doc, ek doosre ka haal mitate, bill do PC uthate). Ab 'DESKTOP-' / 'LAPTOP-' hata kar 10 harf (app ki hadd `appSales.counter` <= 20). Kisi bhi naye 'har PC ka' doc / id ka number banao to pehle jaancho ke do PC ek hi number na banayein (naam ka shuru sab ka ek hota hai). Server PC par NT-PRINT parchi KABHI nahi chhapta (malik: printer na chherna) — wahan print chahiye ho to malik se poochh kar.
- **🖐 Live mein POS screen dabba khiskao (v2.321.0, 18 Nov)** (malik: 'background nazar nahi aa raha — movable, hum khud aage peeche karein'): landscape / PC par patti se ghaseeto, ◢ se naap, ↺ wapas; jagah % mein (`sam-lv-shotpos`), portrait pehle jaisa. Video ke upar koi naya tairta dabba banao to yehi namoona (khiskane wala, yaad rakhne wala, ↺). Pointer capture ke baad click ka target poora dabba hota hai — dabbe ke andar ke tap ka faisla `closest` se mat karo, khiskane ka waqt (SPM) dekho. **👥 Mulazim screen khamosh nahi (v2.321.0)**: async click handler ki ghalti app mein chup-chaap gum hoti hai (unhandledrejection sirf permission-denied dikhata) — khirki banane wala naya handler ghalti par khirki mein ya notice mein likhe; list ka har card apne try/catch mein (ek kharab doc poori screen na roke).

- **📲 Bill ki video WhatsApp par + 🩺 Live saaf (v2.322.0, rules 2.107, live-cam v1.7 / bill-clip v2.8 / wa-out v1.27 / kh-wa v1.11, 18 Nov)** (malik: 'bill number likhein to us ki video hamare aur Mubeen ke WhatsApp par full speed se — customer ko wait na karna pare' · 'NVR se mangwana — us ko rehne do' · 'video blur aa raha'): tally ⋮ 📲 (CAP nz.wa) / Business number par `video 138841` -> billClipWa -> PC bani clip (concat copy) ya apni recording -> wa-media\bv-<id>.mp4 -> waOut {vf} -> SIRF malik + Mubeen (waParchi prFrom[0] / prTo[0]). Video wala naya kaam isi raaste (doosra socket / NVR playback nahi; gahak ko video kabhi nahi). wa-out mein file sirf `bv-*.mp4` + wa-media folder — waOut ka koi naya file-field banao to yehi pabandi. Firestore set + merge mein `'a.b'` key = seedha naam (naqsha nahi) — map mein jorna = `{a: {b: x}}` ya update(). Live grey / dhabbe = pehle 🩺 chip (NVR→PC / PC ke andar / phone net) dekhne ko kaho; live-cam ka UDP dabba + discardcorrupt mat hatao.
- **📸 Sehat lock (v2.323.0, md pkg v56)** (malik 19 Nov: 'fruit / nuts / coffee / paani na piyun to mobile access na kar paun · picture, AI khud dekhe · LIVE camera, gallery nahi · paani 40 min · coffee 2:00 · fruit 2:30 · nuts 4:00 · khana khatam · 10 min mein photo warna block 10 → 30 → 60 · awaaz nahi, sirf chhoti si bell · lock mein bhi photo se khule'): `md/tools/sehat.py` (💧 Paani badla, ☕ Coffee · Fruit · Nuts = purane Khana-peena ka GUID, 📸 Sehat lock naya) + app `sehat.js` (#sehat=<kind>.<nonce>, sirf getUserMedia, `aiImage`, webhook `nt_sehat` shOk / shZ). Band SIRF `raatband.PK` list (list ke bahar kuch nahi) — nayi app band karni ho to wahi list. Waqt 11:00–23:59. Reminder ka phone par bolna (SpeakText) wapas mat lao. Photo / gallery ka raasta (file input) is screen mein KABHI nahi. Waqt / list / lock ke minute malik ke kehne par hi badlo.
- **🔢 WhatsApp PIN — malik ka apna link bhi (v2.323.0, wa-parchi v2.39)** (malik: 'oper barcode ke sath pin ka option'): waCmd {do 'code', ph} -> requestPairingCode (print phone wala tareeqa). Naya WhatsApp link banao to QR ke saath PIN bhi do; 'off' haalat mein PC ka `err` dikhao (sirf 'PC par band' likhna jhoota tha).
- **📲 WhatsApp setting = ① / ② + naam (v2.324.0)** (malik ne print phone ka number upar apni jagah jor diya — 'screen par sab ka naam likh do, phir confusion nahi'): ① upar = SIRF malik ka apna WhatsApp (prFrom[0]), ② neeche = dukan / Mubeen ka print phone; ghalat / dohra number = laal warning. Har number chip `numTxt` (naam + number). Jahan bhi number dikhao, naam saath (`nmOf`) — akela number malik ko samajh nahi aata. Naya link / number wala dabba banao to heading mein saaf likho kis ka number. **v2.324.1 ghalti**: patti par 'Settings → 📲 WhatsApp Business' likha jo row hai hi nahi — Business number = Settings → 🔌 POS aur AI → '📲 Gahak ko WhatsApp parchi (naya number)' (ab patti mein seedha button). Malik ko kisi setting / screen ki jagah batao to asal naam code (setRow label) se dekh kar, andaze se nahi.
- **🏦 Alfa notification = bank entry (v2.325.0, PC bank-parchi v2.8, md pkg v57)** (malik 19 Nov: 'aisa pop aaye to add kar do · SMS aaye to double na ho'): phone '🏦 Alfa notification' (md/tools/alfa.py) -> WAHI bankSms (src 'notif', from 'Alfa'); chaabi app bank-phone.js webhook `nt_bank` (bkU / bkK, din mein 1, khamosh). 'BAF A/C' wali notif = Alfalah parser (bank-parchi + bank-sms.js EK jaisa — ek badlo to doosra). Double ki hifazat = jurwan (ZA+dir+amt ±30 min) + Tx ID — kisi aur bank ki notification jodo to pehle dekho us ka SMS / email bhi aata hai aur dono mein ek hi Tx ID / waqt hai. OTP / PIN wali notification server par KABHI nahi.
- **📸 Phone se Blue Khata kholna = installed app (v2.325.0, md v57)** (malik: 'Chrome mein khulta hai, speed se nahi — do tab ka paigham'): phone macro mein Blue Khata ka link OpenWebPage se seedha mat kholo — sehat.py `OPEN` jaisa: Java queryIntentActivities -> package 'webapk' (installed app) -> startActivity, na mile tab Chrome. App mein phone se aane wala koi bhi #hash kaam pehle UI / camera dikhaye, login / data ka intezar baad mein (sehat.js need()). ⛔ Lock lagte hi (ya agli dafa phone kholte hi) photo ka popup (shLkA) — lock wala koi naya kaam bhi saath 'abhi khol do' ka raasta de.
- **🔤 Label par Urdu kabhi chup-chaap gayab nahi (label-print v5.1, 19 Nov)** (malik: 'naya server lagne se Urdu nahi aa raha'): saved naam na ho = roman2ur (bina AI); Urdu tasveer fail ho to wajah job ke `ur` mein -> app label patti par narangi line. Ek dafa ki ghalti ko HAMESHA ke liye yaad mat karo (cache mein null) — thori der baad dobara. Naya server / naya PC lage to Urdu label (aur thermal Urdu) pehle din hi chhap kar dekho.
- **📸 Sehat lock v58 (v2.326.0)** (malik: 'phone kholne par photo ka option nahi · apps 1-2 min khuli rehti · app mein bhi photo ki screen'): lock / photo baqi = HAR unlock par popup (ek dafa wala qaida nahi); lock + screen on = ek 2 s loop (shGd) — 'rok' wala koi bhi phone macro sirf app-khuli trigger ya 30 s tick ke bharose kabhi nahi. ☰ 📸 Sehat photo (sehat.js mode app) -> `shOk=a:<kind>&shT` (3 min) — phone ko app se koi bhi naya 'kar diya' paigham do to waqt (shT) saath bhejo, purana link dobara na chale. Remote ki Gmail export (NT phone macros vN) import ke foran baad banti hai — naya macro us mein na dikhe to 'nahi laga' mat kaho, agli email / malik se poochho.
- **🎬 Factory video (v2.327.0, PC wa-parchi v2.40 + fac-voice.js v1.0)** (malik 19 Nov: 'factory ki all items ka video · subah tasveer · AI crop pehle se · HD · upar bolne wala · sab tum khud handle karo'): fac-video.js (Factory ⋮ / ☰ #fvNav, sirf malik) — tasveer WAHI factory/phIdx + ph_* (fac-photo saveHd / hdOf), naya data model mat banao; crop = Online Dukan wala 🤖 box_2d (malik ✅ Save se pehle dekhta). Awaaz = factory/vq_* {st new → PC mp3 st ok}, tasveer lagte hi pehle se maango (PC raat ko band — video ke waqt intezar par bharosa nahi), phone cache 'sam-fac-vc'. Video phone par (canvas + WebAudio → MediaRecorder, H.264 pehle) — PC ki zaroorat nahi. Gaana / khareed rate / nafa video mein KABHI nahi. Naya 'factory ki video / awaaz' kaam isi screen + isi data se; bolne wala jumla PC fac-voice `sentence()` mein (app sirf k/id/nm/rs bhejti).
- **👥 Har bill ki video WhatsApp GROUP mein + 🪟 transparent patti (v2.328.0, bill-clip v2.9 / wa-out v1.28, 19 Nov)** (malik: 'din mein jitni videos banein SAB group mein · counter-wise · video ke upar items naam + tadad, transparent — background nazar aaye, blur nahi'): tally ⋮ '👥 Group videos' (sirf malik) -> blueAccess/billWaGroup {on, g, gd, gn, lim, end} -> bill-clip har kamyab clip -> waOut {grp} (20 s faasla, raat band, din ki hadd) -> billClip.wg (dobara nahi). Patti = sirf gol kinara + kaale kinare wali likhai (andar blur / rang KABHI nahi — malik ka chuna); drawtext hamesha relative fontfile + textfile + expansion=none (Windows raasta / % / ' tor dete hain); patti na bane to bina patti video (kabhi rukna nahi). Bill ki video ka har naya raasta = `mkVideo` (patti khud). wa-out mein group = apni line: gahak ka faasla / hadd kabhi kam nahi, group us mein nahi ginta; group ka paigham parhna nahi. Group sirf malik ka chuna — gahak / kisi number ko video kabhi nahi.
- **📸 Sehat photo = foran band, AI service worker mein (sehat.js v1.3, v2.328.0)** (malik: 'picture lagate hi screen band — AI background mein · notification: apps khul gayi / dobara picture'): 📸 -> khirki band -> sw.js `shJob` (Gemini + webhook, app band ho tab bhi) -> notification ✅ / ✕ (✕ par tap = `#sehat=app.<cheez>`). SW mein naya kaam = `message` + `e.waitUntil` + MessageChannel ack (purana SW jawab na de = app mein wahi kaam). sw.js classic script hai — `import()` nahi chalta, is liye Gemini call wahan seedha (ai-tally ka generate nahi). AI key SW ko sirf us kaam ke message mein, kahin save nahi.
- **📹 Live WhatsApp par + mazeed cameras (v2.329.0, bill-clip v2.10 / live-cam v1.8 / kh-wa v1.12, 19 Nov)** (malik: 'live likhein to camera ke naam khud aa jayein · number · 30 sec … 30 min · tarteeb se · counter ke ilawa mazeed cameras · recording chalu'): asal chalti video WhatsApp par mumkin NAHI (computer wala WhatsApp video call nahi karta) — 📸 tasveer + pichhle waqt ki video + app ka #live link. Cameras = counter (CF.ch, recording hamesha) + `billClip.xc` [{n, ch, r}] (ARRAY — merge mein poora badalta; map rakhoge to hataya camera wapas aa jayega). Camera ki nayi cheez isi camList / xc se; tasveer recording wale camera ki file se (NVR par naya rabta nahi), sirf recording band camera par ek RTSP rabta. WhatsApp ka live sirf malik / Mubeen, recording chalu / band sirf malik; video SIRF maangne wale ko, 5 min ke hisse tarteeb se (WhatsApp 16 MB hadd).
- **📦 Loading ginti — lakeer wala camera (v2.331.0, rules 2.108, PC ginti-cam v1.0 + wa-out v1.30, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'install kar do · camera baad mein lagega · sab ek saath — ginti + note milan + bina note alarm + Gemini naam + reply se seekhna'): PC ginti-cam.js bill-clip ki recording ke BAND tukron (clip-rec) se — NVR par naya rabta KABHI nahi, camera ka ⏺ Rec ON zaroori; YOLOE ONNX (pc/ginti-model.onnx) onnxruntime-node apne folder ginti-mods mein; waqia gintiEv · note milan gintiDoc (posTransfers / gatePass ±win, AI NAHI) · 🚨 bina note = collage + Gemini andaza → waOut gi- SIRF malik · reply (wa-out giReply) → gintiEv.rep → item yaad (agli dafa Gemini ko misaal). App ginti.js (☰ / Bill tally ⋮, CAP nz.ginti OPT_IN, setup sirf malik: camera · godam · lakeer 2 tap · alarm). Godam camera ka naya kaam (ginti / chori / AI) isi data + isi recording raaste se — doosra camera reader / NVR rabta mat banao; ginti 1-2 min peeche hoti hai (tukra band hone ke baad) — malik ko 'live' mat kaho. Model ki asal training nahi — 'seekhna' = malik ke bataye naam misaal ban kar Gemini ko jaate hain. Lakeer / ginti ki shart badalne se pehle asal camera ki video par jaancho.
- **🕒 WhatsApp par waqt ki video (PC kh-wa v1.13 / bill-clip v2.14 / wa-out v1.31, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'live ke andar time ka clip — hum time likhein, us waqt ki video'): live → camera → 8 🕒 (ya camera menu par seedha / 'live 3 3:45') → '3:45' (2 min) · '3:45 5' · '3:40-3:50' (30 min tak), am / pm na ho = aaj ka guzra waqt → billClipWa {f0, f1} → bill-clip apni recording se (NVR nahi). Mazeed camera ki ⏺ recording ab 9 par. Video sirf utni purani jitni recording (`billClip.rk`, default 3 ghante; raat PC band = kal ki nahi) — purana maange to saaf paigham, khamosh nahi. WhatsApp mein naya 'aage likhi baat ka intezar' wala qadam banao to `waits(number, likhai)` mein us ki shart jodo (bina shart har paigham kh-wa kha jayega).
- **💾 Recording 2 din + 🕒 waqt jaisa likha kabhi khamosh nahi (v2.333.0, PC bill-clip v2.15 / kh-wa v1.14, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'recording poora do din ki rehni chahiye · 750 GB ki hard pari hai' + '11:06 SE 7 minute ki' ka jawab hi nahi aaya): recording ka waqt ab `billClip.rkh` GHANTE (default 48, purana `rk` min nahi parha jata) — app ⚙️ Camera setup; drive 25 GB se kam = 6 ghante se purane tukre khud mitte (`diskGuard`, haal billClipPc.dk → setup mein 💾 line). Un-posted bill ki clip ki khirki 3 ghante hi (rk badhane se PC purane bills ki clips na banaye). Waqt = kh-wa `tParse` ('X se N minute ki / tak' · 'kal' · 'subah / shaam' · 'X se Y'); WhatsApp ke kisi sawal ke baad jawab ka andaza (regex) TANG mat rakho — us stage par jawab jaisa kuch bhi aaye (waqt mein ':' wala hindsa) kh-wa tak pohnchao aur samajh na aaye to madad bhejo (malik ki apni likhai ka koi bhi tareeqa = jawab zaroor). Mit chuki recording wapas nahi aati — retention kam karne se pehle malik se poochho.
- **💽 Recording kahan + 10 din (v2.334.0, PC bill-clip v2.16 / rec-dir.js / ginti-cam v1.1, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'PC scripts mein poora drive show — C ya D · phir selection · us mein 10 din ki video bhi pari rahe'): drive = `billClip.rdrv`, din = `billClip.rkd` (default 10; v2.333 ka rkh ab nahi) — app Settings → 🖥️ PC scripts → 💽 (pc-drives.js, `setBillClipPart` bina `at`). Recording ka path HAMESHA `pc/rec-dir.js` se (active / roots / segFiles) — recording parhne wali koi bhi nayi script (camera AI / clip) seedha `DIR\clip-rec` mat likhe, warna doosri drive ki recording nazar nahi aayegi. Drive badli = purani recording wahin parhi jati hai aur din pure kar ke mitti hai (move / copy kabhi nahi — dukan ke waqt hard par bojh). Recording mitane wala kaam sirf setting aane ke baad (`cfReady`) — khali setting = sab channel 'nikle' samjhe jate. USB / network drive chunne nahi dete (nikal sakti hai).
- **⏱ Bill video ki raftar (v2.336.0, PC bill-clip v2.17, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'fi video kitna time laga'): counter clip meta `rd` (tayyar) + `mk` (banane ke ms) — app 📋 Bills mein har bill ka ⚡ / 🐢 chip + average ki patli line. 'Video der se / slow' ki shikayat = pehle yeh list dekhne ko kaho (🐢 = NVR se bani = recording nahi thi / mit chuki thi). Clip ka naya raasta banao to `rd` / `mk` bhi likho.
- **🚪 Gate pass = godam ka camera (v2.337.0, PC bill-clip v2.18, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'jis item ka gate pass banta hai us item ka godam ka camera — screen ke upar bhi + WhatsApp wali recording mein bhi'): gatePass ke har godam ki video billClip/<SaleID>~g<godam> (gp 1) — post se 20 s pehle → us godam ki sab items ✓ ke 1 min baad (✓ na ho = post + 10 min, max 15 min), SIRF apni recording se (camera ⏺ Rec ON). Camera: malik ka chuna `billClip.gc` [{g, c}] (LIST — map nahi, merge mein hataya wapas na aaye) › 📦 Loading ginti ka camera › naam se. Tally mein counter video ke upar chhota (apna waqt, sync nahi) · group mein bill video ke BAAD · 'video <bill>' mein bhi. Godam / gate wala naya video kaam isi gp clip + isi recording raaste se; billClip ki koi list / ginti banao to '~g' / '~cam' docs bill nahi hain (sid se jorein).
- **🚪 Ek godam = 2 camera (v2.338.0, PC bill-clip v2.19, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'aik hi godam par two cameras — dono side by side'): `billClip.gc` [{g, c, i 1|2}] (auto = ginti + naam se, 2 tak); doosre camera ki clip `~g<godam>b` (ci 2), WAHI waqt. App tally mein dono saath saath (pehla master — doosra us ke saath chale / ruke); WhatsApp par EK video, ek ke upar doosra (640x720 — phone par side by side bohat chhota). Godam video ki patti camera ki apni ghari (upar daayein) ke NEECHE (y0 48 / 52) — malik ne ghari dhakti dekhi. Kai camera ki video jorni ho to `ovRender` `src.ins` + `o` (W / H / y0 / R) se, naya renderer mat banao.
- **📏 Stock screen = patli line (v2.330.0, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'ek screen par kam az kam 8 chips'): har item 2 patli lines (`rowSlimHTML`, `.stock-row.sk-c`), tap = wohi khule (`skOpen` / `skToggle`, ek waqt ek, sirf class — list dobara nahi), Save / ✓ Tick ke baad agla khud khule (`skNext`), scan = wohi khule. Item ka naya chip / kaam = `.sk-x` ke andar `.sk-tools` qatar mein (band line mein KABHI nahi — 8 items ki jagah); band line par naya nishan sirf `.sk-mk` chip. Purana bara card ⋮ '📋 Poora card' (`sam-sk-big`) — hatao mat. Stock jaisi koi aur lambi list ho to yehi namoona.
- **🎥 NVR ka IP pakka = Static 192.168.10.7 (20 Nov)** (malik: 'har dafa IP badal deta hai'): Dahua NVR DHCP par tha, router naya IP deta, camera / live / bill video ruk jate. Ab Static (NIC1 ka Modify ✏️ -> DHCP band; bahar wala DHCP switch sirf DNS ka hai). Camera / live phir ruke to pehle NVR ka 'Mode' (Static / DHCP) aur app ki 'NVR ka IP (LAN)' milao — code mein IP dhoondne wala naya kaam bina malik ke OK mat banao.
- **🪟 Video ki patti = DAAYEIN patli column (bill-clip v2.11, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'aik side par — right side'): bill video ki patti ab daayein (chaurai 214, upar se, 12 items fi safha) — beech / baayein ka counter khula. Patti wapas poori chaurai / neeche mat le jao; naya likha hissa isi column mein (naam `cut` se chhota). Video / patti ki shakal badalne se pehle malik ko asal ffmpeg se bani namoona tasveer dikhao (is dafa malik ne 'picture do' kaha). Manifest ka note badalna ho to line-war badlo (note mein `\"` hote hain — regex `[^"]*` JSON tor deta hai).

- **⚡ Bill clip = DO LINE + 🎥 2-3 camera + 🪟 PC patti (v2.332.0, rules 2.109, PC bill-clip v2.12, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'clip BHT slow' — 12:30 ke bill 14:20 tak ⏳ · '2-3 cameras ki ikatthi recording' · 'PC par bhi WhatsApp wala view'): PC ki clip ab do line mein — 'a' naya bill PEHLE sirf apni recording se (NVR ka intezar kabhi nahi, deferA), 'b' purana pehle + NVR playback; ek bill ek line (inProg). Clip / camera ka koi naya kaam lambe intezar (NVR playback / bari download) wala ho to us ko tez line ke saath kabhi ek qatar mein mat daalo. Doosre camera ki video = billClipCam -> billClip/<SaleID>~<key> (counter clip ka WAHI t0 + dur, sirf recording wale camera) — billClip ki list / ginti mein '~' wale docs bill nahi hain. PC par tally = isWa() (patti video ke upar daayein, ⋮ '🪟 View' se purana) — tally ka naya hissa PC par isi patti / stage mein. Video ka naap vh se nahi: fitStage() (html zoom .92 + 100vh = ghalat naap, asal rect se). **📦 Loading ginti** PC par bhi poori screen + ☰ chip Nigrani login mein bhi.

- **📄 Video patti ka safha = NT-PRINT ka waqt + ⚡ group video tez (PC bill-clip v2.13, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'jaise hi last item red lobia NT print par OK ho to agla safha auto' · 'bill ki video WhatsApp par aur tez'): patti ka safha `pageAt` — posAdds (counter ke ADD waqt, `addTimes`) se: pichhle safhe ki AAKHRI item ke 2 s baad agla (agli item pehle aaye to usi waqt), aakhri safha phir wahi rehta; ADD na milein / ginti ajeeb (0.6x–2x) = purana 5 s ka chakkar. Video ka t=0 = clip ka t0 — ovRender ko `t0` zaroor do (rec = w.t0, clip = meta.t0). Raftar: apni recording ka intezar `REC_WAIT` 8 s (pehle 20) · `armDue` har agle tayyar bill ka timer (30 s ke chakkar ka intezar nahi) · 👥 group video line 'a' pick par hi (`gwAdd` → `gwSeen` dohra nahi) · `GW_GAP` 10 s. Sandbox: print se group video ~42 s (20 s post samet), safha 58 s par badla. Bill video ka naya kaam `dueOf` / `gwSeen` se — ek bill ki dohri video kabhi nahi. AI NAHI.
- **📞 Khaas contacts (20 Nov, md pkg v59 → v60 — EK DAFA, ONCE)** (malik: 'kuch contacts ki call par volume 20% · contacts main khud chunoon ga' · 'es droid ko auto lagao' · 'main call karun to bhi kam · WhatsApp par bhi · in numbers ki silent mein bhi call aaye volume full' — wahi contacts): `md/tools/khaasvol.py` = KV '📞 Khaas: ghanti full' (v59 wala WAHI GUID — silent / vibrate / DND mein bhi ghanti 100%, khatam = volume + mode + DND wapas) + KB '📞 Khaas: baat 20%' (CallActive in/out + WhatsApp 'ongoing' notification ka title = naam, VoiceCall 20%, khatam = wapas; `on` guard + BootTrigger). Contacts / naam KHALI (md/ PUBLIC — malik phone par khud chunta / NAAM1|NAAM2 ki jagah likhta). Har pkg update Overwrite by ID = dobara bheja to chune contacts mit jayenge — pkg.py `ONCE = {'60': [mkv, mkb]}`; agle pkg mein kabhi `macros` mein mat daalo. Malik ke chune data wala koi bhi phone macro isi ONCE tareeqe se. Asal phone par nahi azmaya (silent mein beech ghanti chalu hona, OnePlus alert slider, {vol_*} %, WhatsApp VoiceCall stream) — malik se pehli call ka nateeja poochho.
- **🌱 Sabzi photo = Claude 30 min + 📊 Sehat hisaab + 💧 Paani 9-9 (v2.335.0, md pkg v61, 20 Nov)** (malik: 'beej ki picture doon to Claude open ho jaye, baqi apps band · 30 minute tak · kaam mukammal kar ke picture wapis · paani / nuts kitna, aaj kal parso' · 'raat 9 ke baad paani ki zaroorat nahi'): app ☰ 📸 Sehat photo mein 🌱 Sabzi (AI) -> `nt_sehat?shOk=c:sabzi` -> phone `clOk` + `clEm` = SIRF Claude 30 min (raatband + Sehat lock mein `clx()` chhoot, cb.py ka apna zaroori waqt); ✅ Kaam = sirf hisaab. Hisaab = is phone ki IndexedDB `nt-sehat` (sehat.js `idb()` = sw.js `shDb` BILKUL ek jaisa) — naya sehat / aadat ka record isi log mein (Firestore nahi). Paani `PWIN` 9:00-20:59, lock `WIN` 11:00-20:59. Kisi 'band' macro mein ek app ki chhoot do to hamesha is shakal mein: chhoot ka flag (cx) default 0 = band — sharat chooke to kuch na khule. Chhoot ka waqt / din mein kitni dafa malik ke kehne par hi badlo.
- **⏸ 📸 Sehat lock RUKA (md pkg v62, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'paani wala apps band karne wala abhi pause kar do — main shop par nahi hoon'): msl pkg ke macros se bahar + `off` mein — apps band hona (paani / coffee / fruit / nuts sab) ruka, yaad ke popup wahi. Malik 'chalu karo' kahe tabhi wapas (msl macros mein + off se hatao). Malik 'X pause / ruk' kahe to sirf wohi macro jo asal kaam (yahan apps band) karta hai — saath wali yaad / doosre macro bina poochhe band mat karo. **▶ v63 (21 Nov raat, malik: 'paani wala on kar do')**: wapas CHALU — msl macros mein, off se hata. Malik 'paani wala' = poora 📸 Sehat lock (paani + coffee + fruit + nuts) — sirf ek cheez kehe tab alag karo. **⏸ v68 (22 Nov, malik: 'wo off kar do paani coffee wala')**: phir RUKA (msl bahar + off) — yaad ke popup wahi; chalu sirf malik ke kehne par.
- **⌨️ Purchase '📱 Mobile jaisa' = PC keyboard (v2.339.0, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'search mein ↑ ↓ · Enter = item neeche · Tab = naya item · Tab aage / Shift+Tab peeche · khane mein Enter = wapas search'): purchase.js `kbdPc()` + keydown (capture, v2.4.0 se pehle) — sirf asal keyboard wale PC / laptop par aur sirf jab POS table (.pp-posr) nahi; phone ka Enter = agla khana (v2.4.0) wahi. Khanon ki tarteeb `kbFields` (item ke khane, aakhir mein Goda); naya khana / button item mein jodo to socho Tab ki tarteeb mein aaye ya nahi. Purchase / Sale jaisi nayi PC screen banao to keyboard (↑ ↓ / Enter / Tab) ka yehi namoona; number khane mein ↑ ↓ se raqam kabhi na badle. Focus dobara lagane wala timer sirf tab chale jab focus gum ho — user aage nikal gaya ho to purane khane par wapas mat le jao (test mein pakra).

- **🕒 Is waqt ki entries (v2.340.0, waqt.js, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'us time ki entries ka data do to recover kar lein'): Deleted entries ke '🕒 Waqt se' / '🕒 Is waqt ki' chip -> kisi waqt ke aas paas bani / badli / hatai entries (sab khate) + hatai hui ↩ wapas (tick se, default khali). 'Khata bigar gaya / entries gayab' ki shikayat = pehle yeh screen + khata ⋮ 🗑 Din ki entries -> ⚖️ Barabar se bani dekhne ko kaho. Main yahan se malik ka asal data nahi dekh sakta — saaf batao, andaza nahi. Entry wapas lane ka naya raasta `trashRestoreOne` se (transfer / HBL jora / POS bill nahi).

- **↩ Entry wapas = POS mein dohra na bane (v2.341.0, waqt.js v1.1)**: hatai entry wapas lane par PC NAYA POS voucher banata hai — is liye pehle dekho us ka purana voucher POS mein abhi hai ya nahi (`posHas`, posLines): ho to wapas ROK (raasta = Farq screen '📱 App ko POS jaisa'). ⇄ Transfer wapas = naya transfer + note mein '↩<id>' nishan (`trDoneOf`). Entry wapas lane ka koi naya raasta banao to yehi do hifazatein.

- **📅 Tareekh / waqt ka khana likhte waqt dobara mat banao (v2.342.0, ghalti 21 Nov)**: PC par `<input type=date|time>` har hindse par `change` deta hai — us par poori khirki paint ki to saal '0020' reh jata hai aur focus toot-ta hai. Change par sirf LIST badlo (khana wahi rahe), saal adhoora (< 2020) ho to intezar. Naya date / time filter banao to PC keyboard se poori tareekh likh kar dekho.

- **📅 Khata tareekh-war (v2.343.0, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'is ko date wise karo'): khate ki tarteeb = entry ki TAREEKH, phir banne ka waqt (`khDated`) — `khOrdered` aur `runningOf` dono isi se (screen / PDF / parchi / baqaya ek jaisa). Khate ki koi nayi list / baqaya banao to `khDated` se; sirf createdAt (`newestEntries`) se mat karo (purani tareekh ki nayi entry upar aa jati hai).

- **👑 Malik ke devices (v2.344.0, malik-dev.js, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'malik ki bhi aik screen — konsi konsi device par hum login hain · total kitni devices · hataya ja sake · chips mulazim ka alehda, mera alehda'): Mulazim screen ke upar do chips `#mkdChips` (👑 Mere devices · 👥 Mulazim ke devices) -> apni `<dialog id=mkdDlg>` (tabs, kul ginti). Malik ke devices = `blueAccess/malikDevs {d:{<device id>:{nm, ua, at, tab, x}}}` (rules wahi — blueAccess malik ka) — har malik wala device khud darj hota hai (har 10 min, sirf SERVER ka data dekhne ke baad); ✕ = `x` -> wo device apni x dekh kar khud `controller.logout()` (band ho to agli dafa kholte hi), dobara login kare to x khud saaf. Yeh sirf app ka logout hai — code / password wala dobara aa sakta hai (malik ko bataya; pakka ilaaj code badalna). 'Yehi device' par ✕ nahi. Mulazim ka ✕ = WAHI `cloud.deleteStaffDevice`. Device / login ki koi nayi list isi data se; malik ke device ka x cache (purani yaad) dekh kar kabhi mat mitao.
- **🧾 Sale ki poori report (v2.345.0, sale-rep.js, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'itna bill, itna cancel, itna return, minus · poori screen · sale wale chip par click · with return aur without return — phir pata chale sahi kya hai'): Daily ke '➕ Sale+Wasooli+Kal' chip (`.dy-sr`, CAP `d.salerep` OPT_IN) -> `<dialog id=srDlg>` — data WAHI `posSales/<din>` {bills, sum} (PC ki koi file nahi badli, nayi SQL nahi): do nateeje (Return minus kar ke / Return ke baghair) + 'Galle mein hona' dono tarah + hisse (Kul bill · ⛔ Cancel · ↩ Return · ➖ bill ke andar minus · stock fraq) + 📄 PDF + 🖨 thermal (khataPrints k 'txt'). SIRF dikhana — Daily ka hisaab is se nahi badalta; auto sale ka qaida (return minus) badalna = malik ke alag OK par. Wapsi ke 'badle ka bill' sirf '≈ andaza' likh kar (PC yeh rishta nahi jaanta). Sale / wapsi / cancel ka naya dikhana isi screen mein. Sabaq: Daily mein ab gina hua closing cash nahi hai — 'Kharcha + Aaj change' ko 'gina hua cash' keh kar faisla mat sunao (21 Nov ko pehle aisa keh diya); 'Galle mein hona' MINUS aaye = aaya paisa kam likha hai.
- **🏦 Alfa popup ki likhai = pehle local variable (md pkg v64, 21 Nov)** (malik: 'SMS wala lagta hai, pop wala nahi' — Rs 100 Raast ka test): `alfa.py` pehle JavaAction se seedha `{notification}` / `{not_title}` maangta tha (azmaya hua nahi tha) — ab SetVariable se `bkT` / `bkX` (local), Java wahan se. Phone macro mein trigger ka magic text ({notification}, {not_title}, {sms_message}…) Java mein seedha mat maango — pehle SetVariable se local variable mein lo. Popup ki likhai (PKR 100.0 received … BAF A/C *0294 … Tx ID FT…) app / PC ke parser se theek parhi jati hai (jaancha); 'SAMIH ULLAH' (H ke saath) apne naam ke regex mein NAHI — aam gahak ki tarah lagta hai. Dohra nahi: Tx ID + jurwan (taraf + raqam ±30 min). Entry PC lagata hai — raat PC band ho to subah; app ki Bank screen mein baqi mein dikhe. Yeh ilaaj phone par azmaya nahi — malik se agla popup test poochho; na chale to agla shak = phone par link (bkU / bkK: malik login se app ek dafa khole) ya Alfa app ka package naam.
- **🧹 Phone update ginti ZERO kar deta tha (md pkg v65, 21 Nov — MERI GHALTI)** (malik: 'TikTok band karne wala macro kuch time ke baad 25 minute dobara chalu kar deta hai'): MacroDroid ka ImportMacros file ki `variables` list ko phone ki POORI global list ki jagah rakh deta hai (schema: 'global variables … applied as in a manual import') — Remote file mein sirf NAYE variable daalta tha, is liye HAR pkg update par sab ginti / link zero: tkMin / tkLock (TikTok), fb / yt, Sehat lock, pnCount, bkU / bkK (Alfa link — pop wali entry isi se rukti thi), nbU (nigrani), ntU / ntK, shK, jgList (jagah ke naam). Saboot: update ke baad wali email export mein har qeemat 0 aur jo naam sirf Java / magic text se chalte hain wo ek update chhor kar gayab. Ilaaj `remote.py` BUILD: aam update mein bhi backup ke MAUJOODA variables (qeemat samet) + naye. Remote 5 khud pkg mein hai (wahi GUID, har update apne aap ko badalta hai) — Remote ka naam `patch` mein KABHI nahi (patch phone ki purani copy ko wahi ID se package ke aakhir mein daal deta = naya code na lagta); 60 s ab remote.py mein. Email wali `nt-now.txt` mein variables ki likhai sirf lambai `<N>` (link / chaabi / jagah kabhi nahi), hindse rehte hain — fix ki jaanch = agle update ki export mein tkLeft / fbLeft ≠ 0. (v66 se jaancha ✅: 94 variable, qeemat waise hi.)
