QAIDA: phone par 'ginti / lock khud khul gaya / link gum' ki shikayat = pehle dekho us waqt koi pkg update gaya tha; naya 'yaad rakhne wala' phone kaam banao to update ke baad ki export se jaancho ke qeemat bachi. Ek din mein kam se kam update bhejo (har update chalte loop tor deta hai).
- **📸 Phone kholne par photo ka popup NAHI (md pkg v67, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'unlock karne par paani ki photo do — us ko khatam kar do'; teen raaston mein se 'sirf unlock wala popup' chuna): `sehat.py` 📸 Sehat lock se har-unlock wale dono popup hate ('⛔ Sehat lock' aur '📸 … ki photo baqi' — v58 ka qaida mansookh); lock shuru hote hi popup sirf jab phone istemal mein ho (`NOT_T4`, `shLkA` usi daur mein saaf). Photo ka raasta: band app kholne par popup · bell notification · Blue Khata ☰ 📸 Sehat photo. Paani / coffee ki YAAD ka popup (40 min / waqt par, phone band tha to kholne par ek dafa) wahi hai — wo bhi hatana ho to malik ke kehne par. Phone ke unlock par koi naya popup bina malik ke kahe mat lagao.
- **🏬 Godam = sab model (v2.346.0, rules 2.110, PC ginti-cam v2.0 + ginti-ai v1.0 + bill-clip v2.20, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'SB model lagao godam wla cameras me' · 'hd kr do'): lakeer wala camera ab carton (YOLOE) + 🧍 pose (ginti-pose.onnx — banda kuch utha kar guzra = po / pi, carton na dikhe tab bhi waqia) + 🙂 chehra (YuNet + SFace — gintiEv.fc). Har waqie ki video bill-clip khud banata hai (billClip/gv~<evId>, 2 camera = …b; 📹 0 = note bana, camera ne kuch nahi gina = gv~d-<doc>), SIRF apni recording se; godam camera ki recording HD (billClip.hdc / auto). App godam-v.js '🏬 Godam videos' (CAP nz.gvid OPT_IN): ✓ Sahi / 🔢 Ginti / ✕ Ghalat = gintiEv.lb (training ka data — malik ne training baad mein rakhi, 200-300 faisle ke baad; segment model nahi), ❓ chehre par naam (malik) = gintiEv.fl -> PC seekhta. Larkon ki tasveerein faceRef (chhota th) + faceImg (bari) SIRF malik + PC — kisi mulazim / WhatsApp / website ko KABHI nahi. Chehre wala koi naya kaam isi data se; chehre ka model commercial-OK license wala hi (YuNet MIT, SFace Apache). Chehra / pose AI = PC ka apna (internet / Gemini nahi). billClip ki koi nayi list / ginti banao to gv~ docs (x 1, gv 1) bill nahi hain. Asal camera par pehle hafte ✓ / ✕ dekh kar qaide (cos 0.40, carryOf) theek karo — andaze se nahi.
- **🤖 Camera AI = default BAND (v2.347.0, PC ginti-cam v2.1 + bill-clip v2.21, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'AI ko filhal band kar do, script mein on karne ka option'): switch `blueAccess/gintiCam.ai` (1 = chalu, na ho = band) — sirf app Settings → 🖥️ PC scripts → '🤖 Camera AI' (pc-drives.js #pcAi). Band = ginti-cam koi tukra / model / milan / alarm / chehra nahi, bill-clip auto HD + 🏬 waqia video nahi; recording / bill / gate pass / live wahi. Godam camera ka koi naya AI kaam isi `ai` switch ke peeche rakho (band ho to chup + haal 'AI band'); malik ke kahe baghair khud chalu mat karo. ginti setup ka save explicit fields likhta hai — `ai` us mein kabhi mat daalo (warna switch mit jaye).
- **🏬 Live: godam ke sab cameras ek video (v2.348.0, PC live-cam v1.9, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'live ma godam ka cameras akhetha chahie'): liveReq pc `@godam` -> PC EK ffmpeg (xstack: 2 aamne saamne · 3-4 = 2x2 · 5-6 = 3x2, 448x252 khana, 8 fps) — cameras: billClip.gc › gintiCam cams › xc naam mein 'godam' (max 6); har input recording wali udp copy se (forwarder 127.0.0.1:48900+), NVR par naya rabta nahi; jawab `mo` {c, r, n} -> app khane par naam + tap = wohi camera. Live mein kai camera ek saath ka naya kaam isi mosaic se (har camera ka alag WebRTC rabta mat banao — MAX_V 4 aur phone ka net). ffmpeg inputs ek ek khulte hain — probe chhota (1 s) rakho warna har camera par intezar barhta hai.
- **⚖️ Khate ke upar 'POS = App' (v2.348.0, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'har khate ke upar chip · aik click · aik hi voucher · alehda entries nahi · only malik'): pos-milan `lnFixHTML` — pm-line ke saath chip (sirf malik, farq par) -> ek confirm -> WAHI milanJobs ['@balance'] (EK JV) -> ⏳ / ✅ + ↩. Khate ko POS jaisa / POS ko khate jaisa karne ka naya button isi job se — naya raasta / alag entries wala tareeqa mat banao.
- **🖼 Chipki bill tasveer = pehle POORI (v2.349.0, 22 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: 'yahan zoom nahi, pic ki original picture aani chahiye — chahein to zoom kar sakein'): phone ke POS Purchase mein upar lagi bill tasveer pehle poori nazar aati hai (`billZoom` 0.5 = fit, `zDef()`; CSS `.pp-billbar[data-zoom="0.5"]`), + se bari (1 = poori chaurai, phir 1.5 … 3), − se wapas, ⤢ Poori screen wahi. PC (posMode) pehle jaisa (1 se shuru). Tasveer dikhane wali koi nayi jagah banao to pehle poori tasveer, zoom malik ke haath mein — khud kaat / zoom kar ke mat dikhao.
- **🧾 Purchase parchi par bill ki tasveer bhi (PC pos-khata-sync w7, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'is par bill ki photo bhi aani chahiye'): samaan ki tasveer ke neeche 'Bill ki tasveer:' = bill ki PEHLI tasveer, poori (crop 0, 480 dots tak). 12 Oct ka 'bill ki tasveer nahi' mansookh — wapas mat hatao. Parchi par nayi tasveer = `goodsPhoto(e, key)` + pn ka 3 min intezar; kaghaz ka kharcha pehle malik ko batao.

- **🔎 POS report se milao (v2.350.0, sale-rep.js v1.1, 22 Nov)** (malik ne POS Sale Register ki photo bheji: 'POS minus kar raha hai ke nahi'): 🧾 Sale ki poori report mein malik POS ka 'Total Sale' likhta hai -> app batati hai wo kis hisaab se bana (`milan`: bill · − wapsi · + fraq) aur bacha farq kis bill ka hai; minus total wale sale bill = ⚠ (wapsi do dafa). SIRF dikhana. POS ki report ka total aur app ka hisaab alag lagein to pehle yeh hissa bharwao — photo ke totals se andaza mat lagao (22 Nov: totals se tay nahi hua). Auto sale ka qaida (return minus) malik ke alag OK par hi badlo.

- **📸 Sehat lock = raat 8 baje ka hisaab (v2.351.0, md pkg v69, 22 Nov raat)** (malik: 'paani ki yaad 1 ghanta · on kar do · in par apps 8 baje · coffee 30 · fruit 20 · nuts 20 · paani hadaf 6 bottle, har kam bottle 15 min'): DIN MEIN APPS BAND NAHI — sirf yaad + photo (8 baje se pehle kisi bhi waqt). 20:00 par `HISAAB` (sehat.py, TimerTrigger + 20:00-20:59 ki pehli jaanch, din mein ek dafa `shHd`) -> jo reh gaya us ke minute `shLock` -> wohi chowkidar (raatband.PK list). 8 baje ke baad photo se lock nahi khulta; 🚨 10 min zaroori popup ke button se. Purana 10 min -> 10/30/60 lock HATA — wapas mat lao. Minute (`FINE`, `PER`), hadaf (`GOAL`), waqt malik ke kehne par hi badlo. Phone macro mein 'aaj' ki pehchan `{dayofmonth}` string se (din ka hisaab `dNum` gol ho sakta hai — us par lock ka waqt mat banao). Macro ka logic badlo to Python simulator (action list chala kar) se jaancho. Phone par azmaya nahi — pehli raat ka nateeja malik se poochho.

- **➕➖ Items se hisaab (v2.352.0, PC pos-sales-dekho v2.12, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'bill ke total ke bajaye plus wali all items ka data, minus wali alehda, udhaar bill bhi plus mein — phir pata chale kya sahi kya ghalat'): 🧾 Sale ki poori report ka hissa — data `posSales/<din>-it` (PC `itAgg`: har line Qty x Rate, cancel + stock fraq bahar; plus cash / udhaar · bill ke andar minus · wapsi ki items), purana din `blueAccess/saleItReq` (malik). Teen nateeje (sab kaat kar · sirf bill ka minus · sirf plus) + har ek ka 'galle mein hona'. SIRF dikhana — auto sale ka qaida malik ke alag OK par hi badlo. Sale ka item-war naya hisaab isi doc se (nayi SQL nahi); posSales mein '-it' wale doc bill ka din nahi hain. Lines ka jor aur bill total ka farq = discount / gol (alag line) — us ko ghalti mat kaho.
- **🎓 Video samjhao (v2.353.0, video-sam.js, 22 Nov)** (malik: 'YouTube videos mein Hindi alfaz samajh nahi aate · har 2-3 minute explain · audio bhi'): ☰ 🎓 (sirf malik) — YouTube link Gemini ko SEEDHA (file_data.file_uri, 15-15 min ke tukre), jawab r (Roman Urdu, screen) + u (Urdu harf, SIRF awaz ke liye — screen par Urdu harf kabhi nahi) + w (lafz = matlab); awaz = phone ki speechSynthesis. Kisi ki video download / repo (PUBLIC) mein upload KABHI nahi; kisi ki awaz ki naqal nahi. Video / lambi audio ka naya AI kaam isi file se (apna fetch, 4 min hadd — ai-tally ka 50 s kam hai). Malik ko video samjhao to Hindi lafz ki jagah aam Urdu lafz.
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.1 (v2.354.0, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'pehla part sunun, baqi peeche load · Raj aurat ki awaz, doctor mard · background mein chale · Gemini ki awaz · reader app'): guftagu `d` [[A|B, Roman, Urdu]] (A zanana 'Kore', B mardana 'Charon'), agle tukre khud (`MAXAUTO`), awaz Gemini TTS -> WAV -> `<audio>` + Media Session (background), audio IndexedDB `nt-vs`, na bane = phone ki awaz, 📤 Reader = share. Kisi ASAL insaan (video wale / kisi aur) ki awaz ki naqal KABHI nahi — sirf bani banai awazein; video ki lafz-ba-lafz transcript nahi (mukhtasar, un ke andaz mein). App mein background mein chalne wali awaz chahiye ho to speechSynthesis nahi (Android rok deta hai) — audio file + `<audio>` + Media Session. TTS model ka naam pakka mat likho — list se (naam badalte rehte hain).
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.2 (v2.355.0, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik ki screenshot: har line 'Raj:', jumle English, ek hi awaz · 'auto banta rahe' · 'chalti line yellow'): video English thi aur prompt sirf Hindi lafz badalne ko kehta tha. AI se tarjuma maango to saaf likho 'har jumle ka tarjuma' + misaal, aur jawab ko JAANCHO (`badOut`: English / ghair-Latin / sab ek naam = ek dafa dobara) — AI ke jawab par aankh band kar ke bharosa nahi. Bolne wala NAAM se maango (A / B jaisa code AI sab par ek hi laga deta hai). Lamba kaam (poori video) khud chale + ghalti par khud dobara — malik se baar baar button mat dabwao. Sunte waqt jagah dikhao (peeli line). Naya AI feature asal jawab ki screenshot dekhe baghair 'ban gaya' mat samjho.
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.3 (v2.356.0, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik: 'yeh bhi video 15 minute ki hi hai, is se aage ki nahi'): AI ko 15 min ka tukda dete hain aur wo tukre ke khatam ko 'video khatam' (`end` 1) keh deta tha -> app ruk jati. Ab `end` sirf tab: tukra khali, ya aakhri line tukre ke khatam se 90 s pehle ruke; purani ruki video par chip '▶ Video aage bhi hai'. AI ko hissa de kar us se 'poori cheez khatam hui?' mat poochho — us ka jawab hisse ke baare mein hota hai; aisa faisla app apne hisaab se kare. Asal Gemini par azmaya nahi (malik ki screenshot se jaancho). **🔊 Awaz (isi update mein)** (malik: 'Gemini wali pehle sahi thi, ab ek hi hai · ⋮ mein awaz chun-ne ka option, Raj / doctor ke aage, larki larka, 3-4 awazein'): v1.2 mein do bolne wale pehchane gaye to do-awaz wali darkhwast 400 ('speech_metadata.speaker for each text part') -> phone ki ek awaz. Ab `ttsBody` har line alag part + `speechMetadata.speaker` (Gemini 3.x; chali shakal 'sam-vs-ttsf' mein yaad), na chale = purani shakal, phir har bolne wale ki baat ek-awaz wale raaste se + PCM jor (`pcmOf`). ⋮ -> 🔊 Awaz chuno (`VOICES` 2 larki + 2 larka, 'sam-vs-voice', dabao = chun li + namoona; audio ki key mein awaz). AI ka koi naya raasta (naya request format / model) lagao to purana chalta raasta fallback mein rakho — 'pehle chalta tha' wali cheez kabhi poori band na ho. Awaz hamesha bani banai (kisi asal insaan ki naqal nahi).
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.4 — awaz poore page ki EK (v2.358.0, 23 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: 'Gemini ki awaz nahi bani (Google ki awaz ki hadd poori)' · 'ek hi dafa, hisson ki bajaye poora page ban jaye · har hissa alag alag ka kya faida · paison wala bhi use kiya ja sakta hai'): app poori video ke har 1-2 min ke hisse ki awaz pehle se banwa rahi thi (1 ghanta = ~50 darkhwastein) -> Google ki hadd. Ab `groupsOf`: AI ke ek jawab (15 min ka page, part `b`) ki EK awaz, har hissa usi awaz ka tukda (`f0`–`f1`, likhai ki lambai se andaza — peeli line / hisse par tap wahi), sirf chalta + agla page (`AHEAD` 1), ek waqt ek darkhwast, 429 = 65 s ruk kar dobara (3 dafa = phone ki awaz), Google lamba page na de = khud aadha (`S.cap`). AI se awaz / tasveer jaisi mehngi cheez kabhi 'sab pehle se' mat banwao — sirf jo abhi chahiye + thora aage; hadd (429) par foran band mat karo, ruk kar dobara. Billing malik ka faisla (Google AI Studio) — app mein kuch nahi badalta. `export const _t = { fn }` mein baad mein bane const ka seedha naam mat likho (TDZ = poori file load nahi hoti; `node --check` nahi pakarta) — file ko node se IMPORT kar ke dekho. Asal Gemini par azmaya nahi.

Kharcha / bank / band / baqaya 0 wale kabhi nahi; raqam PAISA. Khaton ki koi nayi list-print / PDF isi `rowsOf` se (naya raasta / collection nahi).
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.5 — 9 kaam (v2.359.0, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'sab bana do'): wahin se shuru (`sam-vs-pos` / `sam-vs-last`) · 😴 timer · ⭐ line par tap (`sam-vs-star`) · 📖 lafz (`sam-vs-words`) · 📄 PDF (`A.pdf`) · YouTube Share -> Blue Khata (manifest `share_target` GET -> app.js `vsGo`, `A.share`) · 📌 Khulasa (khatam par khud) · ✅ Kaam ki baatein · ❓ Sawal. AI sirf aakhri teen mein aur sirf LIKHAI (`txOf`) se — video dobara mat bhejo (mehenga). Naya kaam ⋮ (`menuHTML`) mein, card guftagu ke NEECHE (`extraHTML`) — upar nayi qatar nahi. paint() poori khirki dobara banata hai: likhne wala khana ho to us ki likhai + focus bachao (`vsQ` namoona). Share target ka GET form action ki query MITA deta hai — pehchan sirf `text` / `url` param se karo.
- **🏦 Alfa notification SAADA (md pkg v70, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik: 'Alfalah wala nahi chal raha' — Rs 100 Raast 13:43, Bank screen ki 'aakhri notification' 11:57 hi rahi = phone se aaya hi nahi; is transfer ka Alfalah SMS aata hi nahi, sirf popup): `alfa.py` ab HBL wale popup macro (jo chalta hai) jaisa — Alfa ka HAR popup pakro, likhai ki jaanch macro ke andar (BAF + PKR; OTP / PIN / passw / code nahi), seedha HttpRequest (`{v=bkU}` / `{v=bkK}`); 2xx na aaye = purani Java line (bkQ, 5 min). Ginti `bkN` (len-den popup dekha) · `bkS` (seedha gaya) · `bkC` (aakhri code) — phone ki report (NT phone macros) mein hindse nazar aate hain: masla ho to pehle yeh dekho. Phone ka koi naya 'bhejne wala' macro banao to pehle se chalte macro ki shakal (trigger -> HttpRequest) lo aur ginti ke hindse rakho — azmaye baghair Java par mat tiko; malik ke 'nahi chala' par pehle app ki screen (aakhri notification ka waqt) dekho. Phone report mein 30+ harf wali chaabi '***' ban kar lambai 3 dikhti hai (bkK '<3>' = chaabi maujood). Phone par azmaya nahi — agla Rs 100 test malik se.
- **0 Sab zero (v2.357.0, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'Rack bharo par ek chip — Sab zero, dabayein sab zero ho jaye'): `.rk2-zero` chip 'Sab items' ke saath usi qatar mein (alag qatar nahi) -> `rkZero()` list ki har item + har packet ki tadad 0 (`rk.sel` / `rk.pQ`), dobara dabao '↺ Wapas' = pehle wali tadad (`rk.z0`). Sirf screen ki tadad — stock / POS / qaide par asar nahi.
- **🧠 Ek macro band = us par tika doosra bhi band (md pkg v73, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik: 'Paani wale ke saath recording wala pop bhi band kar diya'): 💧 Paani band hua to `pnDue` ruk gaya aur Falsafa + 'Sona hai?' popup ki shart hamesha jhooti. Ab `qaide.py` `PAANI_ON` (False = Paani ki sharten nahi) — Paani / Coffee dobara chalu karo to True. Phone ka koi macro `off` karne se pehle grep karo us ke global variables (`pnDue`, `kpPend`…) kaun se doosre macro parhte hain, aur malik ko batao kya kya saath rukega.
- **🎛 Sab cameras ek screen (v2.360.0, PC live-cam v2.0 + pos-sales-dekho v2.15, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'aik screen jis par sab cameras aik saath · gate pass bane to godam wali par gate pass, bill wali par bill ki screen live — aik hi screen ke andar'): Live ka chip '🎛 Sab cameras' (CAP `nz.sab` OPT_IN) -> liveReq pc `@sab` -> PC EK ffmpeg (counter -> godam -> mazeed, 9 tak; 7-9 = 3x3, khana 384x216, 6 fps; port 48920+ / 48930+; recording wali udp copy — NVR par naya rabta nahi) + jawab `mo` {n, k:[c|g|x], g:[godam branch], tw, th}. Bill / gate pass video par NAHI likhe jate — app har khane ke upar dikhati hai (live.js `tbSync` / `tbPaint`, `.lv-tb`: sirf kinara + kaale kinare wali likhai): counter = posLive + POS screen ki tasveer (`tbFollow` aakhri item), godam = WAHI gatePass data (`tbGate`: sab ✓ ke 20 s baad / 10 min baad hat-ta; godam = mo.g warna camera ke naam ka hindsa). Tap = wohi camera akela (‹ 🎛 Sab wapas). pos-sales-dekho `@sab` ke counter mo.k se leta hai. Kai camera ek saath ka naya kaam isi mosaic (`startMosaic(codec, kind)`) se; kisi khane par naya data = `tbPaint` mein nayi qism (naya listener sirf jab khana ho). Asal NVR par azmaya nahi (ffmpeg 3x3 filter + tile logic node test). AI nahi.
- **⚡ Sab cameras tez (v2.361.0, PC live-cam v2.1, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'speed tez ka rasta nikalo · PC par bojh na pare'): der ki wajah = ffmpeg har input ek ek khol kar 1-2 s data ka intezar karta tha. Ab har recording wali line ke aakhri ~3.5 s PC yaad rakhta hai (`ringAdd`, aakhri keyframe se `ringHead`) aur mosaic ke input TCP (127.0.0.1, `moTcp`) — ffmpeg jure to pehle yaad wala hissa, phir chalti line. Namoona test: 6 camera 20.7 s -> 0.6 s, 8 camera 0.7 s. Video na aaye = khud purana UDP tareeqa (`MO_FASTBAD`). Mosaic 5 min garam (pehle 2); app pichhli dafa ka chip yaad rakhti hai (`sam-lv-last` — Sab cameras tha to khirki khulte hi wohi). 'Hamesha chalu' (dukan ke waqt) malik ne nahi chuna — PC ka bojh; kehne par hi. Live / clip ka koi naya ffmpeg input recording ki line se lena ho to isi ring + TCP se (probe chhota karne se nahi — purana qaida). Asal NVR par azmaya nahi.
- **⚡ Bills screen aur tez (v2.362.0, PC pos-sales-dekho v2.16, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'is ko aur tez kar do, jitna kar sakte ho'): PC ki jaanch har 0.15 s (pehle 0.5; sawal sasta COUNT / MAX) · naya / badla bill pehle CHHOTE doc `posSales/<din>-f` {d, at, b: aakhri 5 s ke badle bill} mein, poori list (`posSales/<din>`) apni line mein saath — dono likhai ki line 'ek waqt ek, aakhri wala' (`fastPut` / `bigPut`), jaanch kabhi likhai ka intezar nahi karti. App `cloud.listenPosSales` dono ko milata hai (tez doc sirf jab us ka `at` poori list se naya ho) — bills ka naya parhne wala isi se. posSales mein `-f` / `-it` wale doc din ke bill nahi hain (field `d`, `day` nahi — `day` wali query mein na aayein). Bills ki list `nbH()` se: likhai na badle to DOM nahi chhoota. Hadd: internet ka aana-jana (0.3-0.5 s) baqi hai — us se tez sirf LAN ki seedhi line (malik ke kehne par). Nakli Firestore test PASS; asal PC par azmaya nahi — ⏱ Raftar ki photo se jaancho.
- **🎛 Sab cameras: naam + khali screen (v2.363.0, PC live-cam v2.2, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik ki screenshot: khanon par 'Abdurehman' / 'Bilal (server)', items nahi · 'larke ki PC screen live aani chahiye'): khane ka naam camera setting ki PURANI chaabi tha, app `posLive/<naam>` us naam se sunti thi = kuch nahi aata. Ab `allList` naam `PN.nowName()` se (counter ka aaj ka naam). App: POS screen chal rahi ho = '⏳ bill ban raha' + screen ki tasveer; screen khali (bill chhap chuka — `tbFollow` sach) = bill number + aakhri 5 items. Counter ka naam kisi bhi nayi jagah app ko bhejo to `PN.nowName` / `PN.names()` se — camera setting (`CF.ch`) ki chaabi seedha kabhi nahi (purane insaani naam hote hain). Asal PC par azmaya nahi.

Ab naqshe mein na ho to `CAPS` se khud dhoondta hai. Naya CAP naye prefix ke saath banao to mulazim ke FORM se Save kar ke dekho (sirf card ke chip se nahi).
- **🧾 Sale ki report: 📦 Wholesale hissa + ➕➖ Items se hisaab CHHUPA (v2.365.0, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'wholesale ke bill ko jama kar ke bata do' · 'stock fraq ko add nahi karna' · 'plus minus nazar hi nahi aana chahiye'): sale-rep.js `sec('ws')` = wholesale bill ki ginti + kul + cash mila + list (Kul bill mein pehle se shamil — dobara jama nahi); PDF + thermal mein bhi. `IT_ON=false` = ➕➖ Items se hisaab screen / PDF / thermal se hata (data / PC wahi) — wapas mat lao jab tak malik na kahe. Stock fraq kisi total mein KABHI nahi jorna (sirf alag hissa). - **🎛 Sab cameras: counter ke khane par kuch na aaye = PC ka naam jura nahi (v2.366.0, 23 Nov)** (malik ki photo: khana 'KEIME1D' khali, upar chip 'RN6QU6D' alag): camera setting ki purani chaabi ('Abdurehman' = purana PC KEIME1D) ka aaj ka PC RN6QU6D hai magar 🔗 (blueAccess/pcAlias) nahi juda — bill / POS screen `posLive/RN6QU6D` mein jati hai, khana KEIME1D sunta hai. Yeh rishta code andaze se nahi jor sakta (qaida) — malik Bill tally ⋮ ⚙️ Camera setup mein ⚪ qatar par 🔗. App ab 12 s baad khane par saaf likhti hai (khamosh nahi). Live / tally mein 'counter ka data nahi aa raha' = pehle upar ke chips dekho: ek hi counter do naam se (purana + naya) = 🔗 baqi hai.
- **⏸ 📸 Sehat lock RUKA (md pkg v71, 23 Nov raat 9:08)** (malik: 'wo paani wala droid band kar do abhi'): msl pkg ke macros se bahar + `off` mein (8 baje ke hisaab wala lock + apps band hona ruka); 💧 Paani / ☕ Coffee ki yaad ke popup wahi. Chalu sirf malik ke kehne par (msl macros mein wapas + off se hatao). `off` list ab 5 (hadd) — agla macro band karna ho to pehle purana `off` se nikaalo.

- **⏸ 💧 Paani + ☕ Coffee ki yaad bhi RUKI (md pkg v72, 23 Nov raat)** (malik: 'wo bhi band'): `mp` / `mk` pkg ke macros se bahar + `off` mein — paani / coffee / fruit / nuts ke popup + Paani popup ke andar wala 📱 screen time bhi ruka; 📸 Sehat lock pehle se ruka (v71). Chalu sirf malik ke kehne par (mp, mk macros mein wapas + off se hatao; Sehat lock alag poochho). `off` ki hadd 5: '🧘 10 min break' aur '⛔ Subah 11 tak' list se nikle — dono pkg mein nahi aur phone par pehle se band hain (Remote sirf `off` wale band karta hai, kisi ko khud chalu nahi karta); in ko pkg `macros` mein wapas kabhi mat daalo. Phone par azmaya nahi.

- **📼 Sab cameras ki recording -> WhatsApp group (v2.367.0, PC live-cam v2.3 + bill-clip v2.22 / kh-wa v1.16 / wa-out v1.34, 23 Nov raat)** (malik: 'recording ko PC mein save karne ki zaroorat nahi — us ki jagah sab cameras ki live screen record karta rahe · un ki recording ek naye group mein jo hum select karein · lagatar · ek hi screen mein sab cameras'): `blueAccess/sabRec` {on, g, gn, kd, st, end} (Bill tally ⋮ 👥 Group videos ke neeche '📼 Sab cameras ki recording', sirf malik) -> live-cam `srStart` EK ffmpeg (🎛 wali mosaic, recording wali line ki copy se — NVR par naya rabta nahi; line na ho = khali khana, jagah pakki) -> `sab-rec\sab-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.mp4` 5-5 min (crf 30, maxrate 380k = 16 MB ke andar, faststart) -> band tukra `wa-media\bv-sab-*.mp4` + waOut {grp, vf} (wa-out ka purana group raasta) · PC par `kd` din · haal `liveCamPc.sr`. ON karte waqt app `billClip.rkd` 0.125 likhti hai = har camera ki ALAG recording sirf 3 ghante (1 se kam = ghante, kam az kam 3; bill / gate pass / waqt ki video isi se banti hain — is se kam / band KABHI nahi). Camera ki lambi recording ka naya kaam isi sab-rec se; har camera ki alag video group mein mat bhejo (roz 1000+ = number ban). Mit chuki alag recording wapas nahi aati — malik ne OK diya (confirm bhi hai). ffmpeg ko band karna = stdin 'q' (kill = adhoori mp4; adhoori file `srOkMp4` se pakri jati hai, group mein nahi jati). Sandbox: ffmpeg TCP mpegts -> segment + 'q' PASS; asal NVR / WhatsApp par azmaya nahi — PC ka bojh (9 camera decode) 🔥 dabbe se dekhna. AI nahi.
- **🎛 Live dabbe screen ke hisaab se (v2.368.0, 23 Nov)** (malik: 'dabbon ka size screen ke mutabiq · upar neeche aur chhota bara apni marzi se'): mosaic (🎛 Sab / 🏬 Godam) ab app mein har camera ka ALAG dabba — live.js `TL`: canvas har 100 ms PC ki ek video ke us hisse se (`tlDraw`, mo.c / mo.r se), video neeche chalti (chhupao / display:none mat karo — Chrome chhupi muted video rok sakta hai). ▦ chip = Auto · 1 · 2 · 3 ('sam-lv-cols', `tlLayout`), ½ s daba kar ▲ ▼ ✓ ('sam-lv-ord:<mode>', DOM node khiskao — dobara paint nahi, warna bill ki patti mit-ti). Dabbe ka naya kaam (naam / patti / tap) isi `.lv-tlg>.lv-mot` mein; `data-tb` = mosaic ka asal index (dikhane ki tarteeb nahi). PC ko alag stream mat maango (ek ffmpeg wala qaida).
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.6 (v2.368.0, 23 Nov — ghalti)** (malik: 'naya link paste karein to time bhi khud aaye · purana save hi rahe'): link likhte hi `urlAuto` (?t= / pehle samjhi = khud khule + khana khali = aage se / nayi = 0:00). Pehle 🎬 waqt badalne par purani guftagu MITA deta tha — ab `mergeIn`. Saved / bani hui cheez ko kabhi `parts = []` se khali mat karo; naya data hamesha purane mein joro. localStorage ki save ka quota bhar jaye to chup-chaap poori fail hoti thi — `store` ab purani hata kar dobara.
- **🎓 Video samjhao v1.7 — awaz kabhi na ruke (v2.369.0, 24 Nov)** (malik: 'baar baar ruk jati hai — aankhein band, khol kar dabana parta hai · auto kar do'): intezar (agla page / Google ki hadd / AI ka agla hissa) mein WAHI `<audio>` ek na sunai dene wali dhun chalata rehta hai (`keep`, 22 Hz 12 s loop) — awaz chalane wale kisi bhi web kaam mein beech ka khamosh waqfa = Android page sula deta hai aur baad ka play() mana; intezar mein element kabhi pause mat karo. Screen band (`hid()`) par speechSynthesis kabhi mat chalao (atak jata hai) — intezar + screen khulte hi usi line se (`onVis`); phone ki awaz ke waqt wake lock. Google 429 ko foran 'band' mat samjho: minute wali hadd = ruk kar khud dobara, sirf 'per day' = band. play() ke `.catch` mein AbortError (naya src) ko 'ruka' mat samjho.
- **📼 Group recording = phone khara wali shakal (PC live-cam v2.4, 24 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: video beech mein patli patti, baqi screen kaali): WhatsApp par jane wali kai-camera video hamesha phone KHARA rakh kar dekhne ke hisaab se — 7 tak ek column (`srGrid`), 8-9 do column, khali khana nahi; har khane par camera ka naam (`srLab`, drawtext + `srNoTxt` fallback). Chauri (3x2 / 3x3) shakal sirf app ke Live screen ke liye (wahan ▦ hai). Malik ne Video samjhao ka '📖 Poori baat' (tafseel) plan NAHI chuna — mat banao jab tak na kahe.

- **🧾 Live tile = scan ke naam + 🖨 bill chips + ⏰ Ruke bills (v2.370.0, PC nt-print v1.36 / pos-sales-dekho v2.17, 24 Nov)** (malik: 'pehle bill bane items ke names · phir number + amount side par · post = ghayab · 30 min post na ho = video ke saath alag screen'): POS bante bill ki item SQL mein print se pehle NAHI likhta — bante bill ke naam ka raasta SIRF scanner: NT-PRINT `scanLive` -> posLive/<naam>__scan (sirf jab _want mein wohi counter) -> server `scanNames` (sn) -> app tile. Counter ka naya 'live' kaam bhi isi tarah: sirf dekhte waqt likhai, naam server se (Nigrani posStock nahi parh sakta). Chhape magar post na hue bill = posSales `rkUnp` (ct > 0, x / ps 2 / ps 3 nahi) — Live screen par bill ki koi nayi list isi se. ⏰ Ruke = 30 min (`RK_MIN`) malik ke kehne par hi badlo. Purana PC hatana ho (KEIME1D) to pc-names LEGACY + tally.js LEG dono mein us ka naam naye host par.

- **🩺 Live tile khamosh nahi (v2.371.0, nt-print v1.37, 24 Nov)** (malik ki photo: bill chips aaye, items nahi — 'dekh kar batao kahan issue'): counter khane par items na hon to peeli `.lv-tbd` line (scan ginti · aakhri scan · aakhri print · PC ghari farq). Live par koi naya data dikhao to 'kuch nahi aaya' ki haalat mein bhi wajah ki line do — malik ki agli photo se pakka karo, andaze se 'theek kar diya' mat kaho. Do waqt milane hon (counter ka scan vs POS ka print) to ghari ka farq bhi dikhao; bill khatam = sirf print waqt (ct), post waqt (pt) kabhi nahi.

- **⚡ WhatsApp DO LINES (v2.372.0, PC wa-out v1.35, 24 Nov)** (malik: gate pass ka jawab 2-3 min — 'kuch seconds mein la aao'): wa-out mein 't' tez line (sirf LIKHAI: gate pass + jawab, malik / Mubeen / jure number) aur 'm' line (video / tasveer / group / gahak) saath chalti hain — kisi bhi bari upload (video / PDF) ko tez likhai ke saath EK qatar mein kabhi mat daalo. Video ka waqt naap se (90 s + 30 s fi MB); waqt khatam = dobara NAHI (peeche upload chal rahi ho sakti = dohri video + internet do mein), der se pohanche to 'sent', aur tab tak agli video nahi. 📼 sab cameras (bvs) line mein sab se peeche. waOut ka naya paigham banane wala raasta ho to 'gone' / 'inF' ka khayal (der se aaya snapshot dobara na bheje). 'Video nahi aayi' ki shikayat = pehle 👥 Group videos ka 📼 haal + '📲 WhatsApp' line (vq / vl) dekhne ko kaho; group list mein group na ho = Business number (wahin likha) group mein nahi.

- **🏠 Nigrani = MENU + 📊 PC ka bojh (v2.373.0, PC doctor v3.14 + wa-out v1.36, 24 Nov)** (malik: 'monitoring ki zaroorat nahi — off ka option · screen khule to seedha menu' · 'PC ka mukammal data usage, CPU, RAM — chart — bojh kam ho'): Nigrani screen har dafa MENU se khulti hai (tally.js `home`, `homeCards`) — tally sirf 👀 card se, back = menu; Nigrani ka naya kaam = menu mein naya card (saamne tally jaisa bara view nahi). 👀 tally BAND (sam-bt-off, har phone) = clips / tally ke listener band (`stopT`). PC ka 24 ghante ka hisaab = blueAccess/pcLoad (doctor `loadAdd`, har 5 min); naya 'PC ka bojh' naap isi doc / isi PowerShell mein jodo (alag har-minute process nahi). Network ka kul hai, har program ka internet Windows nahi deta — app mein 'download mein cameras ka LAN bhi' likha rehe. App mein PC ke kaam ka naya naam = pc-load.js `NAMES` / `tipOf`.
- **🎞 Grey camera = poori tasveer se jurna + beech se na girana (v2.374.0, PC live-cam v2.6, 24 Nov)** (malik ki screenshots: 🎛 Sab cameras grey + WhatsApp wali 📼 video bhi grey — 'cwo cbi ok h'): recording ki line ki yaad (ring) ab AAKHRI keyframe se (naya keyframe = purana hissa hata, 20 s / 10 MB tak; video PID PMT se), har naya dekhne wala ffmpeg `joinW` = poori tasveer se (yaad na ho to agli poori tasveer ka intezar); kisi ffmpeg ka dabba bhare to `relay` tukre BEECH SE nahi girata — `_skip` agli poori tasveer tak (grey ki jagah pal bhar ruki tasveer). 30 s koi keyframe na dikhe (`kOk`) = purana tareeqa (kabhi kaali screen nahi). Recording ki line se video lene wala koi bhi naya kaam (live / AI / clip) `joinW` + `relay` se — line mein seedha `w.write(m)` mat likho. 🎛 / 🏬 / 📼 ke inputs `-skip_loop_filter all` (CPU kam). 🩺 mosaic ka haal bhi app ko + `q.kf` (camera ki poori tasveer har N s) — app chip ki baat mein N > 4 = NVR mein I-frame interval 2 s + Smart Codec / H.264+ band ka mashwara. Ab bhi grey = pehle 🩺 chip dekho (NVR→PC / PC ke andar / phone net). Netflix jaisa (phone par 2-3 s ka bafar) agla qadam — sirf malik ke kehne par. Sandbox: 6 s GOP par beech se jurna 246 decode ghaltiyan -> 0, dabba bharna 5 -> 0. Asal NVR par azmaya nahi.
- **🧾 Live counter khana: scan na ho to PEHLE jaisa + 🩺 scan system ka haal (v2.375.0, PC nt-print v1.38 / nt-scan v1.6, 24 Nov — GHALTI)** (malik ki photo: 'Live NT print ka data nahi aa raha' — teeno counter par '30 min mein scan 0 · kul ginti 0', na POS screen na aakhri bill): v2.370 / v2.371 mein scan doc maujood hote hi khane par SIRF scan wala hissa dikhta tha — scan khali = baqi sab (POS screen ki tasveer / aakhri bill ki items) chhup jata. Ab EK counter branch: scan ke naam hon to wohi, warna pehle jaisa + neeche chhoti 🩺 line; 🖨 bill chips har counter par. Naya hissa kisi purane dikhawe ke UPAR lagao to us ke khali hone par purana raasta zaroor bacha rahe (fallback) — naya feature purani dikhti cheez ko kabhi chhupaye nahi. Sab counter par 'sahi scan 0' = NT-PRINT ka scan system scan pakar hi nahi raha (andaza: hook / window / Chrome) — v1.38 doc mein rd (READY) · al (chal raha) · rw (kitni pakri) · sp (Chrome mein) · w (ghalat window) · er · fg; app 🩺 line par. Malik ki agli photo se wajah pakki karo, andaze se theek mat karo.
- **📶 PC ka network = asal naap + Wi-Fi / LAN (v2.376.0, PC doctor v3.15, 24 Nov — GHALTI)** (malik ki photo: 📊 PC ka bojh mein '↑ 0 KB · ↓ 0 KB' — aur bill ki video bhi grey, 'subah theek tha'): v3.14 ka Get-NetAdapter -Physical | Get-NetAdapterStatistics is PC par khali deta tha aur app '-1' ko '0 KB' dikhati thi (khamosh ghalti). Ab .NET NetworkInterface (GetIPv4Statistics, Up + virtual / VPN / Bluetooth bahar, har interface apne try mein) + pcLoad.nic (kaun sa network: Wireless80211 / Ethernet + link Mbps). App: naap na ho = saaf 'naap abhi nahi'; 🔌 LAN taar / 📶 Wi-Fi + Mbps; sirf Wi-Fi = warning (NVR video + WhatsApp uploads ek Wi-Fi par = rush mein grey). doctor ki CPU list ki hifazat: PowerShell ke network hisse se output khali aaye to 2 dafa ke baad network hissa band (CPU.noNet) — naap ka naya hissa kabhi CPU / zaroori list ko na rok sake. Naap ka 'nahi mila' kabhi 0 mat dikhao. Grey camera: bill ki video (recording file) bhi grey = masla NVR -> PC (RTSP) ya PC ka bojh, live-cam ka relay nahi; pehle 📊 network + Wi-Fi / LAN dekho. PowerShell yahan azmaya nahi ja saka (parser check hath se) — malik ki agli photo se jaancho.
- **🎞 Grey camera ka sabaq (24 Nov 2026 shaam — GHALTI, malik: 'problem abhi bhi wohi hai')**: maine teen update (relay / scan / network naap) bheje aur malik ne har ek ko ilaaj samjha — grey wahi raha. Bill ki video (PC ki apni recording) bhi grey ho = video NVR se PC tak aate hue hi tooti hai; app / live-cam ke code mein ilaaj mat dhoondo. Pehle 2 minute ka pakka test: (1) DMSS mein wohi camera — wahan bhi grey = camera / NVR, saaf = PC tak ka network; (2) 👥 Group videos + 📼 Sab cameras 15 min band — grey gaya = uploads network gher rahe hain. Sirf naap / jaanch wala update ho to malik ko PEHLI line mein likho 'yeh ilaaj nahi, sirf jaanch hai'. Server PC ki log files yahan se kholne ki ijazat (C:\khata-sync) nahi milti — andaza saaf andaza kaho.
- **📼 / ⏪ = PC ki RECORDING files se (v2.377.0, PC live-cam v2.7, 24 Nov shaam)** (malik: 'issue sirf live par aata hai · DMSS par issue nahi · 11 baje ke baad se · PC khud se live banaye jo kuch minute peeche chale to shayad problem na aaye' — OK): bill ki video (recording files) saaf thi, grey sirf usi second wale kaam (Live + 📼) mein. (1) 📼 group video ab live line se NAHI — `srMake` har 5 min ka tukra band 1-min recording tukron (`recSegs`, rec-dir) se 2-7 min BAAD (concat + `duration` = recording ka waqfa ruki tasveer, `tpad` = camera der se shuru, recording na ho = khali khana; `os.setPriority` below normal, -threads 2, tmp mein ban kar rename). (2) ⏪ Saaf live: app chip `data-lv-dl` ('sam-lv-dl') -> liveReq pc '<naam>~' -> `startDelayed`: har camera ka feeder (ffmpeg -c copy mp4 -> mpegts, BINA -re) + Node ghari `dlPump` (DTS / PTS se tasveer-war, agla tukra pehle se line mein, mpegts ki ginti (CC) Node khud likhta — warna har tukre par 'Packet corrupt'), TCP 127.0.0.1 -> wahi mosaic encoder. ⏪ mein bill / gate pass ki patti NAHI (2 min purani video par abhi ka bill ghalat). (3) `-skip_loop_filter all` HATA — H.264 ki reference tasveer bina deblock = har GOP mein dhabbe barhte; decoder ki 'tez' setting jo tasveer badle wapas mat lagao. Camera / live ka koi naya kaam jahan der chal sakti ho = recording files se (NVR / live line se nahi). Sandbox: 3 camera 5 min 14 s mein bani, sync sahi; feeder 50 s, sab se bara waqfa 0.085 s; asal PC par azmaya nahi.

- **🎥 Live grey = pehle Main / Sub stream dekho (24 Nov raat, server PC ka log — malik ne `C:\khata-sync` folder Claude app mein khud joda)**: recording har camera ki **Main stream (HD)** par thi (⚙️ Camera setup chip 'Main stream', `CF.sub` 0) aur live / 🎛 usi ki copy decode karta hai — 🩺 mein 'PC ke andar gire' hazaron fi 10 s (6:48–6:59) = PC paanch HD camera decode nahi kar pa raha (network ka masla nahi; Godam 1 ghee H.265 aur bhaari). Malik ko mashwara: 'Sub stream (halki)' (bill video waise bhi 640 px). Raat 10:11–10:34 paanchon channel EK saath -138 (waqt khatam) = NVR / network — us waqt recording nahi bani, is liye ⏪ Saaf khali (code null). ⏪ feeder (mp4 -> mpegts `-c copy`) asal recording ke HAR tukre par 'Error muxing a packet' deta hai — sandbox ki testsrc file par nahi aaya: recording file ka naya remux / copy kaam ASAL recording file par jaancho (recording `D:\NT-Recording`, alag folder). Server ka log dekhna ho to malik se kaho Claude app mein `+` -> Add folder -> `C:\khata-sync` (tool ki folder darkhwast classifier rok deta hai). Grey ki shikayat par pehle yeh log padho, phir code badlo.

- **⚡ Tez raasta khud band na rahe (PC live-cam v2.8, 24 Nov raat — GHALTI)** (malik: 'kuch problems abhi bhi hain'): 10:34 par recording band thi, 🎛 ka ek input RTSP par gaya, us ki -138 ghalti par `MO_FASTBAD` HAMESHA ke liye laga — poori raat 🎛 do UDP hop par chala (log mein 'ch4/rec', 'rec⚡' nahi). Ab tez raasta sirf tab band jab wohi laga ho aur ghalti RTSP ki na ho, aur 10 min baad khud dobara. Koi bhi 'yeh tareeqa nahi chala, purane par jao' wala jhanda banao to (a) sirf usi raaste ki ghalti par (b) waqt ke saath khud khatam. Sabaq 2: maine 'PPS id out of range' ko 'camera ki maloomat nahi milti' samjha aur dump_extra ka plan diya — sandbox test (HEVC RTSP -> mpegts) mein ffmpeg pehle se har keyframe par VPS/SPS/PPS daalta hai: plan ghalat tha, nahi banaya. Asal ghalti PC ke andar tukre girna (🩺 'NVR→PC ghalti' = recording ffmpeg -> live-cam UDP ki CC ginti, yani PC ke ANDAR ka nuqsan, NVR ka nahi — naam ghalat hai). Plan dene se pehle wajah ka chhota test karo. ⏪ Saaf ka 'Error muxing a packet' asal recording file ke baghair theek nahi hoga (sandbox ki HEVC file par nahi aata) — `D:\NT-Recording` ka ek tukra chahiye.
- **📶 Live: Wi-Fi par theek, 4G par 'Rabta nahi bana' = relay (TURN) nahi hai (25 Nov 00:26, malik: 'WiFi pr shi chlta ha')** (malik: 'pehle chal raha tha, PC band hone ke baad nahi'): live / 🎛 / ⏪ ka WebRTC sirf STUN (Google) se PC -> phone SEEDHA jurta hai; mobile 4G ka network (CGNAT) aksar seedha rasta nahi banne deta -> phone par '⏳ Video aa rahi…' phir '✕ Rabta nahi bana', PC log mein sirf 'gaya (band)' (phone ne chhora) — 'failed' zaroori nahi. Ilaaj = TURN relay (Cloudflare, muft hadd) — malik ke OK par. GHALTI (24 Nov raat): maine pehle PC ka bojh, ⏪ ka bug aur PPS ko wajah bataya — malik ne CPU 19% dikha kar rad kiya; asal mein 12 baje ke baad PC log mein 🩺 line hi nahi thi (PC ki video saaf). Sabaq: 'live nahi chal raha' par PEHLA sawal = phone Wi-Fi par hai ya 4G (aur 'pehle chalta tha' to us waqt phone kis network par tha?); phir PC log — 🩺 na ho + sirf 'band' = rabte ka masla, PC / video ka nahi. Grey / dhabbe (PC ki taraf) aur 'video aati hi nahi' (rabta) do alag masle — ek ka ilaaj doosre par mat lagao. PC restart ko wajah tab hi maano jab Wi-Fi par bhi na chale.
- **🔁 Relay (TURN) = 4G par live (v2.378.0, rules 2.111, PC live-cam v2.9, 25 Nov)** (malik: 'Plane ok ha'): malik Settings → 🖥️ PC scripts → 🔁 Relay mein Cloudflare ka Turn Token ID + API Token (blueAccess/turnKey — SIRF malik; screen par dobara nahi dikhta, sirf aakhri 4 harf) → PC live-cam 24 ghante ka chhota pass (rtc.live.cloudflare.com generate-ice-servers, ttl 86400, 18 ghante se kam baqi = naya, :53 bahar; 401 / 404 = 30 min, baqi ghalti = 5 min) → liveIce/cur {j, exp} (malik / Full / Nigrani sirf get) → live.js STUN + TURN, offer se pehle RELAY candidate ka intezar (3 s), juda = getStats se rasta (status '🔁 relay se' + 🩺 'Rasta: relay / seedha'). PC (werift) khud TURN NAHI leta — werift setLocalDescription TURN allocation ka intezar karta hai (Cloudflare dheera = jawab hi ruk jata); phone ka relay kaafi (sandbox: node-turn + Chrome relay-only + werift bina TURN = video chali). Pass na ho / ghalat / purana / dheera (1.5 s) / RTCPeerConnection gire = pehle jaisa sirf STUN — live kabhi na ruke. Live / WebRTC ka koi naya raasta = isi liveIce pass se; chaabi (tok) kabhi log / app ke doosre doc / phone / repo / chat mein nahi (sirf Cloudflare ka Authorization header). Muft hadd 1,000 GB / mahina — relay sirf jab seedha rasta na bane. Test file kisi PC script ko require kare to `.alive` file chhoo leta hai (zinda.js) — commit se pehle `git checkout pc/*.alive`.
- **⏭ ‹ › mein posted bill KHUD chhoot (v2.379.0, 25 Nov)** (malik ki screenshot: 'Bill 2909 POS mein POSTED hai — OK = Unpost / Cancel = agla' — 'yahan posted bill na aaye · agla posted ho to skip ho kar agla un-posted aaye'): POS Purchase ka ‹ Pichhla / Agla ›, ☐ check aur ✓ Check + Post ke baad wala agla (`numNbr` → `navGo` → `openBillForEdit` nav) ab SAB ke liye sirf KHULE bill — `numNbr` mein `!posted` (khate ki posStatus 2), aur khata purana ho to PC ke doc ka docStatus 2 / 3 = foran 'skip' (tafseel ka intezar bhi nahi, OK / Cancel sawal kabhi nahi); sab chhoot jayein = paigham 'Aage koi khula bill nahi'. Posted bill kholna / ↩ Unpost = Purchase list se do tap (wahan sawal pehle jaisa). Bill aage-peeche karne ka koi naya raasta banao to posted / cancel isi tarah khud chhoro — beech mein sawal se malik ka silsila na toote.
- **🎨 Purchase lines: rang + PC par har item EK qatar (v2.380.0, 25 Nov)** (malik ki PC '📱 Mobile jaisa' tasveer: 'fonts dark + thore bare · dabba sirf hindson jitna · item ka naam dabbon ki seedh mein · wholesale laal · parchoon neela · kul raqam peela · carton / piece ka rang na chherna'): rowsHTML ke dabbon par `ppk-*` (q · kc · kp · a · w · r · c · p · m) + groups `ppk-g1` / `ppk-g2`; sale-cart par `.pp-pur` (sirf purchase — wa-sale ki `.pp-cr` alag rehti hai) aur PC (isPcW) par `.pp-wide` = naam baayein 280px, phir 10 chhote dabbe (17px moti likhai), tang jagah = container query se do qatar. Rang phone par bhi (layout wahi): W laal · R neela · Raqam peela · CTN / PCS / Kh wahi; badla hua W / R = 2px kinara (pehle hara). Rang `!important` + oonchi specificity (html.dk / html.lt themes inputs safed kar deti hain). PC wala naya CSS `@media (min-width:1100px)` mein mat daalo (tools/pcm.py us ko html:not(.pcm) bana deta) — `.pp-wide` jaisi class se. Purchase line mein naya dabba = K(…, 'ppk-…') + `.pp-wide` mein us ki chaurai. % wala sawal (Kh badle to 'purana nafa' W / R khud barhata hai, % wahi rehta) malik ne 'filhal rehne do' kaha — bina kahe mat chhero.

## 4. POS / PC — KHATARNAK, dhyan se
- POS = Cognitive Solutions (SQL Server). Jo script POS mein **likhti** hai (item-post, purchase-post, transfer-sync, sale-post…) us mein **koi value kabhi 0/khali na jaye jis se POS divide karta ho** — khas taur par **PackQty (carton pcs) kam az kam 1**. (18 Oct 2026: "kala tail khula" ka PackQty 0 gaya → counter sale "Divide by zero", bill band.)
- Parhne wali scripts (rack, irep, stats) POS mein kabhi kuch nahi likhti.
- **🩹 PC processes (22 Oct 2026, doctor v2.9)**: `taskkill /T` (poora tree) **KABHI NAHI** — Windows mare hue parent ka PID naye process ko de deta hai, /T phir be-taaluq scripts aur doctor ki apni loop bhi maar deta (21-Oct shaam 6:50: doctor chup + stock band, subah tak). Har `execSync` / `execFileSync` (wmic, taskkill, powershell, netstat) par `timeout` zaroor — bina hadd ek atka WMIC poore doctor ko hamesha ke liye rok deta tha. Process list na mile = andhe ho kar kuch shuru / band nahi.
- **🔥 PC ka CPU (doctor v3.0, app v2.225.0)**: doctor har 1 min (async, hadd 20 s) `blueAccess/pcCpu` — kul CPU / RAM + top 5 program (node / ffmpeg = script / kaam ka naam). Process ki COMMAND LINE kabhi Firestore / log mein nahi (NVR / SQL password hota hai) — sirf us se nikla naam. "PC dheera / CPU" ki shikayat = pehle Settings → 🖥️ PC scripts ka 🔥 dabba dekhne ko kaho, andaza nahi. Doctor mein naya kaam hamesha async + hadd (chakkar na ruke).
- **🚑 Doctor ka chowkidar (NT-PRINT v1.22)**: server PC par NT-PRINT har 3 min `blueAccess/pcStatus` dekhta hai — doctor 15 min chup = taza doctor.js + port 47830 wala atka doctor band (netstat, sirf wahi PID) + KHATA-DOCTOR.bat chupke (30 min mein ek dafa, host = pcStatus.host). Doctor aur NT-PRINT ek doosre ke nigraan — doctor wala PC badle to dono khud samajh lete (host).
- **📡 Local server laptop (v2.114.4)**: laptop = `pc/nt-local.js` (port 47900) — net na ho to WiFi par app. Laptop par KHATA-DOCTOR / POS likhne wali scripts KABHI nahi (do PC = bill do dafa). Asal server DESKTOP-Q1SLV77 hi. **24 Oct 2026: malik ne backup internet (4G failover) chuna — local server ka agla kaam (print / purchase / sale / poori app offline) ROKA hua, jab tak malik khud na kahe.** Laptop par NT Camera (galla camera) setup ne band kiya tha.
- **Godam/branch ko NAAM se pehchano, number se nahi** — POS mein branch 2 ka naam "Godam 1" hai. Godam 2 = naam mein "godam 2" (`collect().g2` / `jamaG2`). Branch 1 = NOOR TRADERS (dukan).
- Ata/maida jaisa maal sirf godam se bikta hai — dukan ki sale mein mat gino.
- **🧱 POS ka khana pehle jaancho (14 Nov 2026, raat-report v1.9 / item-report v1.9)**: malik ke POS ke `dbo.Sale` mein `COGS` ka khana HAI HI NAHI (sirf procs ka `@COGS` param) — `s.COGS` wali SQL se raat ki report kabhi nahi bani aur Item report ka nafa chup-chaap gayab tha ("Invalid column name 'COGS'"). Koi bhi 'shayad ho' khana SELECT karne se pehle `colOf` (sys.columns, asal naam) se jaancho; na ho = NULL / andaza — ek ghayab khane se poori report / script kabhi na gire. Report ki tareekh (`reportDay`) sirf usi hukum ke saath chale (`rdAt == reportNow`), warna AAJ.
- Diagnostic scripts SQL password kabhi print na karein.
- **💓 Har lambi PC script mein `.alive` (zinda.js) ZAROOR (8 Nov 2026, wa-out v1.2)**: wa-out mein zinda nahi tha → atka hua node doctor ko 'zinda' dikhta (process naam se), kabhi dobara nahi chalata, naya bat lock par exit 3 — app mein ghante bhar 'PC chup'. Nayi service = `const ZINDA = require('./zinda.js')(__filename, {})` (gaari-gps.js jaisa) + app ko heartbeat juda na ho tab bhi. **wa-out v1.3 (8 Nov)**: heartbeat zinda magar kaam ruka (10:57 ke baad kuch nahi gaya — onSnapshot chup / busy atka) → har queue wali service mein (a) `onSnapshot` ke saath 30 s ka POLL bhi (b) `busy` watchdog (har job par `busyAt` taza; 4 min atka = `process.exit` → bat / doctor dobara). Qaida: 'zinda' = kaam ho raha hai, sirf saans nahi.
- **🐞 Bug safai ke sabaq (v2.177.0)**: (1) PC ki Firestore likhai jo app ko "jawab" deti hai (jaise posLedger `req`) HAR likhne mein saath rakho aur do async likhne ek waqt ek (queue) — beech wala poora doc likh kar jawab mita deta hai. (2) "kitna purana" hamesha SERVER waqt (updateTime / readTime) — phone ki ghari nahi. (3) PC script shuru mein pehle ka data na parh sake to LIKHNA band jab tak parh na le (warna sab dobara + waqt badal jaata). (4) Ek printer / kaam ki line doosre ke peeche na ruke (thermal ≠ A4). (5) Bank parchi ka naam bank wale khate se, party ke naam se nahi.

- **🖥 NAYA SERVER PC (v2.216.0, rules 2.97, PC pc-vault v1.0 nayi service + server-setup.ps1 + server-join.js; malik 9 Nov: "Firestore mein aisi file — new server par lagayein to sab chal jaye")**: purana server `pc-vault.js` har 6 ghante firebase-key + local-config (SQL / pcName / printer) + label-settings + label-numbers → `pcVault/main` {j} (SIRF malik parhe, sirf admin likhe — NVR password wala faisla; sirf jis PC ki local-config mein SQL ho). Naya PC: `irm …/pc/server-setup.ps1 | iex` (malik ko sirf yehi EK pehli line — PC par command na kehne ke qaide ki chhoot, naye PC par doctor hi nahi) → Node → `server-join.js` (bina npm: fetch + webcrypto) gumnaam login → ECDH P-256 jori + 6 hindse code → `pcJoin/<uid>` → app Settings `#nayaServerBtn` → `naya-server.js` `sealFor` (AES-GCM, code = AAD) → PC sirf ALLOW 4 files rakhe (purani `.old-<waqt>`) → KHATA-DOCTOR (npm, Startup, sab services khud). Purana server pcStatus 10 min se taza = laal warning + do-qadam (dono chale = bill do dafa). WhatsApp QR dobara. Crypto format app ↔ server-join HUBAHU — ek badlo to doosra. Server par koi naya local file zaroori bane (setting) to pc-vault FILES + server-join ALLOW dono mein jodo. Test: mock Firebase + Chromium (https) — pairing, ghalat code nahi khula, faltu file nahi likhi. **Malik ka faisla (9 Nov 18:08)**: naye server PC ka Windows naam BHI `DESKTOP-Q1SLV77` rakhna (counters ka Cognitive POS, pcNames 'Bilal (server)', camera channel, printer — kuch chherna na pare). Hamara system kisi PC ka naam KHUD nahi badalta; purana PC chalana ho to pehle us ka naam badlo + us par KHATA-DOCTOR band (Startup se `KHATA-DOCTOR.vbs` hatao) — warna naam takrao + bill do dafa. Alag naam ka auto (SQL localhost / pcNames) malik ne nahi chaha.

- **🔒 Server PC ka naam (v2.283.0, doctor v3.2)** (malik 15 Nov: 'PC ka naam ho, wohi server uthaye'): `blueAccess/serverPc.host` (default `DESKTOP-Q1SLV77`, app Settings → PC scripts, sirf malik) — doctor sirf isi naam wale PC par scripts chalata hai; doosra PC `blockedCycle` (scripts band, `pcBlocked`, pcStatus kabhi nahi). Server par chalne wali nayi service doctor ke `services` se hi chale (apna Startup / task nahi) warna tala us par nahi lagta; server badalna = pehle app mein naam, NT-PRINT is tale se bahar.

- **🆔 Server ka tala = PC ki apni pehchan (v2.291.0, doctor v3.4, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'script mein PC ka naam hai to purane ka rabta khud tootna chahie' — dono PC ka naam ek tha, purane ne app ki entries hata din): `serverPc.mid` (MachineGuid ka hash, malik Settings → PC scripts mein '✅ Yehi server') ho to SIRF wohi PC server, naam nahi dekha jata; har PC `blueAccess/pcSeen` mein nazar aata hai. 'Entry save ke baad ghayab / parchi do jagah se' ki shikayat = pehle yeh list dekhne ko kaho (2 PC 🟢 = yehi wajah). Server ka naya tala / shart banao to apni pehchan na mile = naam wala tala (asal server kabhi khud band na ho); mid khud kabhi mat chuno — sirf malik.

- **🛑 Purana server band karna (16 Nov 2026, haath se)**: purane PC par doctor nahi chalta tha (9 purani scripts seedhi `node x.js`) — naam / pehchan ka tala us tak pohancha hi nahi, us ne app ki entries / bill hata diye ('⛔ POS mein nahi (delete)'). Koi PC 🆔 list mein na aaye aur phir bhi kaam kare = us par haath se: scripts band + `firebase-key.json` / `local-config.json` ka naam `.BAND` (folder rename atak sakta hai). Malik ko PC command do to poori command EK `if(){ }else{ }` block (line-line paste par akela `return` nahi rokta), process ko 'khata-sync' lafz se mat dhoondo (command line sirf `node x.js`), aur aakhir mein sabz / laal nateeja likhwao.

- **🛑 Print Spooler ka chowkidar (v2.292.0, doctor v3.6, thermal-name v1.1, 16 Nov)** (naye server par `spoolsv` 100% CPU, doctor chup — malik dukan par nahi: 'auto karo'): `spoolWatch` spoolsv 2 min lagatar bhaari = thermal print ROK (thermal-off.txt) + Spooler saaf / dobara + thermal scripts dobara; wapas chalu SIRF malik ke '▶ Thermal dobara chalu' se (🖨 dabba). **v3.8**: thermal ki line mein 5+ parchi 2 jaanch tak phansi = wohi rok (asal wajah nikli: 'EPSON TM-T88IV Receipt' 10 job line mein — printer naam se mil gaya magar chhaap nahi raha). 'Naam mil gaya ✅' ka matlab 'chhap raha hai' NAHI — line ki ginti (⏳ N job) dekho. **POS (Cognitive) usi Epson par theek chhaapta hai** — printer / taar theek; hamari scripts RAW bhejti hain jo Epson ke APD driver wali line se nahi nikalta. RAW ke liye alag line 'TM-T88IV' ('Generic / Text Only' driver, wohi port) — POS wali line kabhi mat chhero. **v3.9 / thermal-name v1.2**: khud chuna thermal naam SIRF malik ki ijazat (thermal-ok.txt) se — default BAND; naye / badle printer par scripts ko khud print shuru mat karne do (driver ghalat ho to Spooler poora CPU kha jata hai, POS dheema). Printer ka naam / raasta badalne wali koi tabdeeli push karne se pehle socho: printer us PC mein laga bhi hai? Naye printer par pehla print malik ki maujoodgi mein karwao — ghair-hazri mein server ka CPU atak gaya.

- **🧾 Thermal printer ka naam khud (doctor v3.5, thermal-name.js v1.0, 16 Nov)** (malik: naye server par naam 'EPSON TM-T88IV Receipt' — 'us ko add kar do auto'): thermal par chhapne wali har script naam `require('./thermal-name.js').name()` se le (try/catch, na mile = 'TM-T88IV') — `'TM-T88IV'` pakka kabhi mat likho; nayi thermal script banao to yehi line. Chunao `pick` (bilkul TM-T88IV -> naam mein TM-T88 -> purana). Windows ka printer naam / setting hum kabhi nahi badalte; nt-print (counter) alag.

- **🖨 Server ke printers app mein (v2.290.0, doctor v3.3, 16 Nov)** (malik: 'script wali screen mein jo printer add ho jayein un ka naam'): doctor `prnTick` -> `blueAccess/pcPrinters` (list + `want` = scripts ke zaroori naam: thermal `thermal-name.js` se, label `label-settings.json`) -> Settings -> PC scripts ka '🖨 Printers' dabba. 'Parchi / label nahi chhap raha' ki shikayat = pehle yeh dabba dekhne ko kaho (❌ = naam ghalat, 🔴 = printer band). Koi script naye naam ka printer use kare to `prnWant` mein jodo. Sirf dikhana — app se printer ka naam / setting mat badlo.

- **📌 NAYA SERVER PC — 16 Nov 2026 ke masle aur un ke sabaq (malik: 'important cheezein / jo problems aayen wo qaide mein likha karo')**:
  1. **Purana server chalta raha** (naam ek hi, purana POS data) -> app ki entries / bill 'POS mein nahi (delete)' keh kar hata diye. Naya server lagao to PEHLE purana pakka band (scripts + `firebase-key.json` / `local-config.json` ka naam badlo), phir naya chalao. Do server ek saath = data ka nuqsan.
  2. **Naam ka tala kaafi nahi** — purane PC par doctor chalta hi nahi tha, tala us tak nahi pohancha. Tala sirf un PC par chalta hai jahan doctor ho; baqi haath se band.
  3. **Thermal printer**: POS (Cognitive) Windows ke driver se chhaapta hai, hamari scripts RAW data bhejti hain. Epson ke apne driver wali line ('EPSON TM-T88IV Receipt') par RAW phans gaya -> 10 parchi line mein, Print Spooler 100% CPU, POS dheema. Hal: usi port par DOOSRI line **'TM-T88IV'** ('Generic / Text Only' driver) — POS wali line kabhi nahi chherni. Naye server ke setup mein yeh line banana ab ek zaroori qadam hai.
  4. **Printer ka naam khud chun-na** (meri tabdeeli) ne malik ki ghair-hazri mein server atka diya. Printer / POS / paise ko chhoone wali tabdeeli = pehle test malik ki maujoodgi mein, default BAND, chalu sirf malik ke button se.
  5. **PC command dena**: hamesha EK line (`try{ … }catch{ … }`, `;` se jora) — AnyDesk se kai lines paste hon to ULTI tarteeb se chal jati hain (13:33: pehle printer, phir driver -> 'driver does not exist'). Aakhir mein sabz 'HO GAYA' / laal 'NAHI HUA'. Process ko 'khata-sync' lafz se mat dhoondo.
  6. **Andaza = andaza keh kar batao**: 'taar nahi lagi hogi' ghalat nikla (taar lagi thi). Wajah pakki na ho to pehle app ke dabbe (🖨 Printers ki '⏳ N job', 🔥 CPU, 🆔 PC list) ki photo maango.
  11. **❌ GHALAT NIKLA (16 Nov 14:32, Windows ka record)**: doosri line 'TM-T88IV' (Generic / Text Only, port ESDPRT001) banne ke 2 minute baad se Print Spooler 3 dafa GIRA (13:37, 13:41 ntdll; 14:18 `EpsonPH.dll` v1.3.8.0 — Epson ka 2009 ka port handler) aur teesri dafa band hi reh gaya -> thermal + label + POS sab ke print ruk gaye. 8-10 parchi nikli thin, is liye 'chal gaya' laga. Sabaq: Epson ke port (ESDPRT…) par DOOSRI line kabhi mat banao; 'ek parchi nikal aayi' = pakka nahi — Windows ka record (Service Control Manager 7031 / 7034, Application 1000 spoolsv) dekho. Neeche wala nuqta 9 mansookh: ab raasta = POS ki tarah DRIVER se chhaapna (malik ka pehla hukum yehi tha) ya Epson ka naya driver — malik ke OK par.
  12. **🖨 Asal printer TM-T88V hai, driver TM-T88IV ka (malik 16 Nov 14:53)**: naye server par Epson ka GHALAT model ka purana driver ('EPSON TM-T88IV ReceiptE4', EpsonPH.dll 2009) laga hai — wohi Spooler gira raha tha. Hal = TM-T88V ka apna Epson driver (ReceiptE4 2017 / APD5) lagana (dukan ka banda, POS printer badalta hai), POS bill test, phir scripts ko naya naam + malik ke saamne parchi test. Printer ka masla aaye to pehle printer ka ASAL model (printer ke neeche likha) aur driver ka naam milao.
  9. **(mansookh — nuqta 11 dekho) (16 Nov 14:04, malik: 'chal gaya hai print')**: doosri line 'TM-T88IV' ('Generic / Text Only', port ESDPRT001) se hamari parchi nikal aayi, POS apni line se chhaapta raha. Naye server par thermal ka yehi tareeqa — andaza nahi, azmaya hua. Driver ke zariye tasveer bana kar chhaapne wala bara kaam ZAROORAT NAHI (mat banao).
  10. **Ghalat line ki rok (v2.292.5, doctor v3.12)**: 🖨 dabba thermal line ka driver dikhata hai (`want.raw`); saada driver na ho = ⛔ + chalu ka button band. `server-setup.ps1` qadam 6 yeh line khud banata hai. Kisi printer par RAW bhejne wali nayi script banao to us line ka driver bhi isi tarah jaancho — 'naam mil gaya' kaafi nahi.
  8. **PC ka haal har kuch second (v2.292.4, doctor v3.11)**: `seenBeat` har 20 s, app ka 🖥 dabba har 10 s khud taza. App mein PC ka koi bhi 'kab dekha' dikhao to us ki likhai kitni der baad hoti hai pehle jaancho — 30 min wali likhai ko 'PC chup hai' mat samjho (16 Nov: isi se ghalat kaha 'doctor chup').
  7. **Malik ka naya qaida**: jo bhi bara masla aaye, us ka sabaq usi din CLAUDE.md + AI_NOTES mein likho — malik ke kahe baghair.

## 5. Security (kabhi nahi)
- **📱 Device jorna = mulazim ka WAHID login (v2.231.0, rules 2.102)** (malik 11 Nov: "card par device jorne ka option · ID likhein to password / link nahi · code password sirf malik ke liye · device ko access / edit / delete"): phone login screen par bari Device ID -> `devWait/<uid>` (cloud.devWait, controller.hold) · malik card ka `📱 Device jorein` (`stDjOpen`) -> `cloud.devJoin` usi uid ka session (malik ka haq, PC nahi chahiye) -> phone `controller.claim()`. Device ka access = `session.dp` (screens, app ki satah) · ✕ = session bhi delete. Mulazim ke liye code / link / password ka UI wapas mat lao; naya login raasta = devWait jaisa (uid se rishta, chhoti ID sirf pehchan). `hold` ke dauran restore gumnaam user ko na chhere — auth-controller mein yeh shart mat hatao.
- **🔐 Gumnaam login = koi bhi (v2.221.0, rules 2.101, PC owner-login v2.0)**: app ki Firebase settings public hain — koi bhi program se anonymous login bana sakta hai. Is liye rules mein mulazim ka har naya haq `realStaff()` (session + chalu key) se, akela `isStaff()` KABHI nahi (sirf login se pehle wale: `staffLogin`, `ownerLogin`, `pcJoin`, `geoLog`). Session (`blueStaffSessions`) SIRF shop PC banata / badalta hai — phone `staffLogin/<uid>` likhta hai (code / link / password / screen badalna), 10 ghalat = 1 ghanta band; naya login raasta banao to `staffAsk` se. Koi bhi chhota code / ID (6 hindse / 6 harf) jis se doc parh kar andar aa sakein = PC se jaancho, rules mein seedha `get` nahi.
- `firebase-key.json`, `local-config.json` **GitHub par kabhi nahi** (haath se copy).
- Master key, AI key, bank key chat mein kabhi mat likho.
- `node_modules` / `package-lock` commit mat karo.
- **🔓 Bina login / bina chaabi rule KABHI nahi (v2.182.1, rules 2.89)**: repo PUBLIC hai — rules aur firebase config sab parh sakte hain. bankSms ke purane test darwaze (p 1/2/3, v2.40) se koi bhi jhooti HBL SMS daal sakta tha (PC bank-parchi khate mein laga deta). Test ke liye khula rule banao to usi kaam ke aakhir mein hatao; bahar se likhne wala har raasta = khufia chaabi (bankKey / gaariKey jaisa) + `keys().hasOnly`. Malik ka faisla baqi: repo private (Pages / PC update ka plan alag).
- **Login code**: malik ka pakka code sirf hash (`blueAccess/ownerCode`, PBKDF2) — tasdeeq sirf shop PC (`pc/owner-login.js`, admin custom token, 10 ghalat = 1 ghanta band). Plain code kabhi Firestore / log / chat mein nahi. Larke ke code (`deviceJoins pc-…`, keep) ek dafa chalte hain, phir naya khud (v2.105.7).

- **📱 Gumnaam UID = device ki pehchan (v2.231.1, ghalti 11 Nov)**: jura hua phone Logout / ghalat password ke baad dobara login maangta tha — signOut se UID kho jata hai. auth-controller mein gumnaam user ke liye HAMESHA `out()` (kabhi seedha `sdk.signOut`); naya login / logout raasta banao to yehi. Malik us phone par khud login kare = dobara jorna parega (malik ko pehle batao).

- **⚡ Foran data (v2.264.0)**: boot par pehle KhataRecovery 'ledger:' ki copy (packet `uid` + `sc` — sirf wahi login / scope), phir Firestore. Der ki wajah Firestore ki yaad thi (4935 doc = 10 s), hisaab nahi. Packet ke uid / sc ki shart KABHI mat hatao (doosre login ko pehle wale ka khata na dikhe); is copy se sirf DIKHAO, faisla / save hamesha asal data par.
- **📦 Data ka naap (v2.262.0)**: 'app khul jati hai magar data der se' = ⏱ chip ka '📦 Ns' + ⏱ popup ki 📦 / ☁️ / 🧮 lines (`window.ntDataT`, cloud.js listen) — pehle yeh photo maango, phir theek karo (yaad dheeri / server dheera / hisaab dheera teen alag ilaaj).
- **⏱ Boot ka intezar (v2.233.1)**: app ka ⏱ = session banne tak ka waqt (data nahi) — tap par qadam. Login ki jaanch ka koi raasta chup-chaap laute (paigham nahi) to boot 30 s splash par atakta hai: naya chup raasta banao to `controller.idle()` sach ho. Session / login ka faisla hamesha `getDocFromServer` se (cache ka purana 'nahi hai' = jhoota logout). 'App dheeri' ki shikayat = pehle ⏱ par tap ka popup maango, andaza nahi (11 Nov: pehle ghalat wajah bata di).

- **🔑 Mulazim ka password KAHIN NAHI (v2.234.0, malik 11 Nov: 'password poori app mein kahin nahi — naya tareeqa')**: mulazim ke liye password ka koi khana / row / form wapas mat lao (login, screen badalna, yaad rakhna). Malik ka apna login + ⛔ Item band / ⇄ transfer ka password alag cheez hain — malik ke kehne par hi chhero.
- **🔄 App khud reload NAHI (v2.234.0)**: nazar aati app ko kabhi khud `location.reload()` mat karo (naya version = ✨ Update patti); reload sirf button par ya jab app 30 min se chhupi ho.
- **⛔ Subah 11 tak (md pkg v32, 12 Nov 2026)** (malik: '11 baje tak sab apps band · bank, WhatsApp, call, MacroDroid, Chrome khule · zaroori 10 minute'): `md/tools/subah.py` — raat 12 se 10:59. Nayi app khuli rakhni ho = ALLOW / PARTS mein jodo; waqt / emergency (din mein 1 dafa, `EMG` 600 s) malik ke kehne par hi badlo. Bank mode / Claude break se takrao nahi.
- **🌙 Raat ke sawal (md pkg v31)**: `md/tools/raat.py` 20:30 + 21:15 popup — malik ne C chuna (koi app band nahi). Waqt / lafz malik ke kehne par badlo.

## 6. Har update ka checklist
1. Firestore rules badlein to `firestore-rules-X.html` (pichhle versions ke chips ke saath) malik ko bhejo + poora rules text chat mein. **Ab (27 Oct 2026, malik ka OK): shop PC `pc/rules-publish.js` har 5 min GitHub ki `firestore.rules` khud publish karta hai (blueAccess/rulesPub) — bas `firestore.rules` header `BLUE KHATA vX` aur `version.json.rules` ek rakho; html sirf tab bhejo jab app ke 🔐 Rules dabbe mein PC ki ghalti dikhe.**
2. `AI_NOTES.txt` (root) + `pc/AI_NOTES.txt` dono update.
3. Version bump (pehle `git pull` + origin ka `version.json` dekho — 9 Nov: do session ek waqt, dono ne 2.218.0 / rules 2.99 liya, push par takrao; baad wala agla number le): `OLD=x NEW=y; grep -rl "$OLD" --include=*.js --include=*.html . | grep -v node_modules | grep -v firestore-rules | xargs sed -i "s/v=$OLD\b/v=$NEW/g; s/sam-blue-khata-$OLD\b/sam-blue-khata-$NEW/; s/APP_VER='$OLD'/APP_VER='$NEW'/; s/Blue Khata v$OLD\b/Blue Khata v$NEW/g"` + `version.json`.
4. PC file badle to `pc/manifest.json` version + sha256 — likhne ke baad jaancho (`git diff pc/manifest.json` mein version + har file ka hash badla; 9 Nov: script ne manifest likha hi nahi, PC update rukta).
5. `node --check` (copy as .mjs) + tests; phir commit/push, deploy (`gh run list -R samiullah-878/Noor-traders- -L 1`) ka intezar.
6. Malik ko chhota Roman Urdu summary: kya badla, "app Update karein", AI hai ya nahi.
- **POS update** (Cognitive) ke baad procedures mein naye params aa sakte hain ("expects parameter … was not supplied"). `pc/proc-fill.js` har `usp_` call se pehle sys.parameters dekh kar anjaan params 0 / '' / NULL bhar deta hai — har POS-likhne wali script mein `require('./proc-fill.js').patch(sql, log)`. Nayi script banao to ye line zaroor (20 Oct 2026: @IsAdvanceTaxable se naya item ruk gaya tha).
- **Packet-war (rack) hisaab app ka apna hai**: bacha = aakhri likha − us packet ke barcode ki sale. POS ke kul stock se KHUD kabhi mat milao (20 Oct 2026: v2.103.66 mein milaya, malik ne wapas karwaya) — POS mein sirf kul hota hai, packet nahi. POS se bharna sirf malik ke button par.
- **Naya import / dynamic import HAMESHA `?v=<version>` ke saath** (`./x.js?v=2.103.79`) — bina version SW purani file deta hai, PC par app aadhi purani chalti hai (20 Oct 2026). Firestore ko wapas `persistentMultipleTabManager` mat karo — PC par ek tab so jaye to sab tab ka data ruk jata tha.
- **Label printer**: script kabhi khud "retry/dobara bhejo" na kare — spooler ka number na hilna = printer dheere, labels asal mein nikal rahe hote hain (20 Oct 2026: v4.7 retry se 100 label dohre chhape). Ruka ho to sirf intezar + app ko wajah; dobara sirf malik ke hukum se.
- **Poori screen** (fixed) ke liye `100vw/100dvh` mat likho — `html{zoom:.92}` ki wajah se 92% reh jata hai; `inset:0` + `width/height:auto` likho.
- **Comment kabhi code line ke BEECH mein nahi** — `//` ke baad wala sab comment ban jata hai (20 Oct 2026: pos-khata-sync dobara print ka `code / date` comment ban gaya — parchi par voucher no / tareekh gayab). Comment line ke aakhir mein ya alag line. **9 Nov 2026 dobara (bill-clip v1.5)**: ek-line callback ke beech `// v1.5` = baqi line comment, `node --check` fail, phir bhi push ho gaya (check aur push `&&` se jure nahi the). Qaida: check → manifest → commit → push HAMESHA ek `&&` zanjeer; check fail = kuch push nahi. **9 Nov teesri dafa (v2.214.0, check ne pakra)**: python `replace` se kisi ek-line code ke beech naya tukda + `// comment` jora — naye tukde ke saath comment KABHI nahi jab tak wo line ka aakhir na ho; shak ho to comment hi mat likho. **15 Nov paanchvi dafa (raat-report v2.2)**: `python3 - <<'E' … E` heredoc ke BAAD wala hukum nayi line par tha (zanjeer se bahar) — python fail, phir bhi commit + push. Heredoc wale python ko pehle file mein likho (`cat > /tmp/x.py`), phir `python3 /tmp/x.py && check && git …` EK line mein. **10 Nov chauthi dafa (v2.226.0)**: manifest wala python fail hua magar agla hukum `;` se jura tha → app push, manifest reh gaya. Python (manifest / notes) bhi usi `&&` zanjeer ka hissa — beech mein `;` KABHI nahi; manifest ke hash sirf pehli key (`count=1` — aakhir mein ek purani dohri key pari hai).
- **Firestore mein list ke andar list kabhi nahi** (`[[...]]`) — PC admin SDK bhi 'invalid nested entity' deta hai aur poori script ruk jati hai. JSON text (`xj`) bana kar bhejo (20 Oct 2026: sales-stats ph se 4 din Commission report Rs 0).
